-- ##### func account_auth_user(p_email text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.account_auth_user(p_email text)
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, confirmed boolean, linked boolean)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select u.id, u.email_confirmed_at is not null,
         exists (select 1 from memberships m where m.user_id = u.id)
    from auth.users u where lower(u.email) = lower(p_email)
$function$
;
-- ##### func account_prepare(p_kind text, p_ids uuid[])
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.account_prepare(p_kind text, p_ids uuid[])
 RETURNS TABLE(person_id uuid, full_name text, class_name text, login text, email text, status text)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare
  v_org uuid := public.my_org_id(); v_code text; v_dom text := public.login_domain(); v_role uuid;
  v_next int; r record; v_login text; v_email text; v_pid uuid; v_seen text[] := '{}';
  v_need_consent boolean := coalesce((select require_student_consent from security_settings where id = 1), false);
begin
  if p_kind not in ('student', 'teacher', 'parent', 'judge') then raise exception 'Loại tài khoản không hợp lệ' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if not (public.is_admin() or public.has_perm('accounts', 'create')
          or (p_kind = 'judge' and (public.is_authority() or public.is_platform_admin()))) then
    raise exception 'Không có quyền tạo tài khoản' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if coalesce(array_length(p_ids, 1), 0) > 500 then raise exception 'Tối đa 500 người mỗi lần' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  v_code := public.login_school_code(v_org);
  if v_code is null then
    raise exception 'Trường chưa có mã đơn vị — liên hệ KDI đặt mã trước khi tạo tài khoản' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  select id into v_role from roles where key = p_kind and is_system and org_id is null order by id limit 1;
  select coalesce(max(n), 0) + 1 into v_next from (
    select substring(p.login from '^' || v_code || '\.' || case when p_kind = 'judge' then 'gk' else 'gv' end || '(\d+)$')::int as n from profiles p
     where p.login like v_code || case when p_kind = 'judge' then '.gk%' else '.gv%' end
    union all
    select substring(i.email from '^' || v_code || '\.' || case when p_kind = 'judge' then 'gk' else 'gv' end || '(\d+)@')::int from invitations i
     where i.org_id = v_org and i.status = 'pending' and i.email like v_code || case when p_kind = 'judge' then '.gk%' else '.gv%' end) x;

  for r in
    select s.id, s.full_name, c.name as cls, s.profile_id, s.student_code as code, null::text as inv_email
      from unnest(p_ids) u(id) join students s on s.id = u.id and s.org_id = v_org left join classes c on c.id = s.class_id
     where p_kind = 'student'
    union all
    select t.id, t.full_name, null, t.profile_id, null,
           (select i.email from invitations i where i.teacher_id = t.id and i.status = 'pending' and i.email like v_code || '.gv%@%'
             order by i.created_at desc limit 1)
      from unnest(p_ids) u(id) join teachers t on t.id = u.id and t.org_id = v_org
     where p_kind = 'teacher'
    union all
    select g.id, g.full_name, c.name, g.profile_id, g.phone_norm, null
      from unnest(p_ids) u(id) join guardians g on g.id = u.id and g.org_id = v_org
      join students s on s.id = g.student_id left join classes c on c.id = s.class_id
     where p_kind = 'parent'
    union all
    select j.id, j.full_name, (select title from competitions where id = j.competition_id), j.profile_id, null,
           (select i.email from invitations i where i.judge_id = j.id and i.status = 'pending' order by i.created_at desc limit 1)
      from unnest(p_ids) u(id) join competition_judges j on j.id = u.id and j.org_id = v_org
     where p_kind = 'judge'
  loop
    person_id := r.id; full_name := r.full_name; class_name := r.cls; login := null; email := null;
    if r.profile_id is not null then
      select p.login into login from profiles p where p.id = r.profile_id;
      status := 'has_account'; return next; continue;
    end if;
    if p_kind = 'student' then
      v_login := nullif(regexp_replace(lower(coalesce(r.code, '')), '[^a-z0-9]+', '', 'g'), '');
      if v_login is null then status := 'missing_code'; return next; continue; end if;
      if v_need_consent and not exists (select 1 from student_consents sc where sc.student_id = r.id and sc.withdrawn_on is null) then
        status := 'no_consent'; return next; continue;
      end if;
      v_login := v_code || '.' || v_login;
    elsif p_kind = 'parent' then
      if r.code is null then status := 'missing_phone'; return next; continue; end if;
      -- phụ huynh cùng SĐT đã có tài khoản (con khác) → gắn luôn
      select g.profile_id into v_pid from guardians g where g.org_id = v_org and g.phone_norm = r.code and g.profile_id is not null limit 1;
      if v_pid is not null then
        perform set_config('app.login_admin', 'on', true);
        update guardians set profile_id = v_pid where id = r.id;
        perform set_config('app.login_admin', '', true);
        select p.login into login from profiles p where p.id = v_pid;
        status := 'has_account'; return next; continue;
      end if;
      if r.code = any(v_seen) then status := 'same_phone'; return next; continue; end if;
      v_seen := v_seen || r.code;
      v_login := v_code || '.ph' || r.code;
    elsif r.inv_email is not null then
      v_login := split_part(r.inv_email, '@', 1);
    else
      v_login := v_code || case when p_kind = 'judge' then '.gk' else '.gv' end || v_next; v_next := v_next + 1;
    end if;
    v_email := v_login || '@' || v_dom;
    login := v_login;
    if exists (select 1 from profiles p where p.login = v_login) then status := 'login_taken'; return next; continue; end if;
    update invitations i set status = 'cancelled', note = 'Thay bằng lời mời tài khoản mã'
     where i.org_id = v_org and lower(i.email) = v_email and i.status = 'pending';
    insert into invitations (org_id, email, full_name, role_id, teacher_id, student_id, guardian_id, judge_id, invited_by)
      values (v_org, v_email, r.full_name, v_role,
              case when p_kind = 'teacher' then r.id end, case when p_kind = 'student' then r.id end,
              case when p_kind = 'parent' then r.id end, case when p_kind = 'judge' then r.id end, auth.uid());
    email := v_email; status := 'new'; return next;
  end loop;
  for r in select u.id from unnest(p_ids) u(id)
            where not exists (select 1 from students s where s.id = u.id and s.org_id = v_org and p_kind = 'student')
              and not exists (select 1 from teachers t where t.id = u.id and t.org_id = v_org and p_kind = 'teacher')
              and not exists (select 1 from guardians g where g.id = u.id and g.org_id = v_org and p_kind = 'parent')
              and not exists (select 1 from competition_judges j where j.id = u.id and j.org_id = v_org and p_kind = 'judge') loop
    person_id := r.id; full_name := null; class_name := null; login := null; email := null; status := 'not_found'; return next;
  end loop;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func account_reset_target(p_kind text, p_id uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.account_reset_target(p_kind text, p_id uuid)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_org uuid := public.my_org_id(); v_pid uuid; v_name text; v_login text;
begin
  if not (public.is_admin() or public.has_perm('accounts', 'edit') or (p_kind = 'judge' and (public.is_authority() or public.is_platform_admin()))) then
    raise exception 'Không có quyền đặt lại mật khẩu' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if p_kind = 'student' then select s.profile_id, s.full_name into v_pid, v_name from students s where s.id = p_id and s.org_id = v_org;
  elsif p_kind = 'teacher' then select t.profile_id, t.full_name into v_pid, v_name from teachers t where t.id = p_id and t.org_id = v_org;
  elsif p_kind = 'parent' then select g.profile_id, g.full_name into v_pid, v_name from guardians g where g.id = p_id and g.org_id = v_org;
  elsif p_kind = 'judge' then select j.profile_id, j.full_name into v_pid, v_name from competition_judges j where j.id = p_id and j.org_id = v_org;
  else raise exception 'Loại tài khoản không hợp lệ' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if v_pid is null then raise exception 'Người này chưa có tài khoản ở trường' using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;
  select login into v_login from profiles where id = v_pid;
  if v_login is null then
    raise exception 'Tài khoản dùng email thật — người dùng tự đặt lại bằng "Quên mật khẩu"' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  insert into audit_log (actor, org_id, entity, entity_id, action, summary, detail)
    values (auth.uid(), v_org, 'profiles', v_pid::text, 'update', 'Đặt lại mật khẩu tài khoản ' || v_login || ' (' || v_name || ')',
            jsonb_build_object('login', v_login, 'kind', p_kind));
  return v_pid;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func act_norm(t text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.act_norm(t text)
 RETURNS text
 LANGUAGE sql
 IMMUTABLE
AS $function$ select lower(btrim(regexp_replace(coalesce(t, ''), '\s+', ' ', 'g'))) $function$
;
-- ##### func act_shuffle(arr jsonb, seed text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.act_shuffle(arr jsonb, seed text)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 IMMUTABLE
AS $function$
declare r jsonb;
begin
  if arr is null or jsonb_typeof(arr) <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(arr) < 2 then return coalesce(arr, '[]'::jsonb); end if;
  select jsonb_agg(v order by md5(seed || ':' || v::text || ':' || o)) into r from jsonb_array_elements(arr) with ordinality x(v, o);
  if r = arr then
    select jsonb_agg(v order by o desc) into r from jsonb_array_elements(arr) with ordinality x(v, o);
  end if;
  return r;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func activities_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.activities_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_err text;
begin
  if new.status = 'published' then
    v_err := public.activity_problem(new.kind, new.content);
    if v_err is not null then raise exception 'Chưa xuất bản được: %', v_err using errcode = '23514'; end if;
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func activity_grade(p_kind text, c jsonb, a jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.activity_grade(p_kind text, c jsonb, a jsonb)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 IMMUTABLE
AS $function$
declare res boolean[] := '{}'; e jsonb; s text; o int; by_item jsonb := '{}'::jsonb;
begin
  a := coalesce(a, 'null'::jsonb);
  case p_kind
  when 'quiz' then
    for e, o in select x, n from jsonb_array_elements(c->'items') with ordinality t(x, n) loop
      res := res || (jsonb_typeof(a->(o::int - 1)) = 'number' and (a->>(o::int - 1))::int = (e->>'answer')::int);
    end loop;
  when 'truefalse' then
    for e, o in select x, n from jsonb_array_elements(c->'items') with ordinality t(x, n) loop
      res := res || (jsonb_typeof(a->(o::int - 1)) = 'boolean' and (a->>(o::int - 1))::boolean = (e->>'answer')::boolean);
    end loop;
  when 'match' then
    for e, o in select x, n from jsonb_array_elements(c->'pairs') with ordinality t(x, n) loop
      res := res || coalesce(public.act_norm(a->>(o::int - 1)) = public.act_norm(e->>'right') and a->>(o::int - 1) is not null, false);
    end loop;
  when 'order' then
    for s, o in select x, n from jsonb_array_elements_text(c->'steps') with ordinality t(x, n) loop
      res := res || coalesce(public.act_norm(a->>(o::int - 1)) = public.act_norm(s) and a->>(o::int - 1) is not null, false);
    end loop;
  when 'fill' then
    for s, o in select m[1], n from regexp_matches(c->>'text', '\[([^\]]*)\]', 'g') with ordinality t(m, n) loop
      res := res || coalesce(a->>(o::int - 1) is not null and public.act_norm(a->>(o::int - 1)) in
        (select public.act_norm(alt) from unnest(string_to_array(s, '|')) alt), false);
    end loop;
  when 'group' then
    for e in select * from jsonb_array_elements(c->'groups') loop
      for s in select * from jsonb_array_elements_text(e->'items') loop
        res := res || coalesce(jsonb_typeof(a) = 'object' and public.act_norm(a->>s) = public.act_norm(e->>'name'), false);
        by_item := by_item || jsonb_build_object(s, res[array_length(res, 1)]);
      end loop;
    end loop;
  else
    return jsonb_build_object('correct', 0, 'total', 0, 'results', '[]'::jsonb);
  end case;
  return jsonb_build_object('correct', (select count(*) from unnest(res) r where r), 'total', coalesce(array_length(res, 1), 0), 'results', to_jsonb(res))
    || case when p_kind = 'group' then jsonb_build_object('by_item', by_item) else '{}'::jsonb end;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func activity_playable(p_kind text, c jsonb, seed text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.activity_playable(p_kind text, c jsonb, seed text)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 IMMUTABLE
AS $function$
begin
  return case p_kind
    when 'quiz' then jsonb_build_object('items', (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('q', e->'q', 'options', e->'options') order by o), '[]') from jsonb_array_elements(c->'items') with ordinality x(e, o)))
    when 'truefalse' then jsonb_build_object('items', (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('q', e->'q') order by o), '[]') from jsonb_array_elements(c->'items') with ordinality x(e, o)))
    when 'match' then jsonb_build_object(
      'left', (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(e->'left' order by o), '[]') from jsonb_array_elements(c->'pairs') with ordinality x(e, o)),
      'right', public.act_shuffle((select coalesce(jsonb_agg(e->'right' order by o), '[]') from jsonb_array_elements(c->'pairs') with ordinality x(e, o)), seed))
    when 'order' then jsonb_build_object('steps', public.act_shuffle(c->'steps', seed))
    when 'fill' then jsonb_build_object('pieces', to_jsonb(regexp_split_to_array(c->>'text', '\[[^\]]*\]')))
    when 'group' then jsonb_build_object(
      'groups', (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(g->'name' order by o), '[]') from jsonb_array_elements(c->'groups') with ordinality x(g, o)),
      'items', public.act_shuffle((select coalesce(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(s)), '[]') from jsonb_array_elements(c->'groups') g, jsonb_array_elements_text(g->'items') s), seed))
    when 'flashcard' then jsonb_build_object('cards', c->'cards')
    when 'worksheet' then jsonb_build_object('intro', c->'intro', 'fields', c->'fields')
    when 'lesson' then jsonb_build_object('slides', (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(e - 'answer' order by o), '[]') from jsonb_array_elements(c->'slides') with ordinality x(e, o)))
    else c end;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func activity_problem(p_kind text, c jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.activity_problem(p_kind text, c jsonb)
 RETURNS text
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 IMMUTABLE
AS $function$
declare e jsonb; i int := 0; n int;
begin
  if c is null or jsonb_typeof(c) <> 'object' then return 'Nội dung không hợp lệ'; end if;
  case p_kind
  when 'quiz' then
    if jsonb_typeof(c->'items') <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(c->'items') not between 1 and 50 then return 'Cần 1–50 câu hỏi'; end if;
    for e in select * from jsonb_array_elements(c->'items') loop
      i := i + 1;
      if public.act_norm(e->>'q') = '' then return format('Câu %s chưa có nội dung', i); end if;
      if jsonb_typeof(e->'options') <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(e->'options') not between 2 and 6 then return format('Câu %s cần 2–6 phương án', i); end if;
      if exists (select 1 from jsonb_array_elements_text(e->'options') o where public.act_norm(o) = '') then return format('Câu %s có phương án trống', i); end if;
      if jsonb_typeof(e->'answer') <> 'number' or (e->>'answer')::int not between 0 and jsonb_array_length(e->'options') - 1 then return format('Câu %s chưa chọn đáp án', i); end if;
    end loop;
  when 'truefalse' then
    if jsonb_typeof(c->'items') <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(c->'items') not between 1 and 50 then return 'Cần 1–50 nhận định'; end if;
    for e in select * from jsonb_array_elements(c->'items') loop
      i := i + 1;
      if public.act_norm(e->>'q') = '' then return format('Nhận định %s chưa có nội dung', i); end if;
      if jsonb_typeof(e->'answer') <> 'boolean' then return format('Nhận định %s chưa chọn Đúng/Sai', i); end if;
    end loop;
  when 'match' then
    if jsonb_typeof(c->'pairs') <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(c->'pairs') not between 2 and 20 then return 'Cần 2–20 cặp'; end if;
    for e in select * from jsonb_array_elements(c->'pairs') loop
      i := i + 1;
      if public.act_norm(e->>'left') = '' or public.act_norm(e->>'right') = '' then return format('Cặp %s còn trống', i); end if;
    end loop;
    if (select count(distinct public.act_norm(x->>'right')) from jsonb_array_elements(c->'pairs') x) < jsonb_array_length(c->'pairs') then
      return 'Vế phải của các cặp phải khác nhau'; end if;
  when 'order' then
    if jsonb_typeof(c->'steps') <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(c->'steps') not between 2 and 20 then return 'Cần 2–20 bước'; end if;
    if exists (select 1 from jsonb_array_elements_text(c->'steps') s where public.act_norm(s) = '') then return 'Có bước còn trống'; end if;
    if (select count(distinct public.act_norm(s)) from jsonb_array_elements_text(c->'steps') s) < jsonb_array_length(c->'steps') then
      return 'Các bước phải khác nhau'; end if;
  when 'fill' then
    if public.act_norm(c->>'text') = '' then return 'Chưa có đoạn văn'; end if;
    select count(*) into n from regexp_matches(c->>'text', '\[([^\]]*)\]', 'g') m;
    if n = 0 then return 'Đánh dấu chỗ trống bằng [đáp án]'; end if;
    if n > 30 then return 'Tối đa 30 chỗ trống'; end if;
    if exists (select 1 from regexp_matches(c->>'text', '\[([^\]]*)\]', 'g') m where public.act_norm(m[1]) = '') then return 'Có chỗ trống chưa ghi đáp án'; end if;
  when 'group' then
    if jsonb_typeof(c->'groups') <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(c->'groups') not between 2 and 6 then return 'Cần 2–6 nhóm'; end if;
    for e in select * from jsonb_array_elements(c->'groups') loop
      i := i + 1;
      if public.act_norm(e->>'name') = '' then return format('Nhóm %s chưa có tên', i); end if;
      if jsonb_typeof(e->'items') <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(e->'items') = 0 then return format('Nhóm %s chưa có thẻ', i); end if;
      if exists (select 1 from jsonb_array_elements_text(e->'items') s where public.act_norm(s) = '') then return format('Nhóm %s có thẻ trống', i); end if;
    end loop;
    select count(*) into n from jsonb_array_elements(c->'groups') g, jsonb_array_elements_text(g->'items') s;
    if n > 40 then return 'Tối đa 40 thẻ'; end if;
    if (select count(distinct public.act_norm(s)) from jsonb_array_elements(c->'groups') g, jsonb_array_elements_text(g->'items') s) < n then
      return 'Các thẻ phải khác nhau'; end if;
  when 'flashcard' then
    if jsonb_typeof(c->'cards') <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(c->'cards') not between 1 and 60 then return 'Cần 1–60 thẻ'; end if;
    if exists (select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(c->'cards') x where public.act_norm(x->>'front') = '' or public.act_norm(x->>'back') = '') then return 'Có thẻ còn trống mặt trước hoặc mặt sau'; end if;
  when 'worksheet' then
    if jsonb_typeof(c->'fields') <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(c->'fields') not between 1 and 30 then return 'Cần 1–30 mục trả lời'; end if;
    if exists (select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(c->'fields') x where public.act_norm(x->>'label') = '' or coalesce(x->>'type', 'text') not in ('text','long','number')) then
      return 'Có mục trả lời chưa đặt tên'; end if;
  else return 'Loại hoạt động không hợp lệ';
  end case;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func ai_begin(p_task text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ai_begin(p_task text)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare st jsonb;
begin
  if auth.uid() is null then raise exception 'Chưa đăng nhập' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if p_task is null or p_task not in ('lesson','quiz','comment','document','chat','help') then raise exception 'Tác vụ không hợp lệ' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  st := case when p_task = 'help' then public.ai_help_status() else public.ai_status() end;
  if st ->> 'reason' is not null then
    raise exception '%', st ->> 'reason' using errcode = case when (st ->> 'allowed')::boolean then 'P0001' else '42501' end;
  end if;
  return st || jsonb_build_object('org_id', public.my_org_id(), 'user_id', auth.uid());
end $function$
;
-- ##### func ai_help_status()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ai_help_status()
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare st jsonb := public.ai_status(); s ai_settings;
begin
  select * into s from ai_settings where id = 1;
  if s.help_all_roles and auth.uid() is not null then
    st := st || jsonb_build_object('allowed', true);
    if st ->> 'reason' = 'Vai trò của bạn không dùng trợ lý AI.' then st := st || jsonb_build_object('reason', null); end if;
  end if;
  return st;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func ai_status()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.ai_status()
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare s ai_settings; l ai_org_limits; v_org uuid := public.my_org_id(); v_quota int; v_used_org int; v_used_me int; v_allowed boolean;
begin
  select * into s from ai_settings where id = 1;
  select * into l from ai_org_limits where org_id = v_org;
  v_quota := coalesce(l.monthly_quota, s.org_monthly_quota);
  select count(*) into v_used_org from ai_usage where org_id = v_org and ok and created_at >= date_trunc('month', now());
  select count(*) into v_used_me from ai_usage where user_id = auth.uid() and ok and created_at >= date_trunc('day', now());
  v_allowed := public.is_platform_admin() or public.my_portals() && s.portals;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'enabled', s.enabled and coalesce(l.enabled, true), 'allowed', v_allowed, 'model', s.model, 'provider', s.provider,
    'fallback_provider', s.fallback_provider, 'fallback_model', s.fallback_model,
    'org_quota', v_quota, 'org_used', v_used_org, 'user_quota', s.user_daily_quota, 'user_used', v_used_me,
    'reason', case when not s.enabled then 'Trợ lý AI chưa được KDI bật.'
                   when not coalesce(l.enabled, true) then 'Trợ lý AI đang tắt cho đơn vị của bạn.'
                   when not v_allowed then 'Vai trò của bạn không dùng trợ lý AI.'
                   when v_used_org >= v_quota then 'Đơn vị đã dùng hết lượt của tháng này.'
                   when v_used_me >= s.user_daily_quota then 'Bạn đã dùng hết lượt hôm nay.'
              end);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func apply_invitations(p_user uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.apply_invitations(p_user uuid)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_email text; v_confirmed timestamptz; inv invitations; n int := 0; v_phone text;
begin
  select email, email_confirmed_at into v_email, v_confirmed from auth.users where id = p_user;
  if v_email is null or v_confirmed is null then return 0; end if;
  for inv in select * from invitations where lower(email) = lower(v_email) and status = 'pending' order by created_at loop
    insert into memberships (user_id, org_id, role_id, created_by)
      values (p_user, inv.org_id, inv.role_id, inv.invited_by) on conflict do nothing;
    update profiles set full_name = coalesce(nullif(trim(inv.full_name), ''), full_name)
     where id = p_user and (org_id = inv.org_id or full_name is null or full_name = split_part(v_email, '@', 1));
    if inv.teacher_id is not null then
      update teachers set profile_id = p_user where id = inv.teacher_id and (profile_id is null or profile_id = p_user);
    end if;
    if inv.student_id is not null then
      update students set profile_id = p_user where id = inv.student_id and (profile_id is null or profile_id = p_user);
    end if;
    if inv.guardian_id is not null then
      -- mọi dòng phụ huynh cùng SĐT ở trường (anh chị em) dùng chung tài khoản
      select phone_norm into v_phone from guardians where id = inv.guardian_id;
      perform set_config('app.login_admin', 'on', true);
      update guardians set profile_id = p_user
       where org_id = inv.org_id and profile_id is null and (id = inv.guardian_id or (v_phone is not null and phone_norm = v_phone));
      perform set_config('app.login_admin', '', true);
    end if;
    if inv.judge_id is not null then
      update competition_judges set profile_id = p_user where id = inv.judge_id and profile_id is null;
    end if;
    update invitations set status = 'accepted', accepted_at = now(), accepted_user = p_user, note = null where id = inv.id;
    n := n + 1;
  end loop;
  return n;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func apply_supply_move()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.apply_supply_move()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_qty numeric; v_delta numeric := case new.kind when 'out' then -new.qty else new.qty end;
begin
  select qty into v_qty from supplies where id = new.supply_id and org_id = new.org_id for update;
  if v_qty is null then raise exception 'Vật tư không thuộc đơn vị' using errcode = '23514'; end if;
  if v_qty + v_delta < 0 then raise exception 'Không đủ tồn kho (còn %)', v_qty using errcode = '23514'; end if;
  update supplies set qty = qty + v_delta where id = new.supply_id;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func approval_apply(p_request uuid, p_decision text, p_note text, p_sync boolean)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_apply(p_request uuid, p_decision text, p_note text, p_sync boolean)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare r approval_requests; n int; v_final text; st jsonb;
begin
  select * into r from approval_requests where id = p_request for update;
  if r.id is null then raise exception 'Không tìm thấy hồ sơ'; end if;
  if r.status <> 'pending' then raise exception 'Hồ sơ đã được xử lý' using errcode = '55000'; end if;
  if p_decision not in ('approve', 'reject', 'comment') then raise exception 'Quyết định không hợp lệ'; end if;
  n := jsonb_array_length(r.steps);
  st := r.steps -> (r.current_step - 1);
  -- Người duyệt bước hiện tại; riêng TỪ CHỐI thì Ban giám hiệu / quản trị của đơn vị gửi luôn làm được
  if not public.approval_can_act(r.id)
     and not (p_decision = 'reject' and public.my_org_id() = r.org_id and public.is_admin()) then
    raise exception 'Bạn không phải người duyệt bước "%"', st->>'name' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  insert into approval_actions (request_id, step_no, actor, decision, note) values (r.id, r.current_step, auth.uid(), p_decision, nullif(trim(p_note), ''));
  if p_decision = 'comment' then return jsonb_build_object('status', 'pending', 'step', r.current_step, 'steps', n); end if;

  if p_decision = 'approve' and r.current_step < n then
    update approval_requests set current_step = current_step + 1 where id = r.id;
    perform public.approval_notify_step(r.id);
    return jsonb_build_object('status', 'pending', 'step', r.current_step + 1, 'steps', n,
                              'next', r.steps -> r.current_step ->> 'name', 'next_org', r.steps -> r.current_step ->> 'org_name');
  end if;

  v_final := case p_decision when 'approve' then 'approved' else 'rejected' end;
  update approval_requests set status = v_final, decided_at = now() where id = r.id;
  if p_sync then
    perform set_config('app.approval_engine', 'on', true);
    execute format('update %I set status = $1 where id = $2', r.ref_table) using v_final, r.ref_id;
    perform set_config('app.approval_engine', 'off', true);
  end if;
  -- Báo người gửi (đăng ký STEM, phiếu mượn đã có thông báo riêng khi đổi trạng thái)
  if r.kind not in ('stem_registration', 'borrow_request') then
    perform public.notify_user(r.requested_by, r.org_id, 'status',
      public.approval_kind_label(r.kind) || case v_final when 'approved' then ' đã được duyệt' else ' bị từ chối' end,
      r.title || coalesce(' — ' || nullif(trim(p_note), ''), ''), 'Hộp phê duyệt', null, r.id);
  end if;
  return jsonb_build_object('status', v_final, 'step', r.current_step, 'steps', n);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func approval_build_steps(p_org uuid, p_kind text, p_workflow uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_build_steps(p_org uuid, p_kind text, p_workflow uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare s record; v jsonb := '[]'::jsonb; v_org uuid; n int := 0;
begin
  if p_workflow is not null then
    for s in select st.*, r.name as role_name from approval_steps st left join roles r on r.id = st.role_id
              where st.workflow_id = p_workflow order by st.step_no loop
      v_org := case when s.org_type is null then p_org else public.org_ancestor_of_type(p_org, s.org_type) end;
      continue when v_org is null;
      n := n + 1;
      v := v || jsonb_build_object('no', n, 'name', s.name, 'approver', s.approver, 'role_id', s.role_id, 'role_name', s.role_name,
                                   'org_id', v_org, 'org_name', (select name from organizations where id = v_org));
    end loop;
  end if;
  if n = 0 then
    v := jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('no', 1, 'name', 'Duyệt', 'approver', 'perm', 'role_id', null, 'role_name', null,
                                              'org_id', p_org, 'org_name', (select name from organizations where id = p_org)));
  end if;
  return v;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func approval_can_act(p_request uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_can_act(p_request uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare r approval_requests; st jsonb; v_org uuid;
begin
  select * into r from approval_requests where id = p_request;
  if r.id is null or r.status <> 'pending' then return false; end if;
  st := r.steps -> (r.current_step - 1);
  v_org := (st->>'org_id')::uuid;
  if v_org is distinct from public.my_org_id() then return false; end if;
  if st->>'approver' = 'role' then
    return exists (select 1 from memberships m where m.user_id = auth.uid() and m.org_id = v_org and m.role_id = (st->>'role_id')::uuid);
  end if;
  return public.has_perm(public.approval_kind_module(r.kind), 'edit');
end $function$
;
-- ##### func approval_cancel(p_request uuid, p_note text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_cancel(p_request uuid, p_note text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare r approval_requests;
begin
  select * into r from approval_requests where id = p_request for update;
  if r.requested_by is distinct from auth.uid() or r.status <> 'pending' then raise exception 'Không rút được hồ sơ này' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  insert into approval_actions (request_id, step_no, actor, decision, note) values (r.id, r.current_step, auth.uid(), 'cancel', p_note);
  update approval_requests set status = 'cancelled', decided_at = now() where id = r.id;
  perform set_config('app.approval_engine', 'on', true);
  execute format('update %I set status = $1 where id = $2', r.ref_table)
    using case when r.ref_table in ('package_orders', 'education_plans', 'supply_requests', 'stock_transfers', 'student_projects') then 'draft' else 'rejected' end, r.ref_id;
  perform set_config('app.approval_engine', 'off', true);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func approval_decide(p_request uuid, p_decision text, p_note text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_decide(p_request uuid, p_decision text, p_note text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select public.approval_apply(p_request, p_decision, p_note, true)
$function$
;
-- ##### func approval_decide_ref(p_table text, p_ref uuid, p_decision text, p_note text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_decide_ref(p_table text, p_ref uuid, p_decision text, p_note text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v uuid;
begin
  select id into v from approval_requests where ref_table = p_table and ref_id = p_ref and status = 'pending';
  if v is null then raise exception 'Không có hồ sơ chờ duyệt' using errcode = '55000'; end if;
  return public.approval_apply(v, p_decision, p_note, true);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func approval_effective_workflow(p_org uuid, p_kind text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_effective_workflow(p_org uuid, p_kind text)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with recursive up as (
    select id, parent_id, 0 as d from organizations where id = p_org
    union all
    select o.id, o.parent_id, up.d + 1 from organizations o join up on o.id = up.parent_id where up.d < 10
  )
  select w.id from up join approval_workflows w on w.org_id = up.id and w.kind = p_kind and w.is_active
   where up.d = 0 or w.applies_to_children
   order by up.d limit 1
$function$
;
-- ##### func approval_inbox()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_inbox()
 RETURNS SETOF approval_requests
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select r.* from approval_requests r
   where r.status = 'pending'
     and (r.steps -> (r.current_step - 1) ->> 'org_id')::uuid = public.my_org_id()
     and public.approval_can_act(r.id)
   order by r.created_at
$function$
;
-- ##### func approval_kind_label(p_kind text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_kind_label(p_kind text)
 RETURNS text
 LANGUAGE sql
 IMMUTABLE
AS $function$
  select case p_kind when 'stem_registration' then 'Đăng ký dạy STEM' when 'borrow_request' then 'Phiếu mượn thiết bị'
    when 'package_purchase' then 'Đề xuất mua gói STEM' when 'edu_plan' then 'Kế hoạch giáo dục' when 'project' then 'Dự án STEM'
    when 'supply_purchase' then 'Đề xuất mua vật tư' when 'stock_transfer' then 'Phiếu vận chuyển' end
$function$
;
-- ##### func approval_kind_module(p_kind text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_kind_module(p_kind text)
 RETURNS text
 LANGUAGE sql
 IMMUTABLE
AS $function$
  select case p_kind when 'stem_registration' then 'stem_registrations' when 'borrow_request' then 'stem_rooms'
    when 'package_purchase' then 'packages' when 'edu_plan' then 'edu_plan' when 'project' then 'projects'
    when 'supply_purchase' then 'equipment' when 'stock_transfer' then 'equipment' end
$function$
;
-- ##### func approval_notify_step(p_request uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_notify_step(p_request uuid)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare r approval_requests; st jsonb; v_org uuid; v_portal text; u uuid;
begin
  select * into r from approval_requests where id = p_request;
  st := r.steps -> (r.current_step - 1);
  v_org := (st->>'org_id')::uuid;
  v_portal := case when (select type from organizations where id = v_org) = 'school'
                   then coalesce((select portal from roles where id = (st->>'role_id')::uuid), 'school_admin') else 'department' end;
  for u in select distinct m.user_id from memberships m
            where m.org_id = v_org
              and case when st->>'approver' = 'role' then m.role_id = (st->>'role_id')::uuid
                       else public.user_has_perm(m.user_id, public.approval_kind_module(r.kind), 'edit', v_org) end loop
    perform public.notify_user(u, v_org, 'approval', public.approval_kind_label(r.kind) || ' chờ duyệt'
      || case when jsonb_array_length(r.steps) > 1 then format(' (bước %s/%s)', r.current_step, jsonb_array_length(r.steps)) else '' end,
      r.title, 'Hộp phê duyệt', v_portal, r.id);
  end loop;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func approval_open(p_org uuid, p_kind text, p_table text, p_ref uuid, p_title text, p_summary text, p_by uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_open(p_org uuid, p_kind text, p_table text, p_ref uuid, p_title text, p_summary text, p_by uuid)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_wf uuid; v_id uuid;
begin
  update approval_requests set status = 'cancelled', decided_at = now() where ref_table = p_table and ref_id = p_ref and status = 'pending';
  v_wf := public.approval_effective_workflow(p_org, p_kind);
  insert into approval_requests (org_id, kind, ref_table, ref_id, title, summary, requested_by, workflow_id, steps)
    values (p_org, p_kind, p_table, p_ref, p_title, p_summary, p_by, v_wf, public.approval_build_steps(p_org, p_kind, v_wf))
    returning id into v_id;
  perform public.approval_notify_step(v_id);
  return v_id;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func approval_reset_workflow(p_kind text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_reset_workflow(p_kind text)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if not (public.is_admin() or public.is_authority()) then raise exception 'Chỉ quản trị đơn vị được đặt quy trình' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  delete from approval_workflows where org_id = public.my_org_id() and kind = p_kind;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func approval_save_workflow(p_kind text, p_name text, p_applies boolean, p_steps jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_save_workflow(p_kind text, p_name text, p_applies boolean, p_steps jsonb)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_org uuid := public.my_org_id(); v_wf uuid; s jsonb; i int := 0; v_type text;
begin
  if not (public.is_admin() or public.is_authority()) then raise exception 'Chỉ quản trị đơn vị được đặt quy trình' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if jsonb_typeof(p_steps) <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(p_steps) = 0 then raise exception 'Quy trình cần ít nhất một bước'; end if;
  select type into v_type from organizations where id = v_org;
  insert into approval_workflows (org_id, kind, name, applies_to_children, updated_at, updated_by)
    values (v_org, p_kind, coalesce(nullif(trim(p_name), ''), public.approval_kind_label(p_kind)), coalesce(p_applies, false) and v_type <> 'school', now(), auth.uid())
    on conflict (org_id, kind) do update set name = excluded.name, applies_to_children = excluded.applies_to_children,
      is_active = true, updated_at = now(), updated_by = auth.uid()
    returning id into v_wf;
  delete from approval_steps where workflow_id = v_wf;
  for s in select * from jsonb_array_elements(p_steps) loop
    i := i + 1;
    if s->>'approver' = 'role' and not exists (
         select 1 from roles r where r.id = (s->>'role_id')::uuid and (r.org_id is null or r.org_id = v_org)) then
      raise exception 'Vai trò ở bước % không hợp lệ', i;
    end if;
    insert into approval_steps (workflow_id, step_no, name, approver, role_id, org_type)
      values (v_wf, i, coalesce(nullif(trim(s->>'name'), ''), 'Bước ' || i), s->>'approver',
              case when s->>'approver' = 'role' then (s->>'role_id')::uuid end, nullif(s->>'org_type', ''));
  end loop;
  return v_wf;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func approval_source_deleted()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_source_deleted()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  update approval_requests set status = 'cancelled', decided_at = now() where ref_table = tg_table_name and ref_id = old.id and status = 'pending';
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func approval_source_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_source_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_req uuid; res jsonb;
begin
  if coalesce(current_setting('app.approval_engine', true), 'off') = 'on' then return new; end if;
  if new.status is not distinct from old.status or new.status not in ('approved', 'rejected') then return new; end if;
  select id into v_req from approval_requests where ref_table = tg_table_name and ref_id = new.id and status = 'pending';
  if v_req is null then
    if old.status in ('approved', 'lent', 'returned') and new.status = 'rejected' and tg_table_name = 'borrow_requests' then return new; end if;
    raise exception 'Bản ghi phải được gửi duyệt và duyệt theo quy trình' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  res := public.approval_apply(v_req, case new.status when 'approved' then 'approve' else 'reject' end, null, false);
  new.status := case res->>'status' when 'pending' then 'pending' else res->>'status' end;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func approval_source_opened()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approval_source_opened()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_kind text; v_title text; v_sum text; v_by uuid;
begin
  if new.status <> 'pending' or (tg_op = 'UPDATE' and old.status = 'pending') then return null; end if;
  if tg_table_name = 'stem_registrations' then
    v_kind := 'stem_registration'; v_title := new.topic;
    v_sum := public.teacher_name(new.teacher_id) || ' · ' || coalesce((select name from classes where id = new.class_id), '') || ' · ' || public.vn_date(new.on_date);
    v_by := public.teacher_profile(new.teacher_id);
  elsif tg_table_name = 'borrow_requests' then
    v_kind := 'borrow_request'; v_title := new.title;
    v_sum := coalesce(public.teacher_name(new.teacher_id), '') || ' · ' || public.vn_date(new.on_date);
    v_by := coalesce(public.teacher_profile(new.teacher_id), auth.uid());
  elsif tg_table_name = 'package_orders' then
    v_kind := 'package_purchase'; v_title := new.name;
    v_sum := concat_ws(' · ', 'Số lượng ' || new.quantity, nullif(new.programs, ''), 'Khối ' || nullif(new.grades, ''),
                       case when new.budget is not null then replace(to_char(new.budget, 'FM999,999,999,999'), ',', '.') || ' đ' end);
    v_by := coalesce(new.created_by, auth.uid());
  elsif tg_table_name = 'education_plans' then
    v_kind := 'edu_plan';
    v_title := public.teacher_name(new.teacher_id) || ' · ' || new.subject || ' · Khối ' || new.grade;
    v_sum := new.stem_periods || ' tiết STEM';
    v_by := auth.uid();
  elsif tg_table_name = 'supply_requests' then
    v_kind := 'supply_purchase'; v_title := new.title;
    select concat_ws(' · ', count(*) || ' mặt hàng',
                     case when sum(qty * est_price) > 0 then 'dự kiến ' || replace(to_char(sum(qty * est_price), 'FM999,999,999,999'), ',', '.') || ' đ' end,
                     case when new.needed_by is not null then 'cần trước ' || public.vn_date(new.needed_by) end)
      into v_sum from supply_request_lines where request_id = new.id;
    v_by := coalesce(new.created_by, auth.uid());
  elsif tg_table_name = 'stock_transfers' then
    v_kind := 'stock_transfer'; v_title := new.title;
    select (select name from organizations where id = new.org_id) || ' → ' || (select name from organizations where id = new.to_org)
           || ' · ' || count(*) || ' dòng' into v_sum from stock_transfer_items where transfer_id = new.id;
    v_by := coalesce(new.created_by, auth.uid());
  elsif tg_table_name = 'student_projects' then
    v_kind := 'project'; v_title := new.title;
    v_sum := concat_ws(' · ', nullif(new.field, ''), (select string_agg(s.full_name, ', ' order by s.full_name) from project_members pm join students s on s.id = pm.student_id where pm.project_id = new.id),
                       'GVHD ' || public.teacher_name(new.mentor_teacher_id));
    v_by := coalesce(new.created_by, auth.uid());
  end if;
  perform public.approval_open(new.org_id, v_kind, tg_table_name, new.id, v_title, v_sum, v_by);
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func attendance_summary(p_from date, p_to date)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.attendance_summary(p_from date, p_to date)
 RETURNS TABLE(org_id uuid, school text, ward text, parent_name text, planned integer, taught integer, not_marked integer, cancelled integer, makeup integer, stem_planned integer, stem_taught integer, stem_room_periods integer, present integer, absent integer, late integer, excused integer, teachers_not_marked integer)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if not public.is_staff() then raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if p_from is null or p_to is null or p_to < p_from or p_to - p_from > 400 then
    raise exception 'Khoảng ngày không hợp lệ' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  return query
  with sc as (
    select o.id, o.name, o.ward, par.name as parent_name from organizations o
    left join organizations par on par.id = o.parent_id
    where o.type = 'school' and o.id in (select public.my_org_tree())
  ), s as (
    select ts.*, (ts.session_date + p.starts_at) at time zone 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' as starts_at
    from teaching_sessions ts join period_configs p on p.id = ts.period_id
    where ts.org_id in (select id from sc) and ts.session_date between p_from and p_to
  )
  select sc.id, sc.name, sc.ward, sc.parent_name,
    count(s.id) filter (where s.status <> 'cancelled' and s.starts_at <= now())::int,
    count(s.id) filter (where s.status = 'taught')::int,
    count(s.id) filter (where s.status = 'scheduled' and s.starts_at <= now())::int,
    count(s.id) filter (where s.status = 'cancelled')::int,
    count(s.id) filter (where s.is_makeup and s.status <> 'cancelled')::int,
    count(s.id) filter (where s.kind = 'stem' and s.status <> 'cancelled' and s.starts_at <= now())::int,
    count(s.id) filter (where s.kind = 'stem' and s.status = 'taught')::int,
    count(s.id) filter (where s.room_id is not null and s.status = 'taught')::int,
    coalesce(sum(s.present_count), 0)::int, coalesce(sum(s.absent_count), 0)::int,
    coalesce(sum(s.late_count), 0)::int, coalesce(sum(s.excused_count), 0)::int,
    count(distinct s.teacher_id) filter (where s.status = 'scheduled' and s.starts_at <= now())::int
  from sc left join s on s.org_id = sc.id
  group by sc.id, sc.name, sc.ward, sc.parent_name
  order by sc.name;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func audit_commerce()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.audit_commerce()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare
  r jsonb := case when tg_op = 'DELETE' then to_jsonb(old) else to_jsonb(new) end;
  o jsonb := case when tg_op = 'UPDATE' then to_jsonb(old) end;
  v_detail jsonb; v_sum text; v_org uuid; k text;
begin
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' then
    v_detail := '{}'::jsonb;
    for k in select jsonb_object_keys(r) loop
      if r->k is distinct from o->k and k not in ('updated_at') then
        v_detail := v_detail || jsonb_build_object(k, jsonb_build_object('from', o->k, 'to', r->k));
      end if;
    end loop;
    if v_detail = '{}'::jsonb then return null; end if;
  else v_detail := r; end if;
  if tg_table_name = 'contracts' then
    v_org := (r->>'org_id')::uuid;
    v_sum := case tg_op when 'INSERT' then 'Tạo hợp đồng ' when 'DELETE' then 'Xoá hợp đồng ' else 'Sửa hợp đồng ' end
             || (r->>'code') || ' · ' || coalesce((select name from organizations where id = v_org), '?');
  elsif tg_table_name = 'service_plans' then
    v_sum := case tg_op when 'INSERT' then 'Tạo gói dịch vụ ' when 'DELETE' then 'Xoá gói dịch vụ ' else 'Sửa gói dịch vụ ' end || (r->>'code') || ' · ' || (r->>'name');
  else
    v_sum := 'Đổi cài đặt bảo mật';
  end if;
  insert into audit_log (actor, org_id, entity, entity_id, action, summary, detail)
    values (auth.uid(), v_org, tg_table_name, r->>'id', lower(tg_op), v_sum, v_detail);
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func audit_role_permissions()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.audit_role_permissions()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare r jsonb := case when tg_op = 'DELETE' then to_jsonb(old) else to_jsonb(new) end; v_role text; a text;
begin
  select name into v_role from roles where id = (r->>'role_id')::uuid;
  a := concat_ws(', ', case when (r->>'can_view')::boolean then 'xem' end, case when (r->>'can_detail')::boolean then 'chi tiết' end,
                 case when (r->>'can_create')::boolean then 'tạo' end, case when (r->>'can_edit')::boolean then 'sửa' end,
                 case when (r->>'can_delete')::boolean then 'xoá' end);
  insert into audit_log (actor, org_id, entity, entity_id, action, summary, detail)
    values (auth.uid(), public.platform_org_id(), 'role_permissions', (r->>'role_id') || '/' || (r->>'module'), lower(tg_op),
            'Quyền mặc định: ' || coalesce(v_role, '?') || ' · ' || (r->>'module') || ' → ' || case when tg_op = 'DELETE' or a = '' then 'không có quyền' else a end,
            case when tg_op = 'UPDATE' then jsonb_build_object('from', to_jsonb(old), 'to', to_jsonb(new)) else r end);
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func audit_row()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.audit_row()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare
  r jsonb := case when tg_op = 'DELETE' then to_jsonb(old) else to_jsonb(new) end;
  o jsonb := case when tg_op = 'UPDATE' then to_jsonb(old) end;
  v_org uuid; v_sum text; v_detail jsonb; v_name text; v_user text; v_role text; k text;
  act text := lower(tg_op);
begin
  if tg_table_name = 'roles' and r->>'org_id' is null then return null; end if;   -- chỉ vai trò tuỳ chỉnh
  if tg_table_name = 'organizations' then
    v_org := (r->>'id')::uuid;
    v_sum := case tg_op when 'INSERT' then 'Tạo đơn vị ' when 'DELETE' then 'Xoá đơn vị ' else 'Sửa đơn vị ' end || (r->>'name');
  else
    v_org := (r->>'org_id')::uuid;
    select name into v_name from organizations where id = v_org;
    select name into v_role from roles where id = (r->>'role_id')::uuid;
    if tg_table_name = 'memberships' then
      select coalesce(full_name, email) into v_user from profiles where id = (r->>'user_id')::uuid;
      v_sum := case tg_op when 'INSERT' then 'Gán vai trò ' else 'Gỡ vai trò ' end || coalesce(v_role, '?')
               || ' cho ' || coalesce(v_user, 'tài khoản đã xoá') || ' tại ' || coalesce(v_name, '?');
    elsif tg_table_name = 'invitations' then
      v_sum := case when tg_op = 'INSERT' then 'Mời ' when tg_op = 'DELETE' then 'Xoá lời mời ' else
                 case r->>'status' when 'accepted' then 'Đã nhận lời mời: ' when 'cancelled' then 'Huỷ lời mời ' else 'Cập nhật lời mời ' end end
               || (r->>'email') || ' · ' || coalesce(v_role, '?') || ' tại ' || coalesce(v_name, '?');
    else
      v_sum := case tg_op when 'INSERT' then 'Tạo vai trò ' when 'DELETE' then 'Xoá vai trò ' else 'Sửa vai trò ' end
               || (r->>'name') || coalesce(' tại ' || v_name, '');
    end if;
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' then
    v_detail := '{}'::jsonb;
    for k in select jsonb_object_keys(r) loop
      if r->k is distinct from o->k and k not in ('updated_at') then
        v_detail := v_detail || jsonb_build_object(k, jsonb_build_object('from', o->k, 'to', r->k));
      end if;
    end loop;
    if v_detail = '{}'::jsonb then return null; end if;
    -- lời mời: chỉ ghi khi đổi trạng thái
    if tg_table_name = 'invitations' and not v_detail ? 'status' then return null; end if;
  else
    v_detail := r;
  end if;
  insert into audit_log (actor, org_id, entity, entity_id, action, summary, detail)
    values (auth.uid(), v_org, tg_table_name, coalesce(r->>'id', (r->>'user_id') || '/' || (r->>'role_id')), act, v_sum, v_detail);
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func audit_s6()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.audit_s6()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare r jsonb := case when tg_op = 'DELETE' then to_jsonb(old) else to_jsonb(new) end; v_sum text;
begin
  if to_regclass('public.audit_log') is null then return null; end if;
  if tg_table_name = 'school_holidays' then
    v_sum := case tg_op when 'INSERT' then 'Thêm ngày nghỉ ' when 'DELETE' then 'Xoá ngày nghỉ ' else 'Sửa ngày nghỉ ' end
      || (r->>'name') || ' (' || to_char((r->>'starts_on')::date, 'DD/MM/YYYY')
      || case when r->>'ends_on' <> r->>'starts_on' then '–' || to_char((r->>'ends_on')::date, 'DD/MM/YYYY') else '' end || ')';
  else
    v_sum := case tg_op when 'INSERT' then 'Giao bài tự học ' when 'DELETE' then 'Xoá bài tự học ' else 'Sửa bài tự học ' end
      || (r->>'title') || ' · ' || coalesce((select name from classes where id = (r->>'class_id')::uuid), '?');
  end if;
  insert into audit_log (actor, org_id, entity, entity_id, action, summary, detail)
    values (auth.uid(), (r->>'org_id')::uuid, tg_table_name, r->>'id', lower(tg_op), v_sum, r);
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func audit_wave2()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.audit_wave2()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare r jsonb := case when tg_op = 'DELETE' then to_jsonb(old) else to_jsonb(new) end; v_sum text; v_org uuid;
begin
  if tg_table_name = 'rollout_checks' then
    v_org := (r->>'org_id')::uuid;
    v_sum := case when tg_op = 'DELETE' then 'Bỏ đánh dấu bước triển khai ' else 'Đánh dấu bước triển khai ' end || (r->>'step')
             || ' · ' || coalesce((select name from organizations where id = v_org), '?');
  elsif tg_table_name = 'integrations' then
    v_sum := 'Cập nhật kết nối ' || (r->>'name') || ' → ' || (r->>'status');
  else
    v_org := (r->>'org_id')::uuid;
    v_sum := case tg_op when 'INSERT' then 'Thêm gói vào danh mục ' when 'DELETE' then 'Xoá gói khỏi danh mục ' else 'Sửa gói danh mục ' end || (r->>'name');
    if v_org is distinct from public.platform_org_id() then return null; end if;
  end if;
  insert into audit_log (actor, org_id, entity, entity_id, action, summary, detail)
    values (auth.uid(), v_org, tg_table_name, coalesce(r->>'id', (r->>'org_id') || '/' || (r->>'step'), r->>'key'), lower(tg_op), v_sum, r);
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func backup_overview()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.backup_overview()
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if not public.is_platform_admin() then raise exception 'Chỉ quản trị nền tảng' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'db_bytes', pg_database_size(current_database()),
    'tables', (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(t order by t.bytes desc), '[]'::jsonb) from (
        select c.relname as name, greatest(c.reltuples, 0)::bigint as rows, pg_total_relation_size(c.oid) as bytes
          from pg_class c join pg_namespace n on n.oid = c.relnamespace
         where n.nspname = 'public' and c.relkind = 'r' order by pg_total_relation_size(c.oid) desc limit 15) t),
    'exports_30d', (select count(*) from data_exports where created_at > now() - interval '30 days'),
    'orgs_exported_90d', (select count(distinct org_id) from data_exports where created_at > now() - interval '90 days'),
    'last_export', (select max(created_at) from data_exports)
  );
end $function$
;
-- ##### func bank_usable(p_q_org uuid, p_shared boolean, p_org uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.bank_usable(p_q_org uuid, p_shared boolean, p_org uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select p_q_org = p_org or (p_shared and p_q_org in (
    with recursive up as (select parent_id as id from organizations where id = p_org
      union all select o.parent_id from organizations o join up on o.id = up.id where o.parent_id is not null)
    select id from up where id is not null))
$function$
;
-- ##### func booking_put(p_org uuid, p_room uuid, p_holder uuid, p_during tstzrange, p_source text, p_source_id uuid, p_label text, p_soft boolean)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_put(p_org uuid, p_room uuid, p_holder uuid, p_during tstzrange, p_source text, p_source_id uuid, p_label text, p_soft boolean)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare b resource_bookings; v_room text;
begin
  begin
    insert into resource_bookings (org_id, room_id, holder_id, during, source, source_id, label)
    values (p_org, p_room, p_holder, p_during, p_source, p_source_id, p_label);
    delete from booking_conflicts where source = p_source and source_id = p_source_id;
    return true;
  exception when exclusion_violation then
    select * into b from resource_bookings
     where room_id = p_room and during && p_during and holder_id <> p_holder
     order by lower(during) limit 1;
    if p_soft then
      insert into booking_conflicts (org_id, room_id, holder_id, during, source, source_id, label, blocking_source, blocking_id, blocking_label)
      values (p_org, p_room, p_holder, p_during, p_source, p_source_id, p_label, b.source, b.source_id, b.label)
      on conflict (source, source_id) do update set room_id = excluded.room_id, holder_id = excluded.holder_id,
        during = excluded.during, label = excluded.label, blocking_source = excluded.blocking_source,
        blocking_id = excluded.blocking_id, blocking_label = excluded.blocking_label, detected_at = now();
      return false;
    end if;
    select name into v_room from stem_rooms where id = p_room;
    raise exception '% đã có lịch lúc %: %', coalesce(v_room, 'Phòng'), public.slot_text(b.during), coalesce(b.label, 'tiết khác')
      using errcode = '23P01', hint = 'Chọn phòng hoặc tiết khác, hoặc huỷ lịch đang giữ phòng.';
  end;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func booking_refresh_borrow(p_id uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_refresh_borrow(p_id uuid)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare b borrow_requests; v_label text;
begin
  delete from resource_bookings where source = 'borrow_request' and source_id = p_id;
  delete from booking_conflicts where source = 'borrow_request' and source_id = p_id;
  select * into b from borrow_requests where id = p_id;
  if found and b.mode = 'in_room' and b.room_id is not null and b.status in ('pending', 'approved', 'lent') then
    select concat_ws(' · ', 'Phiếu mượn', c.name, b.title) into v_label from (select 1) x left join classes c on c.id = b.class_id;
    perform public.booking_put(b.org_id, b.room_id, coalesce(b.class_id, b.teacher_id, b.id), public.period_slot(b.on_date, b.period_id),
      'borrow_request', b.id, v_label, coalesce(current_setting('kdi.booking_soft', true), '') = 'on');
  end if;
  perform public.booking_retry('borrow_request', p_id);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func booking_refresh_registration(p_id uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_refresh_registration(p_id uuid)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare r stem_registrations; v_label text;
begin
  delete from resource_bookings where source = 'stem_registration' and source_id = p_id;
  delete from booking_conflicts where source = 'stem_registration' and source_id = p_id;
  select * into r from stem_registrations where id = p_id;
  if found and r.room_id is not null and r.status in ('pending', 'approved') then
    select concat_ws(' · ', 'Đăng ký dạy STEM', c.name, r.topic) into v_label from classes c where c.id = r.class_id;
    perform public.booking_put(r.org_id, r.room_id, r.class_id, public.period_slot(r.on_date, r.period_id),
      'stem_registration', r.id, v_label, coalesce(current_setting('kdi.booking_soft', true), '') = 'on');
  end if;
  perform public.booking_retry('stem_registration', p_id);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func booking_refresh_session(p_id uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_refresh_session(p_id uuid)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare s teaching_sessions; v_soft boolean; v_label text;
begin
  delete from resource_bookings where source = 'teaching_session' and source_id = p_id;
  delete from booking_conflicts where source = 'teaching_session' and source_id = p_id;
  select * into s from teaching_sessions where id = p_id;
  if found and s.room_id is not null and s.status <> 'cancelled' then
    v_soft := s.timetable_entry_id is not null and not s.is_makeup
      and s.room_id is not distinct from (select e.room_id from timetable_entries e where e.id = s.timetable_entry_id);
    select concat_ws(' · ', p.name, c.name, s.subject) into v_label
      from period_configs p, classes c where p.id = s.period_id and c.id = s.class_id;
    perform public.booking_put(s.org_id, s.room_id, s.class_id, public.period_slot(s.session_date, s.period_id),
      'teaching_session', s.id, coalesce(v_label, s.subject), v_soft);
  end if;
  perform public.booking_retry('teaching_session', p_id);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func booking_retry(p_source text, p_source_id uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_retry(p_source text, p_source_id uuid)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare c booking_conflicts;
begin
  for c in select * from booking_conflicts where blocking_source = p_source and blocking_id = p_source_id order by detected_at loop
    perform public.booking_put(c.org_id, c.room_id, c.holder_id, c.during, c.source, c.source_id, c.label, true);
  end loop;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func booking_sync()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_sync()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_id uuid := coalesce(new.id, old.id);
begin
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' then
    -- Chỉ đồng bộ lại khi đổi thứ liên quan đến lịch (điểm danh, ghi chú… không đụng sổ giữ chỗ)
    -- (IF lồng nhau: mỗi bảng có cột khác nhau, PL/pgSQL không được đọc cột của bảng khác)
    if tg_table_name = 'teaching_sessions' then
      if (old.room_id, old.class_id, old.session_date, old.period_id, old.status = 'cancelled', old.is_makeup, old.timetable_entry_id)
         is not distinct from (new.room_id, new.class_id, new.session_date, new.period_id, new.status = 'cancelled', new.is_makeup, new.timetable_entry_id) then return null; end if;
    elsif tg_table_name = 'stem_registrations' then
      if (old.room_id, old.class_id, old.on_date, old.period_id, old.status in ('pending', 'approved'))
         is not distinct from (new.room_id, new.class_id, new.on_date, new.period_id, new.status in ('pending', 'approved')) then return null; end if;
    else
      if (old.room_id, old.class_id, old.on_date, old.period_id, old.mode, old.status in ('pending', 'approved', 'lent'))
         is not distinct from (new.room_id, new.class_id, new.on_date, new.period_id, new.mode, new.status in ('pending', 'approved', 'lent')) then return null; end if;
    end if;
  end if;
  if tg_table_name = 'teaching_sessions' then perform public.booking_refresh_session(v_id);
  elsif tg_table_name = 'stem_registrations' then perform public.booking_refresh_registration(v_id);
  else perform public.booking_refresh_borrow(v_id); end if;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func borrow_stock_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.borrow_stock_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_msg text;
begin
  if new.status not in ('approved', 'lent') then return new; end if;
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and old.status in ('approved', 'lent')
     and (old.on_date, old.period_id, old.item_list) is not distinct from (new.on_date, new.period_id, new.item_list) then return new; end if;
  -- Xếp hàng theo trường: hai người duyệt cùng lúc không cùng lấy một thiết bị
  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('borrow_stock'), hashtext(new.org_id::text));
  select string_agg(format('%s: cần %s, dùng được %s, đã cho mượn %s', s.name, s.need, s.usable, s.held), '; ') into v_msg
    from public.equipment_shortage(new.org_id, new.on_date, new.period_id, new.item_list, new.id) s
   where s.need > s.usable - s.held;
  if v_msg is not null then
    raise exception 'Không đủ thiết bị cho khung giờ này — %', v_msg
      using errcode = '23P01', hint = 'Đổi ngày/tiết, giảm số lượng hoặc chờ phiếu khác trả thiết bị.';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func branding_of(p_org uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.branding_of(p_org uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with recursive up as (
    select id, parent_id, 0 as d from organizations where id = p_org
    union all
    select o.id, o.parent_id, up.d + 1 from organizations o join up on o.id = up.parent_id where up.d < 10
  )
  select jsonb_build_object('org_id', b.org_id, 'source', (select name from organizations where id = b.org_id), 'inherited', up.d > 0,
           'display_name', b.display_name, 'short_name', b.short_name, 'logo', b.logo, 'logo_dark', b.logo_dark,
           'primary_color', b.primary_color, 'sidebar_color', b.sidebar_color, 'accent_color', b.accent_color,
           'login_message', b.login_message, 'support_text', b.support_text, 'self_edit', b.self_edit and up.d = 0)
    from up join org_branding b on b.org_id = up.id
   where up.d = 0 or b.applies_to_children
   order by up.d limit 1
$function$
;
-- ##### func broadcast_targets(p_org_ids uuid[], p_org_types text[], p_role_keys text[])
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.broadcast_targets(p_org_ids uuid[], p_org_types text[], p_role_keys text[])
 RETURNS TABLE(user_id uuid, org_id uuid)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with recursive sub as (
    select o.id from organizations o where p_org_ids is null or o.id = any(p_org_ids)
    union
    select o.id from organizations o join sub s on o.parent_id = s.id where p_org_ids is not null
  )
  select distinct m.user_id, m.org_id
    from memberships m
    join organizations o on o.id = m.org_id
    join roles r on r.id = m.role_id
    join profiles p on p.id = m.user_id
   where m.org_id in (select id from sub)
     and (p_org_types is null or cardinality(p_org_types) = 0 or o.type = any(p_org_types))
     and (p_role_keys is null or cardinality(p_role_keys) = 0 or r.key = any(p_role_keys))
     and (o.status = 'active' or o.is_platform) and p.locked_at is null
$function$
;

$function$

-- ##### func can_view_project(p_project uuid)

$function$
;
-- ##### func class_competencies(p_class uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.class_competencies(p_class uuid)
 RETURNS TABLE(student_id uuid, full_name text, student_code text, competency text, evidences integer, avg_level numeric)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_org uuid;
begin
  select org_id into v_org from classes where id = p_class;
  if v_org is null then return; end if;
  if not (public.teaches_class(p_class) or (v_org = public.my_org_id() and public.is_staff() and public.has_perm('students', 'view'))
          or (public.is_authority() and v_org in (select public.my_org_tree())) or public.is_platform_admin()) then
    raise exception 'Không có quyền xem lớp này' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return query
    select s.id, s.full_name, s.student_code, e.competency, count(*)::int, round(avg(e.level), 2)
      from students s
      join public.competency_evidence(array(select id from students where class_id = p_class and coalesce(status, 'studying') = 'studying')) e
        on e.student_id = s.id
     group by s.id, s.full_name, s.student_code, e.competency;
end $function$
;

$function$
;

$function$
