-- ##### func competency_evidence(p_students uuid[])
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.competency_evidence(p_students uuid[])
 RETURNS TABLE(student_id uuid, competency text, level integer, assignment_id uuid, title text, graded_at timestamp with time zone)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select st.id, c.v->>'competency', (x.rubric_scores->>(c.i::int - 1))::int, a.id, a.title, x.graded_at
    from unnest(p_students) st(id)
    join submissions x on x.student_id = st.id
                       or x.group_id in (select m.group_id from student_group_members m where m.student_id = st.id)
    join assignments a on a.id = x.assignment_id
    cross join lateral jsonb_array_elements(a.rubric->'criteria') with ordinality c(v, i)
   where a.rubric is not null and x.status = 'graded' and jsonb_typeof(x.rubric_scores) = 'array'
     and coalesce(c.v->>'competency', '') <> '' and jsonb_typeof(x.rubric_scores->(c.i::int - 1)) = 'number'
$function$
;
-- ##### func competition_add_judge(p_comp uuid, p_name text, p_email text, p_fields text[])
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.competition_add_judge(p_comp uuid, p_name text, p_email text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_fields text[] DEFAULT '{}'::text[])
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare c competitions; v_pid uuid; v_id uuid;
begin
  select * into c from competitions where id = p_comp;
  if c.id is null or not public.can_manage_competition(c.org_id) then raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if coalesce(btrim(p_name), '') = '' then raise exception 'Cần họ tên giám khảo' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if nullif(btrim(p_email), '') is not null then select id into v_pid from profiles where lower(email) = lower(btrim(p_email)) limit 1; end if;
  if v_pid is not null and exists (select 1 from competition_judges where competition_id = c.id and profile_id = v_pid) then
    raise exception 'Người này đã là giám khảo' using errcode = '23505';
  end if;
  insert into competition_judges (competition_id, org_id, full_name, email, profile_id, fields)
    values (c.id, c.org_id, btrim(p_name), nullif(lower(btrim(p_email)), ''), v_pid, coalesce(p_fields, '{}')) returning id into v_id;
  if v_pid is not null then
    perform public.notify_user(v_pid, c.org_id, 'status', 'Bạn được mời làm giám khảo', c.title, 'Chấm thi', null, c.id);
  end if;
  return v_id;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func competition_advance(p_comp uuid, p_entries uuid[])
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.competition_advance(p_comp uuid, p_entries uuid[])
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare c competitions; n int;
begin
  select * into c from competitions where id = p_comp for update;
  if c.id is null or not public.can_manage_competition(c.org_id) then raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if c.current_round >= cardinality(c.rounds) then raise exception 'Đang ở vòng cuối — hãy trao giải' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  update competition_entries set score = (select round(avg(total), 2) from judge_assignments a where a.entry_id = competition_entries.id and a.round = c.current_round and a.submitted_at is not null)
   where competition_id = c.id and round = c.current_round and status = 'submitted';
  update competition_entries set round = c.current_round + 1 where competition_id = c.id and round = c.current_round and status = 'submitted' and id = any(p_entries);
  get diagnostics n = row_count;
  update competition_entries set status = 'eliminated' where competition_id = c.id and round = c.current_round and status = 'submitted';
  update competitions set current_round = current_round + 1, status = 'judging' where id = c.id;
  return n;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func competition_auto_assign(p_comp uuid, p_per integer)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.competition_auto_assign(p_comp uuid, p_per integer DEFAULT 2)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare c competitions; e record; j record; n int := 0; have int;
begin
  select * into c from competitions where id = p_comp;
  if c.id is null or not public.can_manage_competition(c.org_id) then raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  for e in select * from competition_entries where competition_id = c.id and round = c.current_round and status = 'submitted' order by field, code loop
    select count(*) into have from judge_assignments where entry_id = e.id and round = c.current_round;
    for j in select cj.id from competition_judges cj
              where cj.competition_id = c.id and (cardinality(cj.fields) = 0 or e.field = any(cj.fields))
                and not exists (select 1 from judge_assignments a where a.entry_id = e.id and a.judge_id = cj.id and a.round = c.current_round)
              order by (select count(*) from judge_assignments a where a.judge_id = cj.id and a.round = c.current_round), cj.created_at
              limit greatest(p_per - have, 0) loop
      insert into judge_assignments (entry_id, judge_id, round) values (e.id, j.id, c.current_round); n := n + 1;
    end loop;
  end loop;
  return n;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func competition_fees(p_year integer)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.competition_fees(p_year integer DEFAULT NULL::integer)
 RETURNS TABLE(competition_id uuid, title text, organizer text, fee numeric, entries integer, paid integer, unpaid integer, waived integer, collected numeric, outstanding numeric)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select c.id, c.title, o.name, c.fee,
         count(e.id) filter (where e.status <> 'withdrawn')::int,
         count(e.id) filter (where e.fee_status = 'paid')::int, count(e.id) filter (where e.fee_status = 'unpaid' and e.status <> 'withdrawn')::int,
         count(e.id) filter (where e.fee_status = 'waived')::int,
         c.fee * count(e.id) filter (where e.fee_status = 'paid'), c.fee * count(e.id) filter (where e.fee_status = 'unpaid' and e.status <> 'withdrawn')
    from competitions c join organizations o on o.id = c.org_id left join competition_entries e on e.competition_id = c.id
   where coalesce(c.fee, 0) > 0 and (public.is_platform_admin() or public.can_manage_competition(c.org_id))
     and (p_year is null or extract(year from c.created_at) = p_year)
   group by c.id, o.name order by c.created_at desc
$function$
;
-- ##### func competition_finish(p_comp uuid, p_awards jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.competition_finish(p_comp uuid, p_awards jsonb)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare c competitions; r record; n int := 0; u uuid;
begin
  select * into c from competitions where id = p_comp for update;
  if c.id is null or not public.can_manage_competition(c.org_id) then raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  for r in select * from public.competition_results(c.id) x where x.round = c.current_round and x.status = 'submitted' loop
    update competition_entries set score = r.avg_total, rank = r.rank_in_field,
           award = nullif(btrim(p_awards->>r.entry_id::text), ''), status = case when nullif(btrim(p_awards->>r.entry_id::text), '') is not null then 'awarded' else 'submitted' end
     where id = r.entry_id;
    if nullif(btrim(p_awards->>r.entry_id::text), '') is not null then
      n := n + 1;
      for u in select s.profile_id from project_members pm join students s on s.id = pm.student_id where pm.project_id = r.project_id and s.profile_id is not null
               union select t.profile_id from student_projects p join teachers t on t.id = p.mentor_teacher_id where p.id = r.project_id and t.profile_id is not null loop
        perform public.notify_user(u, (select org_id from student_projects where id = r.project_id), 'status', 'Dự án đạt ' || (p_awards->>r.entry_id::text), c.title || ' — ' || r.title, 'Dự án STEM', null, r.project_id);
      end loop;
    end if;
  end loop;
  update competitions set status = 'finished' where id = c.id;
  return n;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func competition_open_to(p_comp uuid, p_school uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.competition_open_to(p_comp uuid, p_school uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select exists (select 1 from competitions c join organizations o on o.id = c.org_id join organizations s on s.id = p_school
                  where c.id = p_comp and s.type = 'school'
                    and (o.is_platform or public.org_in_tree(p_school, c.org_id))
                    and (cardinality(c.grades) = 0 or s.level = any(c.grades)))
$function$
;
-- ##### func competition_results(p_comp uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.competition_results(p_comp uuid)
 RETURNS TABLE(entry_id uuid, code text, field text, title text, school text, members text, status text, round integer, judges integer, scored integer, avg_total numeric, spread numeric, rank_in_field integer, award text, fee_status text, docs jsonb, project_id uuid)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare c competitions;
begin
  select * into c from competitions where id = p_comp;
  if c.id is null or not public.can_manage_competition(c.org_id) then raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  return query
  with s as (
    select e.id, count(a.id)::int as judges, count(a.submitted_at)::int as scored,
           round(avg(a.total) filter (where a.submitted_at is not null), 2) as avg_total,
           max(a.total) filter (where a.submitted_at is not null) - min(a.total) filter (where a.submitted_at is not null) as spread
      from competition_entries e left join judge_assignments a on a.entry_id = e.id and a.round = c.current_round
     where e.competition_id = c.id group by e.id)
  select e.id, e.code, e.field, p.title, o.name,
         (select string_agg(st.full_name, ', ' order by st.full_name) from project_members pm join students st on st.id = pm.student_id where pm.project_id = p.id),
         e.status, e.round, s.judges, s.scored, s.avg_total, s.spread,
         case when s.avg_total is null or e.round <> c.current_round or e.status = 'withdrawn' then null
              else (rank() over (partition by e.field, (e.round = c.current_round and e.status <> 'withdrawn' and s.avg_total is not null) order by s.avg_total desc))::int end,
         e.award, e.fee_status, e.docs, p.id
    from competition_entries e join s on s.id = e.id join student_projects p on p.id = e.project_id join organizations o on o.id = e.school_id
   where e.competition_id = c.id
   order by e.field, s.avg_total desc nulls last, e.code;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func competition_submit(p_comp uuid, p_project uuid, p_field text, p_docs jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.competition_submit(p_comp uuid, p_project uuid, p_field text, p_docs jsonb DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare c competitions; p student_projects; n int; v_id uuid; v_code text; d jsonb; w int;
begin
  select * into c from competitions where id = p_comp for update;
  select * into p from student_projects where id = p_project;
  if c.id is null or p.id is null then raise exception 'Không tìm thấy cuộc thi / dự án' using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;
  if p.org_id <> public.my_org_id() or not (public.is_admin() or p.mentor_teacher_id = public.my_teacher_id()) then
    raise exception 'Chỉ quản trị trường hoặc GV hướng dẫn nộp được dự án của trường' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if c.status <> 'open' or (c.submit_from is not null and now() < c.submit_from) or (c.submit_until is not null and now() > c.submit_until) then
    raise exception 'Cuộc thi không trong thời gian nhận bài' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if not public.competition_open_to(c.id, p.org_id) then raise exception 'Trường không thuộc phạm vi / cấp học của cuộc thi' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if p.status not in ('approved', 'completed') then raise exception 'Dự án phải được trường duyệt trước khi dự thi' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if cardinality(c.fields) > 0 and not (p_field = any(c.fields)) then raise exception 'Lĩnh vực không có trong cuộc thi' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  select count(*) into n from project_members where project_id = p.id;
  if n = 0 or n > c.max_members then raise exception 'Dự án dự thi cần từ 1 đến % học sinh', c.max_members using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if c.max_per_school is not null and (select count(*) from competition_entries where competition_id = c.id and school_id = p.org_id and status <> 'withdrawn' and project_id <> p.id) >= c.max_per_school then
    raise exception 'Trường đã nộp đủ % dự án', c.max_per_school using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  for d in select * from jsonb_array_elements(c.required_docs) loop
    if coalesce((d->>'required')::boolean, true) and coalesce(p_docs->>(d->>'key'), '') !~* '^https?://' then
      raise exception 'Thiếu hồ sơ: %', d->>'label' using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
  end loop;
  if exists (select 1 from jsonb_each_text(coalesce(p_docs, '{}')) x where x.value <> '' and x.value !~* '^https?://') then
    raise exception 'Hồ sơ phải là đường link http(s)' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if c.abstract_words is not null then
    w := coalesce(array_length(regexp_split_to_array(btrim(coalesce(p.abstract, '')), '\s+'), 1), 0);
    if btrim(coalesce(p.abstract, '')) = '' then raise exception 'Dự án chưa có tóm tắt' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    if w > c.abstract_words then raise exception 'Tóm tắt % từ, vượt giới hạn % từ', w, c.abstract_words using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  end if;
  select id into v_id from competition_entries where competition_id = c.id and project_id = p.id;
  if v_id is not null then
    update competition_entries set field = p_field, docs = coalesce(p_docs, '{}'), abstract = p.abstract, status = 'submitted', submitted_at = now(), submitted_by = auth.uid() where id = v_id;
    return v_id;
  end if;
  v_code := upper(left(coalesce(nullif(regexp_replace(p_field, '[^[:alpha:]]', '', 'g'), ''), 'DA'), 2)) || '-' ||
            lpad(((select count(*) from competition_entries where competition_id = c.id) + 1)::text, 3, '0');
  insert into competition_entries (competition_id, project_id, school_id, field, code, docs, abstract, fee_status)
    values (c.id, p.id, p.org_id, p_field, v_code, coalesce(p_docs, '{}'), p.abstract, case when coalesce(c.fee, 0) > 0 then 'unpaid' else 'none' end)
    returning id into v_id;
  return v_id;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func competition_withdraw(p_entry uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.competition_withdraw(p_entry uuid)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  update competition_entries e set status = 'withdrawn'
   where e.id = p_entry and e.school_id = public.my_org_id() and public.is_staff() and e.status = 'submitted'
     and exists (select 1 from competitions c where c.id = e.competition_id and c.status = 'open');
  if not found then raise exception 'Không rút được bài này' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func consent_summary()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.consent_summary()
 RETURNS TABLE(school_id uuid, school_name text, students integer, consented integer)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select o.id, o.name,
         (select count(*) from students s where s.org_id = o.id and coalesce(s.status, 'studying') = 'studying')::int,
         (select count(*) from students s where s.org_id = o.id and coalesce(s.status, 'studying') = 'studying'
            and exists (select 1 from student_consents c where c.student_id = s.id and c.withdrawn_on is null))::int
    from organizations o
   where o.type = 'school'
     and (public.is_platform_admin()
          or (public.is_authority() and o.id in (select public.my_org_tree()))
          or (o.id = public.my_org_id() and public.has_perm('students', 'view')))
   order by o.name
$function$
;
-- ##### func content_for_student(p_org uuid, p_shared boolean, p_class_ids uuid[])
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.content_for_student(p_org uuid, p_shared boolean, p_class_ids uuid[])
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select public.is_student() and public.my_student_id() is not null and (
    (p_org = public.my_org_id() and (p_class_ids is null or cardinality(p_class_ids) = 0 or public.my_class_id() = any(p_class_ids)))
    or (p_shared and p_org in (select public.my_ancestor_orgs())))
$function$
;
-- ##### func copy_stem_package(p_src uuid, p_org uuid, p_catalog boolean, p_order_no text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.copy_stem_package(p_src uuid, p_org uuid, p_catalog boolean DEFAULT NULL::boolean, p_order_no text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_new uuid; t record; v_topic uuid;
begin
  insert into stem_packages (org_id, code, name, tier, provider, programs, grades, starts_on, ends_on, contract_no, order_no, topics, devices, sessions,
                             catalog_active, list_price, description, source_package_id)
    select p_org, code, name, tier, coalesce(provider, 'KDI Education'), programs, grades,
           case when p_catalog is null then current_date else starts_on end, case when p_catalog is null then null else ends_on end,
           contract_no, coalesce(p_order_no, order_no), topics, devices, sessions,
           p_catalog, case when p_catalog is null then null else list_price end, description, p_src
      from stem_packages where id = p_src
    returning id into v_new;
  if v_new is null then raise exception 'Không tìm thấy gói nguồn' using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;
  for t in select * from package_topics where package_id = p_src order by seq loop
    insert into package_topics (org_id, package_id, seq, program, title, minutes, grade_assigned)
      values (p_org, v_new, t.seq, t.program, t.title, t.minutes, case when p_catalog is null then null else t.grade_assigned end)
      returning id into v_topic;
    insert into package_topic_sessions (org_id, topic_id, seq, title, minutes)
      select p_org, v_topic, seq, title, minutes from package_topic_sessions where topic_id = t.id;
  end loop;
  return v_new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func course_manageable(p_course uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.course_manageable(p_course uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select exists (select 1 from courses c where c.id = p_course and public.can_manage_content(c.org_id, c.created_by))
$function$
;
-- ##### func course_visible(p_course uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.course_visible(p_course uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select exists (select 1 from courses c where c.id = p_course and (
    (public.is_staff() and (public.can_access_org(c.org_id) or (c.shared and c.org_id in (select public.my_ancestor_orgs()))))
    or (c.status = 'published' and public.content_for_student(c.org_id, c.shared, c.class_ids))
    or (c.status = 'published' and exists (select 1 from students s where public.is_guardian_of(s.id)
          and (s.org_id = c.org_id or (c.shared and c.org_id in (with recursive up as (select parent_id as id from organizations where id = s.org_id
             union all select o.parent_id from organizations o join up on o.id = up.id where o.parent_id is not null) select id from up)))))))
$function$
;
-- ##### func dept_notify_school(p_school uuid, p_title text, p_body text, p_rule text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.dept_notify_school(p_school uuid, p_title text, p_body text, p_rule text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare n int; v_from text;
begin
  if not public.is_authority() or p_school not in (select public.my_org_tree())
     or (select type from organizations where id = p_school) is distinct from 'school' then
    raise exception 'Chỉ gửi được cho trường trực thuộc' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if coalesce(btrim(p_title), '') = '' then raise exception 'Thiếu nội dung' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  select name into v_from from organizations where id = public.my_org_id();
  insert into notifications (org_id, user_id, kind, title, body, page, portal)
    select distinct p_school, m.user_id, 'status', left(btrim(p_title), 200), left(coalesce(v_from || ': ', '') || coalesce(p_body, ''), 1000), 'Dashboard Trường', 'school_admin'
      from memberships m join roles r on r.id = m.role_id
     where m.org_id = p_school and r.key in ('principal', 'it_admin');
  get diagnostics n = row_count;
  insert into dept_alert_actions (org_id, school_id, rule, status, note)
    values (public.my_org_id(), p_school, coalesce(p_rule, 'nhắc'), 'notified', left(btrim(p_title), 200));
  return n;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func dept_school_stats()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.dept_school_stats()
 RETURNS TABLE(school_id uuid, code text, name text, level text, ward text, activated boolean, teachers integer, stem_teachers integer, students integer, classes integer, stem_classes integer, stem_students integer, programs text[], rooms integer, rooms_active integer, stem_periods integer, total_periods integer, room_periods integer, lesson_plans integer, products integer, trained integer, training_done integer, attendance_rate numeric, units integer, units_broken integer, units_maintenance integer, units_available integer, graded integer, avg_score numeric, k12_synced boolean, has_year boolean, last_activity timestamp with time zone, kpi integer)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with s as (
    select o.* from organizations o
    where o.type = 'school' and public.is_staff() and public.can_access_org(o.id)
  ),
  cur as (select org_id, id from academic_years where is_current),
  base as (
    select s.id as school_id, s.code, s.name, s.level, s.ward, s.activated_at is not null as activated,
      (select count(*) from teachers t where t.org_id = s.id and t.status = 'active')::int as teachers,
      (select count(distinct teacher_id) from stem_teacher_programs x where x.org_id = s.id)::int as stem_teachers,
      (select count(*) from students x where x.org_id = s.id and coalesce(x.status,'studying') = 'studying')::int as students,
      (select count(*) from classes c where c.org_id = s.id)::int as classes,
      (select count(distinct class_id) from class_programs cp where cp.org_id = s.id)::int as stem_classes,
      (select count(*) from students x where x.org_id = s.id and coalesce(x.status,'studying') = 'studying' and x.class_id in (select class_id from class_programs cp where cp.org_id = s.id))::int as stem_students,
      coalesce((select array_agg(distinct program order by program) from class_programs cp where cp.org_id = s.id), '{}') as programs,
      (select count(*) from stem_rooms r where r.org_id = s.id)::int as rooms,
      (select count(*) from stem_rooms r where r.org_id = s.id and r.status = 'active')::int as rooms_active,
      (select count(*) from timetable_entries e where e.org_id = s.id and e.kind = 'stem'
         and (e.year_id is null or e.year_id in (select id from cur where cur.org_id = s.id)))::int as stem_periods,
      (select count(*) from timetable_entries e where e.org_id = s.id
         and (e.year_id is null or e.year_id in (select id from cur where cur.org_id = s.id)))::int as total_periods,
      (select count(*) from timetable_entries e where e.org_id = s.id and e.room_id is not null
         and (e.year_id is null or e.year_id in (select id from cur where cur.org_id = s.id)))::int as room_periods,
      (select count(*) from lesson_plans lp where lp.org_id = s.id and not lp.is_template and lp.status = 'published')::int as lesson_plans,
      (select count(*) from assignments a where a.org_id = s.id and a.kind = 'product')::int as products,
      (select count(distinct en.teacher_id) from training_enrollments en join teachers t on t.id = en.teacher_id where t.org_id = s.id)::int as trained,
      (select count(distinct en.teacher_id) from training_enrollments en join teachers t on t.id = en.teacher_id where t.org_id = s.id and en.completed)::int as training_done,
      (select round(100.0 * count(*) filter (where ta.present) / nullif(count(*) filter (where ta.present is not null), 0), 1)
         from training_attendance ta join teachers t on t.id = ta.teacher_id where t.org_id = s.id) as attendance_rate,
      (select count(*) from equipment_units u where u.org_id = s.id)::int as units,
      (select count(*) from equipment_units u where u.org_id = s.id and u.status = 'broken')::int as units_broken,
      (select count(*) from equipment_units u where u.org_id = s.id and u.status = 'maintenance')::int as units_maintenance,
      (select count(*) from equipment_units u where u.org_id = s.id and u.status = 'available')::int as units_available,
      (select count(*) from submissions x where x.org_id = s.id and x.status = 'graded')::int as graded,
      (select round(avg(x.score / nullif(a.max_score, 0) * 10), 2) from submissions x join assignments a on a.id = x.assignment_id
         where x.org_id = s.id and x.status = 'graded') as avg_score,
      exists (select 1 from teachers t where t.org_id = s.id and t.k12_synced_at is not null) as k12_synced,
      exists (select 1 from academic_years y where y.org_id = s.id) as has_year,
      greatest((select max(created_at) from stem_registrations x where x.org_id = s.id),
               (select max(submitted_at) from submissions x where x.org_id = s.id),
               (select max(started_at) from class_sessions x where x.org_id = s.id),
               (select max(updated_at) from lesson_plans x where x.org_id = s.id)) as last_activity
    from s
  )
  -- KPI tổng hợp 0–100: độ phủ CT (25) + phòng STEM (15) + tiết STEM/lớp (20) + tập huấn (20) + giáo án (10) + đồng bộ K12 (10)
  select b.*,
    round(
      25 * least(coalesce(array_length(b.programs, 1), 0), 5) / 5.0
    + 15 * least(b.rooms_active, 2) / 2.0
    + 20 * least(b.stem_periods::numeric / nullif(b.classes, 0), 2) / 2.0
    + 20 * coalesce(b.training_done::numeric / nullif(b.teachers, 0), 0)
    + 10 * least(b.lesson_plans::numeric / nullif(b.teachers, 0) * 2, 1)
    + 10 * (case when b.k12_synced then 1 else 0 end)
    )::int as kpi
  from base b
  order by b.name
$function$
;
-- ##### func dept_subject_stats()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.dept_subject_stats()
 RETURNS TABLE(subject text, total integer, stem integer, classes integer, schools integer, students integer)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select e.subject, count(*)::int, count(*) filter (where e.kind = 'stem')::int,
    count(distinct e.class_id) filter (where e.kind = 'stem')::int, count(distinct e.org_id) filter (where e.kind = 'stem')::int,
    (select count(*) from students st where st.class_id in (select class_id from timetable_entries x where x.subject = e.subject and x.kind = 'stem'
       and public.can_access_org(x.org_id)))::int
  from timetable_entries e
  where public.is_staff() and public.can_access_org(e.org_id)
    and (e.year_id is null or e.year_id in (select id from academic_years where is_current))
  group by e.subject
  order by 3 desc
$function$
;
-- ##### func end_class_session(p_session uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.end_class_session(p_session uuid)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  update class_sessions set status = 'ended', ended_at = now()
   where id = p_session and status = 'live' and public.teaches_class(class_id);
  if not found then raise exception 'Không tìm thấy phiên đang mở của lớp bạn dạy'; end if;
end $function$
;

 LANGUAGE sql

AS $function$

$function$
;
-- ##### func equipment_availability(p_day date, p_period uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.equipment_availability(p_day date, p_period uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid)
 RETURNS TABLE(type_id uuid, usable integer, held integer, free integer)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select t.id,
         count(u.id) filter (where u.status not in ('broken', 'maintenance', 'lost', 'in_transit'))::int,
         coalesce(h.qty, 0)::int,
         greatest(count(u.id) filter (where u.status not in ('broken', 'maintenance', 'lost', 'in_transit')) - coalesce(h.qty, 0), 0)::int
    from equipment_types t
    left join equipment_units u on u.type_id = t.id and u.org_id = t.org_id
    left join (
      select (i->>'type_id')::uuid as type_id, sum(greatest(coalesce((i->>'qty')::int, 0), 0)) as qty
        from borrow_requests b, jsonb_array_elements(b.item_list) i
       where b.org_id = public.my_org_id() and b.status in ('approved', 'lent') and b.on_date = p_day
         and public.period_slot(b.on_date, b.period_id) && public.period_slot(p_day, p_period)
       group by 1) h on h.type_id = t.id
   where t.org_id = public.my_org_id() and public.is_staff()
   group by t.id, h.qty
$function$
;
-- ##### func equipment_overview()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.equipment_overview()
 RETURNS TABLE(org_id uuid, school text, parent_name text, types integer, units integer, available integer, in_use integer, in_stock integer, broken integer, maintenance integer, warranty_expiring integer, warranty_expired integer, open_tickets integer, borrow_pending integer, borrow_lent integer)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if not public.is_staff() then raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  return query
  with sc as (select o.id, o.name, par.name as parent_name from organizations o left join organizations par on par.id = o.parent_id
               where o.type = 'school' and o.id in (select public.my_org_tree())),
       u as (select * from equipment_units where equipment_units.org_id in (select id from sc))
  select sc.id, sc.name, sc.parent_name,
    (select count(*) from equipment_types t where t.org_id = sc.id)::int,
    (select count(*) from u where u.org_id = sc.id)::int,
    (select count(*) from u where u.org_id = sc.id and u.status = 'available')::int,
    (select count(*) from u where u.org_id = sc.id and u.status = 'in_use')::int,
    (select count(*) from u where u.org_id = sc.id and u.status = 'in_stock')::int,
    (select count(*) from u where u.org_id = sc.id and u.status = 'broken')::int,
    (select count(*) from u where u.org_id = sc.id and u.status = 'maintenance')::int,
    (select count(*) from u where u.org_id = sc.id and u.warranty_until between current_date and current_date + 60)::int,
    (select count(*) from u where u.org_id = sc.id and u.warranty_until < current_date)::int,
    (select count(*) from warranty_tickets w where w.org_id = sc.id and w.status not in ('resolved', 'rejected', 'closed'))::int,
    (select count(*) from borrow_requests b where b.org_id = sc.id and b.status = 'pending')::int,
    (select count(*) from borrow_requests b where b.org_id = sc.id and b.status = 'lent')::int
  from sc order by sc.name;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func equipment_shortage(p_org uuid, p_day date, p_period uuid, p_items jsonb, p_skip uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.equipment_shortage(p_org uuid, p_day date, p_period uuid, p_items jsonb, p_skip uuid)
 RETURNS TABLE(type_id uuid, name text, need integer, usable integer, held integer)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with want as (
    select (i->>'type_id')::uuid as type_id, sum(greatest(coalesce((i->>'qty')::int, 0), 0))::int as qty
      from jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(p_items, '[]'::jsonb)) i where (i->>'type_id') is not null group by 1
  ), held as (
    select (i->>'type_id')::uuid as type_id, sum(greatest(coalesce((i->>'qty')::int, 0), 0))::int as qty
      from borrow_requests b, jsonb_array_elements(b.item_list) i
     where b.org_id = p_org and b.id is distinct from p_skip and b.status in ('approved', 'lent')
       and b.on_date = p_day and public.period_slot(b.on_date, b.period_id) && public.period_slot(p_day, p_period)
     group by 1
  )
  select w.type_id, t.name, w.qty,
         (select count(*) from equipment_units u where u.type_id = w.type_id and u.org_id = p_org and u.status not in ('broken', 'maintenance', 'lost', 'in_transit'))::int,
         coalesce(h.qty, 0)
    from want w join equipment_types t on t.id = w.type_id left join held h on h.type_id = w.type_id
$function$
;
-- ##### func equipment_type_summary()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.equipment_type_summary()
 RETURNS TABLE(name text, category text, brand text, schools integer, units integer, usable integer, broken integer, maintenance integer)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if not public.is_staff() then raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  return query
  select t.name, t.category, min(t.brand), count(distinct t.org_id)::int, count(u.id)::int,
         count(u.id) filter (where u.status not in ('broken', 'maintenance', 'lost', 'in_transit'))::int,
         count(u.id) filter (where u.status = 'broken')::int, count(u.id) filter (where u.status = 'maintenance')::int
    from equipment_types t join organizations o on o.id = t.org_id and o.type = 'school'
    left join equipment_units u on u.type_id = t.id
   where t.org_id in (select public.my_org_tree())
   group by t.name, t.category
   order by count(u.id) desc, t.name;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func erase_guardians()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.erase_guardians()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if new.status = 'erased' and old.status is distinct from 'erased' then
    delete from invitations where guardian_id in (select id from guardians where student_id = new.id);
    delete from guardians where student_id = new.id;
  end if;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func event_for_me(p_event uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.event_for_me(p_event uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select exists (select 1 from events e where e.id = p_event and e.status = 'published'
    and public.my_org_id() is not null
    and (e.org_id = public.my_org_id() or (e.include_children and public.org_in_tree(public.my_org_id(), e.org_id)))
    and e.audience && public.my_event_roles())
$function$
;
-- ##### func event_register(p_event uuid, p_student uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.event_register(p_event uuid, p_student uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare e events; v_role text; v_name text; v_student uuid; v_id uuid; n int;
begin
  select * into e from events where id = p_event for update;
  if e.id is null or not public.event_for_me(p_event) then raise exception 'Sự kiện không dành cho bạn' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if not e.registration then raise exception 'Sự kiện không cần đăng ký' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if e.reg_deadline is not null and now() > e.reg_deadline then raise exception 'Đã hết hạn đăng ký' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if now() > coalesce(e.ends_at, e.starts_at) then raise exception 'Sự kiện đã diễn ra' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if p_student is not null then
    if not public.is_guardian_of(p_student) or not ('parent' = any(e.audience) or 'student' = any(e.audience)) then
      raise exception 'Chỉ đăng ký được cho con của mình' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
    v_role := 'parent'; v_student := p_student; select full_name into v_name from students where id = p_student;
  elsif public.my_student_id() is not null and 'student' = any(e.audience) then
    v_role := 'student'; v_student := public.my_student_id(); select full_name into v_name from students where id = v_student;
  elsif public.my_teacher_id() is not null and 'teacher' = any(e.audience) then
    v_role := 'teacher'; select full_name into v_name from teachers where id = public.my_teacher_id();
  elsif public.is_staff() and 'staff' = any(e.audience) then
    v_role := 'staff'; select full_name into v_name from profiles where id = auth.uid();
  else raise exception 'Bạn không thuộc đối tượng đăng ký' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  select id into v_id from event_registrations where event_id = e.id and profile_id = auth.uid() and student_id is not distinct from v_student;
  if v_id is not null then
    update event_registrations set status = 'registered' where id = v_id and status = 'cancelled';
  end if;
  select count(*) into n from event_registrations where event_id = e.id and status in ('registered', 'attended') and id is distinct from v_id;
  if e.capacity is not null and n >= e.capacity then raise exception 'Đã đủ số lượng đăng ký' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if v_id is null then
    insert into event_registrations (event_id, org_id, profile_id, student_id, role, full_name)
      values (e.id, public.my_org_id(), auth.uid(), v_student, v_role, coalesce(v_name, 'Người tham gia')) returning id into v_id;
  end if;
  return v_id;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func event_unregister(p_event uuid, p_student uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.event_unregister(p_event uuid, p_student uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  update event_registrations set status = 'cancelled'
   where event_id = p_event and profile_id = auth.uid() and status = 'registered'
     and student_id is not distinct from coalesce(p_student, public.my_student_id())
$function$
;
-- ##### func exam_paper(p_attempt exam_attempts)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.exam_paper(p_attempt exam_attempts)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', q.id, 'stem', q.stem, 'options', q.options, 'kind', q.kind) order by o.n), '[]'::jsonb)
    from unnest(p_attempt.questions) with ordinality o(qid, n) join question_bank q on q.id = o.qid
$function$
;
-- ##### func exam_result(p_attempt uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.exam_result(p_attempt uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare a exam_attempts; e practice_exams; v_reveal boolean;
begin
  select * into a from exam_attempts where id = p_attempt;
  if not found or not (public.can_view_student(a.student_id) or (public.is_staff() and exists (select 1 from practice_exams x where x.id = a.exam_id and public.can_access_org(x.org_id)))) then raise exception 'Không tìm thấy lượt làm bài' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  select * into e from practice_exams where id = a.exam_id;
  v_reveal := a.status = 'submitted' and (public.exam_reveal(e) or (public.is_staff() and public.can_access_org(e.org_id)));
  return jsonb_build_object('attempt', a.id, 'exam', e.title, 'status', a.status, 'score', a.score, 'correct', a.correct, 'total', a.total,
    'submitted_at', a.submitted_at, 'late', a.late, 'answers', a.answers, 'revealed', v_reveal,
    'questions', case when v_reveal then (select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', q.id, 'stem', q.stem, 'options', q.options, 'kind', q.kind,
        'answer', q.answer, 'explain', q.explain) order by o.n) from unnest(a.questions) with ordinality o(qid, n) join question_bank q on q.id = o.qid)
      else public.exam_paper(a) end);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func exam_reveal(e practice_exams)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.exam_reveal(e practice_exams)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE
AS $function$
  select e.show_answers = 'after_submit' or (e.show_answers = 'after_close' and (e.status = 'closed' or (e.closes_at is not null and now() > e.closes_at)))
$function$
;
-- ##### func exam_start(p_exam uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.exam_start(p_exam uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare e practice_exams; a exam_attempts; v_st uuid := public.my_student_id(); v_org uuid := public.my_org_id(); v_q uuid[]; v_n int; k text; v_need int;
begin
  if not public.is_student() or v_st is null then raise exception 'Chỉ học sinh làm đề luyện' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  select * into e from practice_exams where id = p_exam and status = 'published' and opens_at <= now() and public.content_for_student(org_id, shared, class_ids);
  if not found then raise exception 'Không tìm thấy đề luyện dành cho em' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if e.closes_at is not null and now() > e.closes_at then raise exception 'Đề luyện đã đóng' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  -- Lượt đang làm còn hạn: làm tiếp
  select * into a from exam_attempts where exam_id = e.id and student_id = v_st and status = 'in_progress' order by started_at desc limit 1;
  if found and (a.deadline is null or now() < a.deadline) then
    return jsonb_build_object('attempt', a.id, 'deadline', a.deadline, 'questions', public.exam_paper(a), 'answers', a.answers, 'title', e.title);
  elsif found then
    perform public.exam_submit(a.id, a.answers);
  end if;
  if e.max_attempts > 0 and (select count(*) from exam_attempts where exam_id = e.id and student_id = v_st) >= e.max_attempts then
    raise exception 'Em đã hết lượt làm đề này' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if e.mode = 'fixed' then
    select array_agg(q.id order by o.n) into v_q from unnest(e.question_ids) with ordinality o(qid, n) join question_bank q on q.id = o.qid where q.status <> 'retired';
  else
    v_n := (e.criteria ->> 'count')::int;
    v_q := '{}';
    -- Theo số câu từng mức độ (nếu có), phần còn lại lấy ngẫu nhiên
    for k, v_need in select key, value::int from jsonb_each_text(coalesce(e.criteria -> 'levels', '{}'::jsonb)) where value ~ '^\d+$' loop
      v_q := v_q || array(select q.id from question_bank q
        where q.status = 'ready' and q.level = k and public.bank_usable(q.org_id, q.shared, e.org_id)
          and (e.subject is null or q.subject = e.subject) and (e.grade is null or q.grade = e.grade)
          and (jsonb_array_length(coalesce(e.criteria -> 'topics', '[]')) = 0 or q.topic in (select jsonb_array_elements_text(e.criteria -> 'topics')))
          and not q.id = any(v_q)
        order by random() limit v_need);
    end loop;
    if cardinality(v_q) < v_n then
      v_q := v_q || array(select q.id from question_bank q
        where q.status = 'ready' and public.bank_usable(q.org_id, q.shared, e.org_id)
          and (e.subject is null or q.subject = e.subject) and (e.grade is null or q.grade = e.grade)
          and (jsonb_array_length(coalesce(e.criteria -> 'topics', '[]')) = 0 or q.topic in (select jsonb_array_elements_text(e.criteria -> 'topics')))
          and not q.id = any(v_q)
        order by random() limit v_n - cardinality(v_q));
    end if;
    v_q := v_q[1:v_n];
  end if;
  if coalesce(cardinality(v_q), 0) = 0 then raise exception 'Đề chưa có câu hỏi phù hợp — báo thầy cô' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  insert into exam_attempts (exam_id, student_id, org_id, questions, deadline)
    values (e.id, v_st, v_org, v_q, case when e.duration_min is not null then now() + make_interval(mins => e.duration_min) end)
    returning * into a;
  return jsonb_build_object('attempt', a.id, 'deadline', a.deadline, 'questions', public.exam_paper(a), 'answers', null, 'title', e.title);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func exam_stats(p_exam uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.exam_stats(p_exam uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare e practice_exams;
begin
  select * into e from practice_exams where id = p_exam;
  if not found or not (public.is_platform_admin() or (public.is_staff() and public.can_access_org(e.org_id))) then
    raise exception 'Không có quyền xem thống kê đề này' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'attempts', (select count(*) from exam_attempts where exam_id = e.id and status = 'submitted'),
    'students', (select count(distinct student_id) from exam_attempts where exam_id = e.id and status = 'submitted'),
    'avg', (select round(avg(score), 2) from exam_attempts where exam_id = e.id and status = 'submitted'),
    'schools', (select count(distinct org_id) from exam_attempts where exam_id = e.id),
    'items', (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(x order by (x ->> 'rate')::numeric), '[]'::jsonb) from (
      select jsonb_build_object('id', q.id, 'stem', left(q.stem, 160), 'level', q.level, 'shown', count(*),
        'rate', round(100.0 * count(*) filter (where (select coalesce(array_agg(v::int order by v::int), '{}') from jsonb_array_elements_text(coalesce(t.answers -> q.id::text, '[]')) v where v ~ '^\d+$')
                                         = (select array_agg(v::int order by v::int) from jsonb_array_elements_text(q.answer) v)) / count(*), 0)) as x
        from exam_attempts t cross join lateral unnest(t.questions) qid join question_bank q on q.id = qid
       where t.exam_id = e.id and t.status = 'submitted' group by q.id) s));
end $function$
;
-- ##### func exam_submit(p_attempt uuid, p_answers jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.exam_submit(p_attempt uuid, p_answers jsonb)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare a exam_attempts; e practice_exams; v_ok int := 0; v_total int; r record; v_given jsonb;
begin
  select * into a from exam_attempts where id = p_attempt for update;
  if not found or a.student_id is distinct from public.my_student_id() then
    raise exception 'Không tìm thấy lượt làm bài' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if a.status = 'submitted' then return public.exam_result(a.id); end if;
  if p_answers is not null and (jsonb_typeof(p_answers) <> 'object' or length(p_answers::text) > 50000) then raise exception 'Bài làm không hợp lệ' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  select * into e from practice_exams where id = a.exam_id;
  v_total := cardinality(a.questions);
  for r in select q.id, q.answer from question_bank q where q.id = any(a.questions) loop
    v_given := coalesce(p_answers -> r.id::text, '[]'::jsonb);
    if jsonb_typeof(v_given) = 'array'
       and (select coalesce(array_agg(x::int order by x::int), '{}') from jsonb_array_elements_text(v_given) x where x ~ '^\d+$')
         = (select array_agg(x::int order by x::int) from jsonb_array_elements_text(r.answer) x) then
      v_ok := v_ok + 1;
    end if;
  end loop;
  update exam_attempts set answers = p_answers, status = 'submitted', submitted_at = now(), correct = v_ok, total = v_total,
    score = case when v_total > 0 then round(10.0 * v_ok / v_total, 1) end,
    late = a.deadline is not null and now() > a.deadline + interval '2 minutes'
  where id = a.id;
  return public.exam_result(a.id);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func fill_post_author()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.fill_post_author()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  new.author_name := coalesce((select full_name from profiles where id = new.author_id), 'Người dùng');
  new.author_role := case when public.is_student() then 'student' else 'teacher' end;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func generate_teaching_sessions(p_from date, p_to date)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.generate_teaching_sessions(p_from date, p_to date)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_org uuid := public.my_org_id();
begin
  if v_org is null or not public.is_staff() then raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if (select type from organizations where id = v_org) is distinct from 'school' then
    raise exception 'Chỉ sinh buổi dạy cho trường học' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if not public.org_has_module(v_org, 'timetable') then raise exception 'Đơn vị chưa dùng phân hệ Thời khoá biểu' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  return public.sync_teaching_sessions(v_org, p_from, p_to);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func generate_upcoming_sessions(p_days integer)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.generate_upcoming_sessions(p_days integer DEFAULT 28)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_today date := public.vn_today(); v_to date; s record; r jsonb; v_run bigint;
  n_sch int := 0; n_err int := 0; c int := 0; u int := 0; d int := 0; v_errs jsonb := '[]';
begin
  if p_days is null or p_days < 1 or p_days > 62 then raise exception 'Số ngày phải từ 1 đến 62' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  v_to := v_today + p_days;
  insert into system_job_runs (job) values ('generate_upcoming_sessions') returning id into v_run;
  for s in select o.id, o.code from organizations o
            where o.type = 'school' and o.status = 'active' and public.org_has_module(o.id, 'timetable')
            order by o.code loop
    begin
      r := public.sync_teaching_sessions(s.id, v_today, v_to);
      n_sch := n_sch + 1;
      c := c + (r->>'created')::int; u := u + (r->>'updated')::int; d := d + (r->>'removed')::int;
    exception when others then                       -- một trường lỗi không chặn các trường khác
      n_err := n_err + 1;
      v_errs := v_errs || jsonb_build_object('school', s.code, 'error', sqlerrm);
    end;
  end loop;
  r := jsonb_build_object('from', v_today, 'to', v_to, 'schools', n_sch, 'failed', n_err,
                          'created', c, 'updated', u, 'removed', d, 'errors', v_errs);
  update system_job_runs set finished_at = now(), ok = (n_err = 0), result = r where id = v_run;
  delete from system_job_runs where job = 'generate_upcoming_sessions' and started_at < now() - interval '90 days';
  return r;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func grant_platform_admin(p_email text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.grant_platform_admin(p_email text)
 RETURNS text
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_uid uuid; v_org uuid; v_role uuid;
begin
  select id into v_uid from profiles where lower(email) = lower(trim(p_email));
  if v_uid is null then return 'Chưa có tài khoản ' || p_email || ' — hãy đăng ký/đăng nhập một lần rồi chạy lại.'; end if;
  select id into v_org from organizations where is_platform;
  select id into v_role from roles where key = 'platform_admin' and org_id is null;
  insert into memberships (user_id, org_id, role_id, created_by) values (v_uid, v_org, v_role, null) on conflict do nothing;
  return 'Đã cấp Quản trị nền tảng cho ' || p_email || '. Chọn "KDI Education" ở ô Đang làm việc tại (nếu có nhiều đơn vị).';
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_class_session()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_class_session()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if current_user = 'authenticated' then
    new.pin := old.pin; new.class_id := old.class_id; new.org_id := old.org_id; new.teacher_id := old.teacher_id;
    new.started_at := old.started_at;
    if old.status = 'ended' then new.status := 'ended'; end if;
    if new.status = 'ended' and old.status = 'live' then new.ended_at := now(); end if;
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_competition()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_competition()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  new.updated_at := now();
  if exists (select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(new.criteria) c where coalesce(c->>'key', '') = '' or coalesce((c->>'max')::numeric, 0) <= 0) then
    raise exception 'Tiêu chí chấm cần mã và điểm tối đa > 0' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and old.status in ('finished', 'cancelled') and new.status = old.status and new.criteria is distinct from old.criteria then
    raise exception 'Cuộc thi đã kết thúc' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and new.criteria is distinct from old.criteria and exists (select 1 from judge_assignments a join competition_entries e on e.id = a.entry_id
       where e.competition_id = new.id and a.submitted_at is not null) then
    raise exception 'Đã có giám khảo chấm — không đổi được tiêu chí' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_contract_milestone()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_contract_milestone()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  new.updated_at := now(); new.updated_by := auth.uid();
  if new.status in ('accepted', 'invoiced', 'paid') and new.accepted_on is null then new.accepted_on := current_date; end if;
  if new.status in ('invoiced', 'paid') and new.invoiced_on is null and new.invoice_no is not null then new.invoiced_on := current_date; end if;
  if new.status = 'invoiced' and new.invoiced_on is null then new.invoiced_on := current_date; end if;
  if new.status = 'paid' then
    if new.paid_on is null then new.paid_on := current_date; end if;
    if new.paid_amount is null then new.paid_amount := new.amount; end if;
  end if;
  if new.status in ('planned', 'cancelled') then new.paid_on := null; new.paid_amount := null; end if;
  if new.status = 'planned' then new.accepted_on := null; new.invoiced_on := null; end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_course()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_course()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then new.created_by := coalesce(new.created_by, auth.uid()); else new.created_by := old.created_by; new.org_id := old.org_id; end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_event()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_event()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  new.updated_at := now();
  if new.room_id is not null and not exists (select 1 from stem_rooms where id = new.room_id and org_id = new.org_id) then
    raise exception 'Phòng không thuộc đơn vị tổ chức' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and old.status = 'cancelled' and new.status <> 'cancelled' then
    raise exception 'Sự kiện đã huỷ' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_exam()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_exam()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_count int;
begin
  if new.mode = 'random' then
    v_count := coalesce((new.criteria ->> 'count')::int, 0);
    if v_count < 1 or v_count > 200 then raise exception 'Đề ngẫu nhiên cần số câu từ 1 đến 200' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then new.created_by := coalesce(new.created_by, auth.uid()); else new.created_by := old.created_by; new.org_id := old.org_id; end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_guardian()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_guardian()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  select org_id into new.org_id from students where id = new.student_id;
  if new.org_id is null then raise exception 'Không có học sinh này' using errcode = '23503'; end if;
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and new.profile_id is distinct from old.profile_id
     and coalesce(current_setting('app.login_admin', true), '') <> 'on' and auth.uid() is not null then
    new.profile_id := old.profile_id;          -- tài khoản chỉ gắn qua lời mời / tạo tài khoản mã
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' and auth.uid() is not null and coalesce(current_setting('app.login_admin', true), '') <> 'on' then
    new.profile_id := null;
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_inventory_refs()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_inventory_refs()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if tg_table_name = 'supply_moves' then
    if (new.teacher_id is not null and not exists (select 1 from teachers where id = new.teacher_id and org_id = new.org_id))
       or (new.class_id is not null and not exists (select 1 from classes where id = new.class_id and org_id = new.org_id)) then
      raise exception 'Người nhận / lớp không thuộc trường' using errcode = '23514';
    end if;
  elsif new.room_id is not null and not exists (select 1 from stem_rooms where id = new.room_id and org_id = new.org_id) then
    raise exception 'Phòng không thuộc trường' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_invitation_update()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_invitation_update()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if current_user = 'authenticated' then
    new.email := old.email; new.org_id := old.org_id; new.role_id := old.role_id; new.invited_by := old.invited_by;
    new.accepted_at := old.accepted_at; new.accepted_user := old.accepted_user;
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_last_platform_admin()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_last_platform_admin()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if exists (select 1 from roles where id = old.role_id and key = 'platform_admin' and org_id is null)
     and (select count(*) from memberships m join roles r on r.id = m.role_id
           where r.key = 'platform_admin' and r.org_id is null) <= 1
     and exists (select 1 from profiles where id = old.user_id) then
    raise exception 'Phải còn ít nhất một quản trị nền tảng' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  return old;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_legal_published()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_legal_published()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if tg_op = 'DELETE' and old.published_at is not null then raise exception 'Không xoá được bản đã công bố' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and old.published_at is not null
     and (new.body is distinct from old.body or new.title is distinct from old.title or new.version is distinct from old.version
          or new.kind is distinct from old.kind or new.published_at is distinct from old.published_at) then
    raise exception 'Bản đã công bố không sửa được — hãy tạo phiên bản mới' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return coalesce(new, old);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_membership()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_membership()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if not public.role_fits_org(new.role_id, new.org_id) then
    raise exception 'Vai trò không áp dụng cho loại tổ chức này' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_notification()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_notification()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
begin
  if current_user = 'authenticated' then
    new.user_id := old.user_id; new.kind := old.kind; new.title := old.title; new.body := old.body;
    new.page := old.page; new.portal := old.portal; new.ref_id := old.ref_id; new.count := old.count;
    new.created_at := old.created_at; new.org_id := old.org_id;
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_order_fulfillment()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_order_fulfillment()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$   -- definer: đọc được danh mục KDI để kiểm tra
begin
  if current_setting('role', true) <> 'authenticated' or public.is_platform_admin() then return new; end if;
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
    new.fulfillment := null; new.kdi_note := null; new.quoted_value := null; new.delivered_at := null; new.delivered_package_id := null;
  elsif (new.fulfillment, new.kdi_note, new.quoted_value, new.delivered_at, new.delivered_package_id)
        is distinct from (old.fulfillment, old.kdi_note, old.quoted_value, old.delivered_at, old.delivered_package_id) then
    raise exception 'Chỉ KDI cập nhật được phần xử lý đơn' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if new.catalog_package_id is not null
     and (tg_op = 'INSERT' or new.catalog_package_id is distinct from old.catalog_package_id)
     and not exists (select 1 from stem_packages where id = new.catalog_package_id and org_id = public.platform_org_id() and catalog_active) then
    raise exception 'Gói không có trong danh mục đang bán' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_org()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_org()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_ptype text;
begin
  if new.parent_id is not null then
    select type into v_ptype from organizations where id = new.parent_id;
    if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and exists (
      with recursive d as (select id from organizations where id = new.id
                           union all select o.id from organizations o join d on o.parent_id = d.id)
      select 1 from d where id = new.parent_id) then
      raise exception 'Không thể đặt đơn vị dưới chính đơn vị con của nó' using errcode = '23514';
    end if;
  end if;
  if not public.org_parent_ok(new.type, v_ptype) then
    raise exception 'Đơn vị loại % không thể nằm dưới %', new.type, coalesce(v_ptype, 'gốc') using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  if new.is_platform and new.status <> 'active' then
    raise exception 'Không tạm dừng đơn vị chủ nền tảng' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_org_branding()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_org_branding()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  new.domains := array(select distinct lower(btrim(d)) from unnest(coalesce(new.domains, '{}')) d where btrim(d) <> '' order by 1);
  if exists (select 1 from unnest(new.domains) d where d !~ '^[a-z0-9]([a-z0-9-]*[a-z0-9])?(\.[a-z0-9]([a-z0-9-]*[a-z0-9])?)+$') then
    raise exception 'Tên miền không hợp lệ' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from org_branding b where b.org_id <> new.org_id and b.domains && new.domains) then
    raise exception 'Tên miền đã gắn với đơn vị khác' using errcode = '23505';
  end if;
  if not public.is_platform_admin() and auth.uid() is not null then
    -- đơn vị tự sửa: không đổi quyền tự sửa, tên miền, phạm vi áp dụng
    if tg_op = 'INSERT' then raise exception 'Chỉ KDI tạo thương hiệu cho đơn vị' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
    if new.self_edit is distinct from old.self_edit or new.domains is distinct from old.domains or new.org_id <> old.org_id then
      raise exception 'Tên miền và quyền tự sửa do KDI quản lý' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
  end if;
  new.updated_at := now(); new.updated_by := auth.uid();
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_org_kdi_fields()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_org_kdi_fields()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if current_user = 'authenticated' and not public.is_platform_admin()
     and (new.kdi_owner is distinct from old.kdi_owner or new.go_live_on is distinct from old.go_live_on
          or new.customer_status is distinct from old.customer_status) then
    raise exception 'Chỉ KDI Education đổi người phụ trách, ngày vận hành, trạng thái khách hàng' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_profile_lock()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_profile_lock()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if (new.locked_at, new.locked_reason, new.locked_by) is distinct from (old.locked_at, old.locked_reason, old.locked_by)
     and coalesce(current_setting('app.login_admin', true), '') <> 'on'
     and current_setting('role', true) = 'authenticated' and not public.is_platform_admin() then
    raise exception 'Không được đổi trạng thái khoá' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;

 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if new.login is distinct from old.login
     and coalesce(current_setting('app.login_admin', true), '') <> 'on'
     and coalesce(nullif(current_setting('request.jwt.claims', true), '')::jsonb ->> 'role', '') in ('authenticated', 'anon') then
    raise exception 'Không sửa được tên đăng nhập' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_profile_org()
