 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if new.org_id is distinct from old.org_id and new.org_id is not null
     and not exists (select 1 from memberships where user_id = new.id and org_id = new.org_id) then
    raise exception 'Người dùng chưa thuộc tổ chức này. Hãy mời qua mục Tài khoản.' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_project_member()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_project_member()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if not exists (select 1 from students s join student_projects p on p.org_id = s.org_id where s.id = new.student_id and p.id = new.project_id) then
    raise exception 'Học sinh không thuộc trường của dự án' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_question()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_question()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v jsonb; n int := jsonb_array_length(new.options);
begin
  if new.kind = 'truefalse' then new.options := '["Đúng","Sai"]'::jsonb; n := 2; end if;
  for v in select * from jsonb_array_elements(new.answer) loop
    if jsonb_typeof(v) <> 'number' or (v #>> '{}')::numeric <> floor((v #>> '{}')::numeric) or (v #>> '{}')::int < 0 or (v #>> '{}')::int >= n then
      raise exception 'Đáp án không khớp với các lựa chọn' using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
  end loop;
  if new.kind in ('single','truefalse') and jsonb_array_length(new.answer) <> 1 then raise exception 'Câu một đáp án chỉ có một đáp án đúng' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then new.created_by := coalesce(new.created_by, auth.uid()); end if;
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' then new.created_by := old.created_by; new.org_id := old.org_id; end if;
  new.updated_at := now();
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_role_update()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_role_update()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if new.key <> old.key or new.org_id is distinct from old.org_id or new.is_system <> old.is_system then
    raise exception 'Không được đổi mã, trường hoặc loại của vai trò';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_service_plan()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_service_plan()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if exists (select 1 from unnest(new.modules) m where m not in (select key from service_modules)) then
    raise exception 'Gói có phân hệ không tồn tại' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  new.modules := array(select distinct m from unnest(new.modules) m order by 1);
  new.updated_at := now();
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_stem_index_config()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_stem_index_config()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare k text; v jsonb;
begin
  for k, v in select * from jsonb_each(new.indicators) loop
    if k !~ '^[a-z_]{2,30}$' or jsonb_typeof(v) <> 'object'
       or (v ? 'weight' and (jsonb_typeof(v->'weight') <> 'number' or (v->>'weight')::numeric not between 0 and 100))
       or (v ? 'target' and v->'target' <> 'null'::jsonb and (jsonb_typeof(v->'target') <> 'number' or (v->>'target')::numeric <= 0)) then
      raise exception 'Chỉ số "%" không hợp lệ', k using errcode = '23514';
    end if;
  end loop;
  new.updated_at := now(); new.updated_by := auth.uid();
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_stock_docs()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_stock_docs()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_engine boolean := coalesce(current_setting('app.approval_engine', true), 'off') = 'on'
                            or coalesce(current_setting('app.stock_engine', true), 'off') = 'on';
begin
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
    if new.status not in ('draft', 'pending') then raise exception 'Phiếu mới phải ở trạng thái nháp hoặc gửi duyệt' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    if tg_table_name = 'stock_transfers' then
      if not public.stock_target_ok(new.org_id, new.to_org) then raise exception 'Đơn vị nhận không hợp lệ' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
    end if;
    return new;
  end if;
  if v_engine then return coalesce(new, old); end if;
  if tg_op = 'DELETE' then
    if old.status not in ('draft', 'rejected', 'cancelled') then raise exception 'Chỉ xoá được phiếu nháp / bị từ chối / đã huỷ' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    return old;
  end if;
  if tg_table_name = 'stock_transfers' then
    if new.to_org is distinct from old.to_org and not public.stock_target_ok(new.org_id, new.to_org) then
      raise exception 'Đơn vị nhận không hợp lệ' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
  end if;
  if new.status is not distinct from old.status then
    if old.status not in ('draft', 'rejected') then raise exception 'Phiếu đã gửi duyệt, không sửa được' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    return new;
  end if;
  -- Đổi trạng thái do người dùng: gửi duyệt; huỷ phiếu chưa xuất. Duyệt/từ chối đi qua engine (approval_source_guard);
  -- nhận hàng / xuất hàng chỉ qua hàm.
  if (old.status in ('draft', 'rejected') and new.status = 'pending')
     or (old.status = 'pending' and new.status in ('approved', 'rejected'))
     or (tg_table_name = 'stock_transfers' and old.status in ('draft', 'rejected', 'approved') and new.status = 'cancelled') then
    return new;
  end if;
  raise exception 'Không chuyển được trạng thái % → %', old.status, new.status using errcode = '22023';
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_stock_lines()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_stock_lines()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_parent text; v_org uuid; r record;
begin
  if coalesce(current_setting('app.stock_engine', true), 'off') = 'on' then return coalesce(new, old); end if;
  if tg_table_name = 'supply_request_lines' then
    select status, org_id into v_parent, v_org from supply_requests where id = coalesce(new.request_id, old.request_id);
  else
    select status, org_id into v_parent, v_org from stock_transfers where id = coalesce(new.transfer_id, old.transfer_id);
  end if;
  if v_parent is null then return coalesce(new, old); end if;            -- phiếu cha đang bị xoá (cascade)
  if v_parent not in ('draft', 'rejected') then raise exception 'Phiếu đã gửi duyệt, không sửa dòng được' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if tg_op = 'DELETE' then return old; end if;
  if new.org_id <> v_org then raise exception 'Dòng không cùng đơn vị với phiếu' using errcode = '23514'; end if;
  if tg_table_name = 'supply_request_lines' then
    new.received_qty := null;
    if new.supply_id is not null and not exists (select 1 from supplies where id = new.supply_id and org_id = v_org) then
      raise exception 'Vật tư không thuộc đơn vị' using errcode = '23514';
    end if;
  else
    new.received_qty := null;
    if new.supply_id is not null then
      select name, unit into r from supplies where id = new.supply_id and org_id = v_org;
      if r is null then raise exception 'Vật tư không thuộc đơn vị xuất' using errcode = '23514'; end if;
      new.name := r.name; new.unit := r.unit;
    else
      select t.name, u.serial, u.status into r from equipment_units u join equipment_types t on t.id = u.type_id where u.id = new.unit_id and u.org_id = v_org;
      if r is null then raise exception 'Thiết bị không thuộc đơn vị xuất' using errcode = '23514'; end if;
      if r.status in ('lost', 'in_transit') then raise exception 'Thiết bị đang thất lạc / vận chuyển' using errcode = '23514'; end if;
      new.name := r.name || coalesce(' · ' || r.serial, ''); new.unit := 'cái'; new.qty := 1;
    end if;
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_stocktake()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_stocktake()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if current_user in ('authenticated', 'anon') then
    if new.status is distinct from old.status and not (old.status, new.status) in (('open', 'submitted'), ('open', 'cancelled'), ('submitted', 'open')) then
      raise exception 'Không chuyển được trạng thái đợt kiểm kê' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
    if old.status in ('approved', 'cancelled') then raise exception 'Đợt kiểm kê đã đóng' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
    new.approved_at := old.approved_at; new.approved_by := old.approved_by;
  end if;
  if new.status = 'submitted' and old.status <> 'submitted' then new.submitted_at := now(); end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_stocktake_lines()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_stocktake_lines()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if (select status from stocktakes where id = new.stocktake_id) <> 'open' then
    raise exception 'Đợt kiểm kê đã nộp — không sửa kết quả được' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if tg_table_name = 'stocktake_items' then new.checked_by := auth.uid(); new.checked_at := now(); end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_student_project()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_student_project()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_engine boolean := coalesce(current_setting('app.approval_engine', true), 'off') = 'on';
begin
  new.updated_at := now();
  if new.mentor_teacher_id is not null and not exists (select 1 from teachers where id = new.mentor_teacher_id and org_id = new.org_id) then
    raise exception 'Giáo viên hướng dẫn không thuộc trường' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(new.links) l where coalesce(l->>'url', '') !~* '^https?://') then
    raise exception 'Đường link phải bắt đầu bằng http:// hoặc https://' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
    if new.status not in ('draft', 'pending') then raise exception 'Dự án mới phải ở trạng thái nháp hoặc gửi duyệt' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    return new;
  end if;
  if v_engine or new.status is not distinct from old.status then return new; end if;
  if (old.status in ('draft', 'rejected') and new.status = 'pending')
     or (old.status = 'pending' and new.status in ('approved', 'rejected'))
     or (old.status = 'approved' and new.status = 'completed' and (public.is_admin() or old.mentor_teacher_id = public.my_teacher_id()))
     or (old.status = 'completed' and new.status = 'approved' and public.is_admin()) then
    return new;
  end if;
  raise exception 'Không chuyển được trạng thái dự án % → %', old.status, new.status using errcode = '22023';
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_submission()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_submission()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare a assignments;
begin
  if public.is_student() then
    if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
      new.status := 'submitted'; new.score := null; new.feedback := null; new.graded_at := null; new.graded_by := null;
      new.submitted_at := now(); new.rubric_scores := null;
    else
      new.assignment_id := old.assignment_id; new.student_id := old.student_id; new.group_id := old.group_id;
      new.status := 'submitted'; new.score := old.score; new.feedback := old.feedback;
      new.graded_at := old.graded_at; new.graded_by := old.graded_by; new.submitted_at := now(); new.rubric_scores := old.rubric_scores;
    end if;
  else
    if new.rubric_scores is not null and (tg_op = 'INSERT' or new.rubric_scores is distinct from old.rubric_scores) then
      select * into a from assignments where id = new.assignment_id;
      if a.rubric is null then raise exception 'Bài tập này không chấm theo tiêu chí' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
      new.score := coalesce(public.rubric_score(a.rubric, new.rubric_scores, a.max_score), new.score);
    end if;
    if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and new.score is distinct from old.score and new.score is not null then
      new.status := 'graded'; new.graded_at := now(); new.graded_by := coalesce(public.my_teacher_id(), new.graded_by);
    end if;
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_supply_qty()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_supply_qty()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if pg_trigger_depth() < 2 then
    if tg_op = 'INSERT' then new.qty := 0; else new.qty := old.qty; end if;
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_survey()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_survey()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$   -- definer: đếm câu trả lời của mọi người (RLS chỉ cho xem của mình)
begin
  if exists (select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(new.questions) q
              where coalesce(q->>'id', '') = '' or coalesce(btrim(q->>'text'), '') = ''
                 or coalesce(q->>'type', '') not in ('single', 'multi', 'scale', 'text')
                 or (q->>'type' in ('single', 'multi') and (jsonb_typeof(q->'options') <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(q->'options') < 2))) then
    raise exception 'Câu hỏi chưa hợp lệ (thiếu nội dung, loại, hoặc lựa chọn)' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if (select count(distinct q->>'id') from jsonb_array_elements(new.questions) q) <> jsonb_array_length(new.questions) then
    raise exception 'Mã câu hỏi bị trùng' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if new.status = 'open' and jsonb_array_length(new.questions) = 0 then raise exception 'Khảo sát chưa có câu hỏi' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and new.questions is distinct from old.questions and exists (select 1 from survey_responses where survey_id = old.id) then
    raise exception 'Đã có người trả lời — không sửa câu hỏi được (hãy tạo khảo sát mới)' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and new.anonymous is distinct from old.anonymous and exists (select 1 from survey_responses where survey_id = old.id) then
    raise exception 'Đã có người trả lời — không đổi chế độ ẩn danh được' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  new.updated_at := now();
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func guard_teacher_assessment()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_teacher_assessment()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if exists (select 1 from jsonb_each(new.levels) l
              where l.key not in (select key from competencies where audience = 'teacher')
                 or jsonb_typeof(l.value) <> 'number' or (l.value)::text::int not between 1 and 4) then
    raise exception 'Mức đánh giá không hợp lệ' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and old.status = 'submitted' and new.kind = 'self' and not public.is_admin() then
    raise exception 'Bản tự đánh giá đã nộp — nhờ quản trị trường mở lại nếu cần sửa' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if new.status = 'submitted' and (tg_op = 'INSERT' or old.status <> 'submitted') then new.submitted_at := now(); end if;
  if new.status = 'draft' then new.submitted_at := null; end if;
  new.updated_at := now();
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func handle_invitation_created()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.handle_invitation_created()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_user uuid;
begin
  select id into v_user from auth.users where lower(email) = lower(new.email) and email_confirmed_at is not null limit 1;
  if v_user is not null then perform public.apply_invitations(v_user); end if;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func handle_new_user()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.handle_new_user()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  insert into profiles (id, full_name, email)
    values (new.id, coalesce(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'full_name', split_part(new.email,'@',1)), new.email)
  on conflict (id) do nothing;
  perform public.apply_invitations(new.id);
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func handle_user_confirmed()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.handle_user_confirmed()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if new.email_confirmed_at is not null and old.email_confirmed_at is null then
    perform public.apply_invitations(new.id);
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func has_perm(p_module text, p_action text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.has_perm(p_module text, p_action text)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select public.org_has_module(public.my_org_id(), p_module) and (public.is_admin() or exists (
    select 1 from public.my_permissions() p
    where p.module = p_module and case p_action
      when 'view' then p.can_view when 'detail' then p.can_detail when 'create' then p.can_create
      when 'edit' then p.can_edit when 'delete' then p.can_delete else false end))
$function$
;
-- ##### func has_role(keys text[])
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.has_role(keys text[])
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select exists (select 1 from memberships m join roles r on r.id = m.role_id
                 where m.user_id = auth.uid() and m.org_id = public.my_org_id()
                   and r.key = any(keys) and (r.org_id is null or r.key like 'custom\_%'))
$function$
;
-- ##### func help_ticket(p_subject text, p_description text, p_category text, p_priority text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.help_ticket(p_subject text, p_description text, p_category text DEFAULT 'technical'::text, p_priority text DEFAULT 'normal'::text)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_org uuid := public.my_org_id(); t support_tickets; v_recent int;
begin
  if auth.uid() is null or v_org is null then raise exception 'Chưa đăng nhập' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if length(btrim(coalesce(p_subject, ''))) < 3 then raise exception 'Nhập tiêu đề yêu cầu' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if length(coalesce(p_description, '')) > 8000 then raise exception 'Nội dung quá dài' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if p_category not in ('technical','account','content','equipment','billing','other') then p_category := 'other'; end if;
  -- Học sinh / phụ huynh không đặt mức khẩn; chống gửi dồn dập
  if not public.is_staff() then p_priority := 'normal'; elsif p_priority not in ('low','normal','high','urgent') then p_priority := 'normal'; end if;
  select count(*) into v_recent from support_tickets where reported_by = auth.uid() and created_at > now() - interval '1 hour';
  if v_recent >= 5 then raise exception 'Bạn vừa gửi nhiều yêu cầu — vui lòng chờ KDI phản hồi' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  insert into support_tickets (org_id, category, priority, subject, description)
    values (v_org, p_category, p_priority, left(btrim(p_subject), 200), nullif(btrim(p_description), ''))
    returning * into t;
  return jsonb_build_object('id', t.id, 'code', t.code, 'due_at', t.due_at);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func holiday_resync()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.holiday_resync()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_today date := public.vn_today(); v_from date; v_to date; v_org uuid; s record;
begin
  -- Khoảng ngày bị ảnh hưởng: hợp của bản cũ và bản mới (sửa ngày nghỉ có thể dời khoảng)
  v_from := least(case when tg_op <> 'INSERT' then old.starts_on end, case when tg_op <> 'DELETE' then new.starts_on end);
  v_to   := greatest(case when tg_op <> 'INSERT' then old.ends_on end, case when tg_op <> 'DELETE' then new.ends_on end);
  v_org  := case when tg_op = 'DELETE' then old.org_id else new.org_id end;
  -- Chỉ trong 63 ngày tới (phạm vi tiết đã/sẽ được sinh); ngày nghỉ đã qua không đụng tiết cũ,
  -- ngày nghỉ xa hơn để lịch nền xử lý khi tới gần.
  v_from := greatest(v_from, v_today);
  v_to := least(v_to, v_today + 62);
  if v_to < v_from then return null; end if;
  for s in with recursive down as (
             select id, type, status from organizations where id = v_org
             union all select o.id, o.type, o.status from organizations o join down on o.parent_id = down.id)
           select id, code from down join organizations using (id)
            where down.type = 'school' and down.status = 'active' and public.org_has_module(id, 'timetable') loop
    begin
      perform public.sync_teaching_sessions(s.id, v_from, v_to);
    exception when others then                     -- lỗi của một trường không chặn việc lưu ngày nghỉ
      raise warning 'Ngày nghỉ: không đồng bộ được buổi dạy trường %: %', s.code, sqlerrm;
    end;
  end loop;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func import_school_locations(p_rows jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.import_school_locations(p_rows jsonb)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare r jsonb; v_id uuid; v_ll double precision[]; updated int := 0; blank int := 0; bad jsonb := '[]'::jsonb; v_msg text;
  v_platform boolean := public.is_platform_admin();
begin
  if not (v_platform or public.is_authority()) then raise exception 'Chỉ Sở/Phòng hoặc quản trị nền tảng' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if jsonb_typeof(p_rows) <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(p_rows) > 5000 then raise exception 'Tối đa 5000 dòng một lần' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  for r in select * from jsonb_array_elements(p_rows) loop
    begin
      v_ll := public.parse_latlng(r->>'lat', r->>'lng', r->>'coord');
      if v_ll is null then blank := blank + 1; continue; end if;
      select id into v_id from organizations where code = btrim(r->>'code') and type = 'school';
      if v_id is null then raise exception 'Không có trường mã %', coalesce(nullif(btrim(r->>'code'), ''), '(trống)'); end if;
      if not (v_platform or v_id in (select public.my_org_tree())) then raise exception 'Trường % không thuộc đơn vị của bạn', r->>'code'; end if;
      if not (v_ll[1] between 7 and 24 and v_ll[2] between 102 and 118) then
        raise exception 'Toạ độ % , % ngoài Việt Nam (vĩ độ 7–24, kinh độ 102–118 — có thể bị đảo cột)', v_ll[1], v_ll[2];
      end if;
      update organizations set lat = v_ll[1], lng = v_ll[2] where id = v_id and (lat is distinct from v_ll[1] or lng is distinct from v_ll[2]);
      updated := updated + 1;
    exception when others then
      get stacked diagnostics v_msg = message_text;
      bad := bad || jsonb_build_object('line', r->'line', 'code', r->>'code', 'reason', v_msg);
    end;
  end loop;
  return jsonb_build_object('updated', updated, 'blank', blank, 'skipped', bad);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func in_my_tree(p_user uuid)

AS $function$

$function$
;

$function$

-- ##### func is_holiday(p_org uuid, p_day date)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_holiday(p_org uuid, p_day date)
 RETURNS text
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with recursive up as (
    select id, parent_id, 0 as d from organizations where id = p_org
    union all select o.id, o.parent_id, up.d + 1 from organizations o join up on o.id = up.parent_id where up.d < 10
  ) select h.name from school_holidays h join up on up.id = h.org_id where p_day between h.starts_on and h.ends_on order by up.d limit 1
$function$
;

 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select public.is_admin() or (public.is_staff() and (select type from organizations where id = public.my_org_id()) <> 'school')
$function$
;

AS $function$

$function$
;
-- ##### func is_platform_admin()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_platform_admin()
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select exists (select 1 from memberships m
                   join roles r on r.id = m.role_id
                   join organizations o on o.id = m.org_id
                  where m.user_id = auth.uid() and m.org_id = public.my_org_id()
                    and r.key = 'platform_admin' and r.org_id is null and o.is_platform)
     and (public.jwt_aal() = 'aal2' or not coalesce((select enforce_mfa_db from security_settings where id = 1), false))
$function$
;

$function$

-- ##### func join_class_session(p_pin text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.join_class_session(p_pin text)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare s class_sessions; v_student uuid := public.my_student_id();
begin
  if v_student is null then raise exception 'Tài khoản chưa được liên kết với học sinh'; end if;
  select * into s from class_sessions where pin = trim(p_pin) and status = 'live';
  if s.id is null then raise exception 'Mã PIN không đúng hoặc tiết học đã kết thúc'; end if;
  if s.class_id is distinct from public.my_class_id() then raise exception 'Mã PIN này dành cho lớp khác'; end if;
  insert into class_session_attendance (org_id, session_id, student_id) values (s.org_id, s.id, v_student)
    on conflict do nothing;
  return s.id;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func judge_score(p_assignment uuid, p_scores jsonb, p_comment text, p_submit boolean)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.judge_score(p_assignment uuid, p_scores jsonb, p_comment text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_submit boolean DEFAULT true)
 RETURNS numeric
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare a judge_assignments; c competitions; e competition_entries; k jsonb; v numeric; tot numeric := 0;
begin
  select * into a from judge_assignments where id = p_assignment for update;
  if a.id is null or not exists (select 1 from competition_judges j where j.id = a.judge_id and j.profile_id = auth.uid()) then
    raise exception 'Không phải bài được phân công cho bạn' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select * into e from competition_entries where id = a.entry_id;
  select * into c from competitions where id = e.competition_id;
  if c.status <> 'judging' or a.round <> c.current_round or e.status <> 'submitted' then raise exception 'Vòng chấm đã đóng' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  for k in select * from jsonb_array_elements(c.criteria) loop
    v := (p_scores->>(k->>'key'))::numeric;
    if v is null then
      if p_submit then raise exception 'Chưa chấm tiêu chí: %', k->>'label' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
      continue;
    end if;
    if v < 0 or v > (k->>'max')::numeric then raise exception 'Điểm "%" phải từ 0 đến %', k->>'label', k->>'max' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    tot := tot + v;
  end loop;
  update judge_assignments set scores = coalesce(p_scores, '{}'), comment = nullif(btrim(p_comment), ''),
         total = case when p_submit then tot end, submitted_at = case when p_submit then now() end
   where id = a.id;
  return tot;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func jwt_aal()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.jwt_aal()
 RETURNS text
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE
AS $function$
  select coalesce(nullif(current_setting('request.jwt.claims', true), '')::json->>'aal', 'aal1')
$function$
;
-- ##### func legal_accept(p_ids uuid[])
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.legal_accept(p_ids uuid[])
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare n int;
begin
  if auth.uid() is null then raise exception 'Chưa đăng nhập' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  insert into legal_acceptances (user_id, document_id)
    select auth.uid(), d.id from legal_documents d where d.id = any(p_ids) and d.published_at is not null
  on conflict do nothing;
  get diagnostics n = row_count;
  return n;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func legal_pending()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.legal_pending()
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, kind text, version integer, title text, body text, published_at timestamp with time zone)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select d.id, d.kind, d.version, d.title, d.body, d.published_at
    from (select distinct on (kind) * from legal_documents where published_at is not null order by kind, version desc) d
   where auth.uid() is not null
     and not exists (select 1 from legal_acceptances a where a.user_id = auth.uid() and a.document_id = d.id)
   order by d.kind desc
$function$
;
-- ##### func lesson_attendance_count()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.lesson_attendance_count()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_id uuid := coalesce(new.session_id, old.session_id);
begin
  update teaching_sessions s set
    present_count = c.p, absent_count = c.a, late_count = c.l, excused_count = c.e
  from (select count(*) filter (where status = 'present') p, count(*) filter (where status = 'absent') a,
               count(*) filter (where status = 'late') l, count(*) filter (where status = 'excused') e
          from lesson_attendance where session_id = v_id) c
  where s.id = v_id;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func lesson_attendance_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.lesson_attendance_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_org uuid; v_class uuid;
begin
  select org_id, class_id into v_org, v_class from teaching_sessions where id = new.session_id;
  new.org_id := v_org;
  if not exists (select 1 from students where id = new.student_id and class_id = v_class) then
    raise exception 'Học sinh không thuộc lớp của tiết này' using errcode = '23514';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func library_adopt(p_item uuid, p_template boolean)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.library_adopt(p_item uuid, p_template boolean DEFAULT false)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare
  i library_items; v_org uuid := public.my_org_id(); v_teacher uuid := public.my_teacher_id();
  v_slides jsonb; v_plan uuid; v_first uuid; v_ids uuid[] := '{}'; v_lesson uuid; v_tpl boolean;
begin
  if v_org is null or (select type from organizations where id = v_org) <> 'school' then
    raise exception 'Chỉ trường học dùng được học liệu vào bài giảng' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if not public.library_visible(p_item) then raise exception 'Không tìm thấy học liệu' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  select * into i from library_items where id = p_item;
  if i.status <> 'published' and i.owner_org_id <> v_org then raise exception 'Học liệu chưa phát hành' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  v_tpl := coalesce(p_template, false) or v_teacher is null;
  if v_tpl and not public.is_admin() then raise exception 'Chỉ quản trị trường tạo bài giảng mẫu của trường' using errcode = '42501'; end if;

  if i.kind = 'program' then
    -- Dùng cả chương trình: lấy mọi bài giảng được liên kết (mà đơn vị này thấy)
    for v_lesson in
      select distinct (l.value #>> '{}')::uuid
        from jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(i.content->'units', '[]')) u, jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(u->'lesson_ids', '[]')) l
    loop
      if public.library_visible(v_lesson) then
        v_ids := v_ids || ((public.library_adopt(v_lesson, v_tpl))->>'lesson_plan_id')::uuid;
      end if;
    end loop;
    insert into library_copies (item_id, org_id, adopted_version, lesson_plan_id) values (i.id, v_org, i.version, v_ids[1]);
    return jsonb_build_object('kind', 'program', 'lesson_plan_ids', to_jsonb(v_ids), 'count', coalesce(array_length(v_ids, 1), 0));
  end if;

  if i.kind = 'lesson' then
    v_slides := coalesce(i.content->'slides', '[]'::jsonb);
  else
    v_slides := jsonb_build_array(
      jsonb_build_object('id', 's1', 'kind', 'title', 'title', i.title, 'body', coalesce(i.summary, '')),
      case when i.content->>'type' in ('video', 'image')
        then jsonb_build_object('id', 's2', 'kind', 'media', 'title', i.title, 'url', coalesce(i.content->>'url', ''))
        else jsonb_build_object('id', 's2', 'kind', 'content', 'title', 'Tài liệu tham khảo', 'body', coalesce(i.content->>'url', '')) end);
  end if;
  insert into lesson_plans (org_id, teacher_id, title, subject, grades, program, is_template, status, summary, slides, library_item_id, library_version)
    values (v_org, case when v_tpl then null else v_teacher end, case when v_tpl then 'Mẫu: ' || i.title else i.title end,
            i.subject, i.grades, i.program, v_tpl, 'draft', i.summary, v_slides, i.id, i.version)
    returning id into v_plan;
  insert into library_copies (item_id, org_id, adopted_version, lesson_plan_id) values (i.id, v_org, i.version, v_plan);
  return jsonb_build_object('kind', i.kind, 'lesson_plan_id', v_plan);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func library_can_manage()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.library_can_manage()
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select public.is_admin() or public.is_authority() or public.has_role(array['provider']) or public.is_platform_admin()
$function$
;
-- ##### func library_item_owner(p_item uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.library_item_owner(p_item uuid)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select owner_org_id from library_items where id = p_item
$function$
;
-- ##### func library_publish(p_item uuid, p_note text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.library_publish(p_item uuid, p_note text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare i library_items; u record; v int;
begin
  select * into i from library_items where id = p_item for update;
  if i.id is null or i.owner_org_id is distinct from public.my_org_id() or not public.library_can_manage() then
    raise exception 'Bạn không phát hành được mục này' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if i.kind = 'lesson' and jsonb_array_length(coalesce(i.content->'slides', '[]')) = 0 then raise exception 'Bài giảng chưa có slide nào'; end if;
  if i.kind = 'material' and coalesce(trim(i.content->>'url'), '') = '' then raise exception 'Học liệu chưa có đường dẫn'; end if;
  update library_items set status = 'published', version = version + 1, published_at = now(), change_note = nullif(trim(p_note), '')
   where id = i.id returning version into v;
  -- Báo người đã dùng bản cũ (một thông báo cho mỗi người, theo đơn vị)
  if v > 1 then
    for u in select distinct c.adopted_by, c.org_id from library_copies c where c.item_id = i.id and c.adopted_by is not null loop
      perform public.notify_user(u.adopted_by, u.org_id, 'library', 'Học liệu có bản cập nhật: ' || i.title,
        'Phiên bản ' || v || coalesce(' — ' || nullif(trim(p_note), ''), '') || '. Bản của bạn không bị thay đổi.',
        'Thư viện STEM', null, i.id, true);
    end loop;
  end if;
  return v;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func library_stats()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.library_stats()
 RETURNS TABLE(item_id uuid, schools integer, copies integer, outdated integer)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select c.item_id, count(distinct c.org_id)::int, count(*)::int,
         count(*) filter (where c.adopted_version < i.version)::int
    from library_copies c join library_items i on i.id = c.item_id
   where i.owner_org_id in (select public.my_org_tree()) and public.is_staff()
   group by c.item_id
$function$
;
-- ##### func library_unpublish(p_item uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.library_unpublish(p_item uuid)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  update library_items set status = 'archived'
   where id = p_item and owner_org_id = public.my_org_id() and public.library_can_manage();
  if not found then raise exception 'Bạn không thu hồi được mục này' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func library_visible(p_item uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.library_visible(p_item uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select exists (
    select 1 from library_items i
     where i.id = p_item and public.is_staff()
       and (i.owner_org_id in (select public.my_org_tree())
            or (i.status = 'published' and (
                  i.scope = 'public'
                  or (i.scope = 'children' and i.owner_org_id in (select public.my_ancestor_orgs()))
                  or (i.scope = 'selected' and exists (select 1 from library_targets t
                        where t.item_id = i.id and t.org_id in (select public.my_scope_orgs())))))))
$function$
;
-- ##### func log_data_export(p_org uuid, p_format text, p_tables text[], p_rows integer, p_note text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.log_data_export(p_org uuid, p_format text, p_tables text[], p_rows integer, p_note text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v uuid;
begin
  if not (public.is_platform_admin() or (public.can_access_org(p_org) and public.is_staff() and public.is_admin())
          or (public.my_org_id() = p_org and (select type from organizations where id = p_org) <> 'school' and public.is_staff())) then
    raise exception 'Không có quyền xuất dữ liệu đơn vị này' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  insert into data_exports (org_id, format, tables, row_count, note)
    values (p_org, p_format, coalesce(p_tables, '{}'), greatest(coalesce(p_rows, 0), 0), nullif(btrim(p_note), '')) returning id into v;
  return v;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func login_branding(p_host text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.login_branding(p_host text)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$

           'primary_color', b.primary_color, 'sidebar_color', b.sidebar_color, 'accent_color', b.accent_color,
           'login_message', b.login_message, 'support_text', b.support_text)
    from org_branding b where lower(btrim(coalesce(p_host, ''))) = any(b.domains) limit 1
$function$
;

-- ##### func mark_teaching_session(p_session uuid, p_marks jsonb, p_note text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.mark_teaching_session(p_session uuid, p_marks jsonb, p_note text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v teaching_sessions; v_admin boolean; v_start timestamptz; v_bad int; v_today date := public.vn_today();
begin
  select * into v from teaching_sessions where id = p_session;
  if not found or not public.can_access_org(v.org_id) then raise exception 'Không tìm thấy tiết dạy' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  v_admin := v.org_id = public.my_org_id() and public.has_perm('timetable', 'edit');
  if not v_admin and (v.teacher_id is null or v.teacher_id is distinct from public.my_teacher_id()) then
    raise exception 'Chỉ giáo viên dạy tiết này hoặc quản trị trường được điểm danh' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if v.status = 'cancelled' then raise exception 'Tiết đã huỷ, không điểm danh' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if not v_admin then
    select (v.session_date + p.starts_at) at time zone 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' into v_start from period_configs p where p.id = v.period_id;
    if now() < v_start - interval '10 minutes' then raise exception 'Chưa đến giờ tiết học' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    if v.session_date < v_today - 7 then
      raise exception 'Quá 7 ngày, nhờ quản trị trường sửa điểm danh' using errcode = '22023';
    end if;
  end if;

  select count(*) into v_bad from jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(p_marks, '[]'::jsonb)) m
   where (m->>'status') is null or (m->>'status') not in ('present', 'absent', 'late', 'excused')
      or not exists (select 1 from students s where s.id = (m->>'student_id')::uuid and s.class_id = v.class_id);
  if v_bad > 0 then raise exception 'Danh sách điểm danh có % dòng không hợp lệ', v_bad using errcode = '22023'; end if;

  insert into lesson_attendance (session_id, student_id, org_id, status, note, marked_by, marked_at)
  select p_session, (m->>'student_id')::uuid, v.org_id, m->>'status', nullif(m->>'note', ''), auth.uid(), now()
    from jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(p_marks, '[]'::jsonb)) m
  on conflict (session_id, student_id) do update set
    status = excluded.status, note = excluded.note, marked_by = excluded.marked_by, marked_at = excluded.marked_at;

  update teaching_sessions set status = 'taught', note = coalesce(p_note, note), marked_by = auth.uid(), marked_at = now()
   where id = p_session;
  select * into v from teaching_sessions where id = p_session;
  return jsonb_build_object('present', v.present_count, 'absent', v.absent_count, 'late', v.late_count, 'excused', v.excused_count);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func membership_added()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.membership_added()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  update profiles set org_id = new.org_id where id = new.user_id and org_id is null;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func membership_removed()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.membership_removed()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if not exists (select 1 from memberships where user_id = old.user_id and org_id = old.org_id) then
    update profiles set org_id = (select org_id from memberships where user_id = old.user_id order by created_at limit 1)
     where id = old.user_id and org_id = old.org_id;
  end if;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func mfa_required()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.mfa_required()
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select exists (select 1 from memberships m join roles r on r.id = m.role_id
                  where m.user_id = auth.uid() and r.key = any(coalesce((select mfa_roles from security_settings where id = 1), '{}'::text[])))
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_ancestor_orgs()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_ancestor_orgs()
 RETURNS SETOF uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with recursive up as (
    select parent_id as id from organizations where id = public.my_org_id()
    union all
    select o.parent_id from organizations o join up on o.id = up.id where o.parent_id is not null
  ) select id from up where id is not null
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_branding()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_branding()
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select case when auth.uid() is null then null else public.branding_of(public.my_org_id()) end
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_class_id()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_class_id()
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select class_id from students where profile_id = auth.uid() and org_id = public.my_org_id() order by id limit 1
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_entitlement()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_entitlement()
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select jsonb_build_object('state', e.state, 'plan', e.plan_name, 'contract', e.contract_code, 'ends_on', e.ends_on,
           'source_org', (select name from organizations where id = e.source_org),
           'gated', (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(distinct pm), '[]'::jsonb) from service_modules s, unnest(s.perm_modules) pm),
           'perm_modules', case when e.state = 'open' then null else
             (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(distinct pm), '[]'::jsonb) from service_modules s, unnest(s.perm_modules) pm where s.key = any(e.modules)) end)
    from public.org_entitlement(public.my_org_id()) e
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_event_roles()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_event_roles()
 RETURNS text[]
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select array_remove(array[
    case when public.my_student_id() is not null then 'student' end,
    case when public.my_teacher_id() is not null then 'teacher' end,
    case when public.is_parent() then 'parent' end,
    case when public.is_staff() then 'staff' end], null)
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_events(p_from timestamp with time zone)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_events(p_from timestamp with time zone DEFAULT (now() - '1 day'::interval))
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, title text, kind text, description text, starts_at timestamp with time zone, ends_at timestamp with time zone, location text, organizer text, audience text[], registration boolean, capacity integer, reg_deadline timestamp with time zone, registered integer, my_regs jsonb)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select e.id, e.title, e.kind, e.description, e.starts_at, e.ends_at, coalesce(e.location, r.name), o.name, e.audience, e.registration, e.capacity, e.reg_deadline,
         (select count(*)::int from event_registrations x where x.event_id = e.id and x.status in ('registered', 'attended')),
         (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('student_id', x.student_id, 'status', x.status, 'name', x.full_name)), '[]'::jsonb)
            from event_registrations x where x.event_id = e.id and x.profile_id = auth.uid())
    from events e join organizations o on o.id = e.org_id left join stem_rooms r on r.id = e.room_id
   where public.event_for_me(e.id) and coalesce(e.ends_at, e.starts_at) >= p_from
   order by e.starts_at
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_group_ids()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_group_ids()
 RETURNS SETOF uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select group_id from student_group_members where student_id = public.my_student_id()
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_judge_ids()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_judge_ids()
 RETURNS SETOF uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select j.id from competition_judges j where j.profile_id = auth.uid()
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_judging()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_judging()
 RETURNS TABLE(assignment_id uuid, competition_id uuid, competition text, round_name text, anonymous boolean, criteria jsonb, entry_code text, field text, title text, school text, members text, abstract text, docs jsonb, doc_labels jsonb, links jsonb, scores jsonb, total numeric, comment text, submitted_at timestamp with time zone, open boolean)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select a.id, c.id, c.title, c.rounds[a.round], c.anonymous, c.criteria, e.code, e.field, p.title,
         case when c.anonymous then null else o.name end,
         case when c.anonymous then null else (select string_agg(s.full_name, ', ' order by s.full_name) from project_members pm join students s on s.id = pm.student_id where pm.project_id = p.id) end,
         coalesce(e.abstract, p.abstract), e.docs, c.required_docs, case when c.anonymous then '[]'::jsonb else p.links end, a.scores, a.total, a.comment, a.submitted_at,
         c.status = 'judging' and a.round = c.current_round and e.status = 'submitted'
    from judge_assignments a join competition_judges j on j.id = a.judge_id join competition_entries e on e.id = a.entry_id
    join competitions c on c.id = e.competition_id join student_projects p on p.id = e.project_id join organizations o on o.id = e.school_id
   where j.profile_id = auth.uid()
   order by c.created_at desc, e.field, e.code
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_org_id()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_org_id()
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select p.org_id from profiles p
    join organizations o on o.id = p.org_id
   where p.id = auth.uid()
     and p.locked_at is null
     and (o.status = 'active' or o.is_platform)
     and exists (select 1 from memberships m where m.user_id = p.id and m.org_id = p.org_id)
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_org_tree()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_org_tree()
 RETURNS SETOF uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with recursive pa as (select public.is_platform_admin() as yes),
  tree as (
    select id from organizations where id = public.my_org_id() and not (select yes from pa)
    union all
    select o.id from organizations o join tree t on o.parent_id = t.id
  )
  select id from tree
  union all
  select id from organizations where (select yes from pa)
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_orgs()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_orgs()
 RETURNS TABLE(org_id uuid, name text, type text, parent_name text, roles text[], active boolean)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select o.id, o.name, o.type, par.name,
         array_agg(r.name order by r.name), o.id = (select org_id from profiles where id = auth.uid())
    from memberships m
    join organizations o on o.id = m.org_id
    join roles r on r.id = m.role_id
    left join organizations par on par.id = o.parent_id
   where m.user_id = auth.uid()
   group by o.id, o.name, o.type, par.name
   order by o.name
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_parent_org()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_parent_org()
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select parent_id from organizations where id = public.my_org_id()
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_pending_counts()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_pending_counts()
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select jsonb_build_object(
    'stem_registrations', case when public.has_perm('stem_registrations','edit')
      then (select count(*) from stem_registrations where org_id = public.my_org_id() and status = 'pending') else 0 end,
    'borrow_requests', case when public.has_perm('stem_rooms','edit')
      then (select count(*) from borrow_requests where org_id = public.my_org_id() and status = 'pending') else 0 end,
    'warranty_tickets', case when public.has_perm('warranty','edit')
      then (select count(*) from warranty_tickets where org_id = public.my_org_id() and status in ('new','accepted','waiting_parts','processing')) else 0 end,
    'to_grade', coalesce((select count(*) from submissions s join assignments a on a.id = s.assignment_id
      where a.teacher_id = public.my_teacher_id() and s.status <> 'graded'), 0),
    'approvals', (select count(*) from public.approval_inbox()),
    'unread', (select count(*) from notifications where user_id = auth.uid() and org_id = public.my_org_id() and read_at is null)
  )
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_permissions()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_permissions()
 RETURNS TABLE(module text, can_view boolean, can_detail boolean, can_create boolean, can_edit boolean, can_delete boolean)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with mine as (
    select m.role_id from memberships m where m.user_id = auth.uid() and m.org_id = public.my_org_id()
  ), mods as (
    select d.role_id, d.module from role_permissions d join mine using (role_id)
    union
    select o.role_id, o.module from org_role_permissions o join mine using (role_id) where o.org_id = public.my_org_id()
  )
  select m.module,
    bool_or(coalesce(o.can_view,   d.can_view,   false)),
    bool_or(coalesce(o.can_detail, d.can_detail, false)),
    bool_or(coalesce(o.can_create, d.can_create, false)),
    bool_or(coalesce(o.can_edit,   d.can_edit,   false)),
    bool_or(coalesce(o.can_delete, d.can_delete, false))
  from mods m
  left join org_role_permissions o on o.role_id = m.role_id and o.module = m.module and o.org_id = public.my_org_id()
  left join role_permissions d on d.role_id = m.role_id and d.module = m.module
  group by m.module
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_portals()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_portals()
 RETURNS text[]
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select coalesce(array_agg(distinct r.portal) filter (where r.portal is not null), '{}')
    from memberships m join roles r on r.id = m.role_id
   where m.user_id = auth.uid() and m.org_id = public.my_org_id()
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_roles()

 RETURNS TABLE(role_id uuid, key text, name text, portal text)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select r.id, r.key, r.name, r.portal from memberships m join roles r on r.id = m.role_id
   where m.user_id = auth.uid() and m.org_id = public.my_org_id()
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_scope_orgs()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_scope_orgs()
 RETURNS SETOF uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select public.my_org_id() where public.my_org_id() is not null
  union all
  select public.my_ancestor_orgs()
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_student_id()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_student_id()
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select id from students where profile_id = auth.uid() and org_id = public.my_org_id() order by id limit 1
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_surveys()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_surveys()
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, title text, description text, audience text, questions jsonb, anonymous boolean, closes_at timestamp with time zone, from_org text, answered boolean, my_answers jsonb)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select s.id, s.title, s.description, s.audience, s.questions, s.anonymous, s.closes_at, o.name,
         r.id is not null, r.answers
    from surveys s join organizations o on o.id = s.org_id
    left join survey_responses r on r.survey_id = s.id and r.respondent = auth.uid()
   where public.survey_for_me(s.id)
   order by r.id is not null, s.closes_at nulls last, s.created_at desc
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_teacher_id()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_teacher_id()
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select id from teachers where profile_id = auth.uid() and org_id = public.my_org_id() order by id limit 1
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_training_lessons()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_training_lessons()
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, title text, grades text, subject text, author text, created_on date, blocks integer, materials integer, videos integer, docs integer, plan_id uuid, plan_name text, program text)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with my_plans as (
    select plan_id from training_enrollments where teacher_id = public.my_teacher_id()
    union
    select s.plan_id from training_attendance a join training_sessions s on s.id = a.session_id
      where a.teacher_id = public.my_teacher_id()
  )
  select l.id, l.title, l.grades, l.subject, l.author, l.created_on::date,
         l.blocks, l.materials, l.videos, l.docs, l.plan_id, p.name, p.program
  from training_lessons l
  join training_plans p on p.id = l.plan_id
  where l.plan_id in (select plan_id from my_plans) and l.status = 'published'
  order by l.created_on desc
$function$
;
-- ##### func my_training_sessions()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_training_sessions()
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, plan_id uuid, plan_name text, program text, provider text, title text, starts_at timestamp with time zone, ends_at timestamp with time zone, mode text, location text, meeting_url text, present boolean, org_name text)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select s.id, s.plan_id, p.name, p.program, p.provider, s.title, s.starts_at, s.ends_at, s.mode, s.location, s.meeting_url,
         a.present, o.name
  from training_attendance a
  join training_sessions s on s.id = a.session_id
  left join training_plans p on p.id = s.plan_id
  left join organizations o on o.id = s.org_id
  where a.teacher_id = public.my_teacher_id()
  order by s.starts_at
$function$
;
-- ##### func notify_assignment()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.notify_assignment()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare p uuid;
begin
  if new.status = 'open' and (tg_op = 'INSERT' or old.status is distinct from 'open') then
    for p in select profile_id from students where class_id = new.class_id and profile_id is not null loop
      perform public.notify_user(p, new.org_id, 'assignment', 'Bài tập mới: ' || new.title,
        case when new.due_at is not null then 'Hạn nộp ' || to_char(new.due_at at time zone 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh', 'HH24:MI DD/MM') else 'Không giới hạn thời gian' end,
        'Bài tập', 'student', new.id);
    end loop;
  end if;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func notify_borrow()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.notify_borrow()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and new.status is distinct from old.status then
    perform public.notify_user(public.teacher_profile(new.teacher_id), new.org_id, 'status',
      'Phiếu mượn: ' || case new.status when 'approved' then 'đã được duyệt' when 'lent' then 'đã giao thiết bị'
        when 'returned' then 'đã trả' when 'rejected' then 'bị từ chối' else new.status end,
      new.title || ' · ' || public.vn_date(new.on_date), 'Đặt phòng STEM', 'teacher', new.id);
  end if;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func notify_elearning()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.notify_elearning()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v uuid; v_page text := 'E-learning & luyện thi';
begin
  if new.status = 'published' and (tg_op = 'INSERT' or old.status is distinct from 'published')
     and (select type from organizations where id = new.org_id) = 'school' then
    for v in select s.profile_id from students s where s.org_id = new.org_id and s.profile_id is not null and s.status = 'studying'
               and (new.class_ids is null or cardinality(new.class_ids) = 0 or s.class_id = any(new.class_ids)) loop
      perform public.notify_user(v, new.org_id, 'elearning',
        case tg_table_name when 'practice_exams' then 'Đề luyện mới: ' else 'Khoá học mới: ' end || new.title, coalesce(new.subject, ''), v_page, 'student', new.id);
    end loop;
  end if;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func notify_event_published()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.notify_event_published()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare u record; v_title text;
begin
  if new.status <> 'published' or (tg_op = 'UPDATE' and old.status = 'published') then return null; end if;
  if (select type from organizations where id = new.org_id) <> 'school' then return null; end if;   -- sự kiện cấp Sở: xem trong danh sách
  v_title := 'Sự kiện mới: ' || new.title;
  for u in
    select distinct m.user_id, r.portal from memberships m join roles r on r.id = m.role_id
     where m.org_id = new.org_id and r.portal in ('student', 'teacher', 'school_admin', 'parent')
       and ((r.portal = 'student' and 'student' = any(new.audience)) or (r.portal = 'teacher' and ('teacher' = any(new.audience) or 'staff' = any(new.audience)))
            or (r.portal = 'school_admin' and 'staff' = any(new.audience)) or (r.portal = 'parent' and 'parent' = any(new.audience)))
  loop
    perform public.notify_user(u.user_id, new.org_id, 'status', v_title,
      to_char(new.starts_at at time zone 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh', 'HH24:MI DD/MM') || coalesce(' · ' || new.location, ''), 'Sự kiện', u.portal, new.id);
  end loop;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func notify_guardians_absence()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.notify_guardians_absence()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare g record; v_day date; v_subj text; v_name text;
begin
  if new.status not in ('absent', 'late') or (tg_op = 'UPDATE' and old.status = new.status) then return null; end if;
  select ts.session_date, ts.subject into v_day, v_subj from teaching_sessions ts where ts.id = new.session_id;
  if v_day < current_date - 1 then return null; end if;                 -- sửa điểm danh cũ: không báo lại
  select full_name into v_name from students where id = new.student_id;
  for g in select distinct profile_id from guardians where student_id = new.student_id and profile_id is not null and receive_alerts loop
    perform public.notify_user(g.profile_id, new.org_id, 'status',
      v_name || case new.status when 'absent' then ' vắng mặt' else ' đi muộn' end || ' ngày ' || to_char(v_day, 'DD/MM'),
      coalesce(v_subj, '') || coalesce(' — ' || nullif(new.note, ''), ''), 'Chuyên cần của con', 'parent', new.student_id, true);
  end loop;
  return null;
end $function$
;

end $function$
;

-- ##### func notify_staff(p_org uuid, p_module text, p_kind text, p_title text, p_body text, p_page text, p_ref uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.notify_staff(p_org uuid, p_module text, p_kind text, p_title text, p_body text, p_page text, p_ref uuid)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare u uuid;
begin
  for u in select distinct m.user_id from memberships m
            where m.org_id = p_org and public.user_has_perm(m.user_id, p_module, 'edit', p_org) loop
    perform public.notify_user(u, p_org, p_kind, p_title, p_body, p_page, 'school_admin', p_ref);
  end loop;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func notify_stem_registration()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.notify_stem_registration()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and new.status is distinct from old.status and new.status in ('approved','rejected') then
    perform public.notify_user(public.teacher_profile(new.teacher_id), new.org_id, 'status',
      case new.status when 'approved' then 'Đăng ký dạy STEM đã được duyệt' else 'Đăng ký dạy STEM bị từ chối' end,
      new.topic || ' · ' || public.vn_date(new.on_date), 'Đặt phòng STEM', 'teacher', new.id);
  end if;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func notify_submission()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.notify_submission()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare a assignments; v_who text; p uuid;
begin
  select * into a from assignments where id = new.assignment_id;
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
    perform public.notify_user(public.teacher_profile(a.teacher_id), new.org_id, 'submission', 'Bài nộp mới: ' || a.title,
      (select count(*) from submissions where assignment_id = a.id)::text || ' bài đã nộp',
      'Chấm bài', 'teacher', a.id, true);
  elsif new.status = 'graded' and old.status is distinct from 'graded' then
    for p in
      select s.profile_id from students s where s.id = new.student_id and s.profile_id is not null
      union
      select s.profile_id from student_group_members m join students s on s.id = m.student_id
       where m.group_id = new.group_id and s.profile_id is not null
    loop
      perform public.notify_user(p, new.org_id, 'graded', 'Bài đã được chấm: ' || a.title,
        'Điểm ' || new.score || '/' || a.max_score || coalesce(' · ' || left(new.feedback, 100), ''), 'Bài tập', 'student', new.id);
    end loop;
  end if;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func notify_user(p_user uuid, p_org uuid, p_kind text, p_title text, p_body text, p_page text, p_portal text, p_ref uuid, p_merge boolean)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.notify_user(p_user uuid, p_org uuid, p_kind text, p_title text, p_body text, p_page text, p_portal text, p_ref uuid, p_merge boolean DEFAULT false)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if p_user is null or p_user = auth.uid() then return; end if;   -- không tự báo cho người thực hiện
  if p_merge and p_ref is not null then
    update notifications set count = count + 1, body = p_body, created_at = now()
     where user_id = p_user and kind = p_kind and ref_id = p_ref and read_at is null;
    if found then return; end if;
  end if;
  insert into notifications (org_id, user_id, kind, title, body, page, portal, ref_id)
    values (p_org, p_user, p_kind, p_title, p_body, p_page, p_portal, p_ref);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func notify_warranty()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.notify_warranty()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_title text; v_body text;
begin
  v_body := coalesce(new.code || ' · ', '') || coalesce(left(new.description, 120), '');
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' and new.status in ('new') then
    perform public.notify_staff(new.org_id, 'warranty', 'approval', 'Báo hỏng thiết bị mới', v_body, 'Bảo hành thiết bị', new.id);
  elsif tg_op = 'UPDATE' and (new.status is distinct from old.status or new.provider_status is distinct from old.provider_status) then
    v_title := 'Ticket bảo hành: ' || public.warranty_label(new.status)
      || case when new.provider_status is distinct from old.provider_status then ' · NCC: ' || public.warranty_label(new.provider_status) else '' end;
    perform public.notify_user(new.reported_by, new.org_id, 'status', v_title, v_body,
      case when public.user_has_perm(new.reported_by, 'warranty', 'view') then 'Bảo hành thiết bị' else 'Tổng quan' end,
      case when public.user_has_perm(new.reported_by, 'warranty', 'view') then 'school_admin' else 'teacher' end, new.id);
    perform public.notify_staff(new.org_id, 'warranty', 'status', v_title, v_body, 'Bảo hành thiết bị', new.id);
  end if;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func org_ancestor_of_type(p_org uuid, p_type text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.org_ancestor_of_type(p_org uuid, p_type text)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with recursive up as (
    select id, parent_id, type, 0 as d from organizations where id = p_org
    union all
    select o.id, o.parent_id, o.type, up.d + 1 from organizations o join up on o.id = up.parent_id where up.d < 10
  ) select id from up where type = p_type order by d limit 1
$function$
;
-- ##### func org_entitlement(p_org uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.org_entitlement(p_org uuid)
 RETURNS TABLE(state text, source_org uuid, contract_id uuid, contract_code text, plan_name text, modules text[], ends_on date)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_depth int;
begin
  with recursive up as (
    select o.id, o.parent_id, 0 as d from organizations o where o.id = p_org
    union all
    select o.id, o.parent_id, u.d + 1 from organizations o join up u on o.id = u.parent_id where u.d < 10
  )
  select min(u.d) into v_depth from up u join contracts c on c.org_id = u.id
   where c.status <> 'draft' and (u.d = 0 or c.include_children);
  if v_depth is null then
    return query select 'open'::text, null::uuid, null::uuid, null::text, null::text, null::text[], null::date;
    return;
  end if;
  return query
  with recursive up as (
    select o.id, o.parent_id, 0 as d from organizations o where o.id = p_org
    union all
    select o.id, o.parent_id, u.d + 1 from organizations o join up u on o.id = u.parent_id where u.d < 10
  ), lvl as (
    select c.*, (c.status = 'active' and current_date >= c.starts_on and (c.ends_on is null or current_date <= c.ends_on)) as live
      from up u join contracts c on c.org_id = u.id
     where u.d = v_depth and c.status <> 'draft' and (u.d = 0 or c.include_children)
  ), pick as (
    select * from lvl order by live desc, starts_on desc limit 1
  )
  select case when k.live then 'active' else 'expired' end, k.org_id, k.id, k.code, p.name,
         case when k.live then p.modules else '{}'::text[] end, k.ends_on
    from pick k join service_plans p on p.id = k.plan_id;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func org_has_module(p_org uuid, p_perm text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.org_has_module(p_org uuid, p_perm text)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare e record;
begin
  if not exists (select 1 from service_modules where p_perm = any(perm_modules)) then return true; end if;  -- phần lõi
  if p_org is null then return false; end if;
  select * into e from public.org_entitlement(p_org) limit 1;
  if e.state = 'open' then return true; end if;
  return exists (select 1 from service_modules s where s.key = any(e.modules) and p_perm = any(s.perm_modules));
end $function$
;
-- ##### func org_in_tree(p_org uuid, p_root uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.org_in_tree(p_org uuid, p_root uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with recursive up as (
    select id, parent_id from organizations where id = p_org
    union all
    select o.id, o.parent_id from organizations o join up on o.id = up.parent_id
  ) select exists (select 1 from up where id = p_root)
$function$
;
-- ##### func org_parent_ok(p_type text, p_parent_type text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.org_parent_ok(p_type text, p_parent_type text)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 IMMUTABLE
AS $function$
  select case p_type
    when 'ministry'   then p_parent_type is null
    when 'provider'   then p_parent_type is null
    when 'province'   then p_parent_type is null or p_parent_type = 'ministry'
    when 'department' then p_parent_type is null or p_parent_type = 'province'
    when 'district'   then p_parent_type = 'department'
    when 'school'     then p_parent_type in ('department', 'district')
    else false end
$function$
;
-- ##### func org_tree_stats()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.org_tree_stats()
 RETURNS TABLE(org_id uuid, members integer, schools integer, schools_active integer, teachers integer, students integer)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with recursive vis as (select t as id from public.my_org_tree() t where public.is_staff()),
  clos as (
    select id as anc, id as des from organizations where id in (select id from vis)
    union all
    select c.anc, o.id from clos c join organizations o on o.parent_id = c.des
  ),
  sch as (
    select o.id, o.activated_at is not null and o.status = 'active' as act,
      (select count(*) from teachers t where t.org_id = o.id and coalesce(t.status, 'active') = 'active') as tc,
      (select count(*) from students s where s.org_id = o.id and coalesce(s.status, 'studying') = 'studying') as sc
    from organizations o where o.type = 'school' and o.id in (select id from vis)
  ),
  mem as (select m.org_id, count(distinct m.user_id) as n from memberships m group by m.org_id)
  select v.id,
         coalesce((select n from mem where mem.org_id = v.id), 0)::int,
         count(s.id)::int, (count(s.id) filter (where s.act))::int,
         coalesce(sum(s.tc), 0)::int, coalesce(sum(s.sc), 0)::int
    from vis v left join clos c on c.anc = v.id left join sch s on s.id = c.des
   group by v.id
$function$
;

AS $function$ select name from organizations where id = $1.owner_org_id $function$

-- ##### func package_catalog()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.package_catalog()
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, code text, name text, tier text, programs text, grades text, topics integer, devices integer, sessions integer, list_price numeric, description text)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select p.id, p.code, p.name, p.tier, p.programs, p.grades, p.topics, p.devices, p.sessions, p.list_price, p.description
    from stem_packages p
   where p.org_id = public.platform_org_id() and p.catalog_active and public.is_staff()
   order by p.name
$function$
;
-- ##### func parent_child(p_student uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.parent_child(p_student uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare s students; v jsonb;
begin
  if not public.is_guardian_of(p_student) then raise exception 'Không phải phụ huynh của học sinh này' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  select * into s from students where id = p_student;
  select jsonb_build_object(
    'student', jsonb_build_object('id', s.id, 'full_name', s.full_name, 'code', s.student_code,
                                  'class', (select name from classes where id = s.class_id), 'school', (select name from organizations where id = s.org_id)),
    'homeroom', (select jsonb_build_object('name', t.full_name, 'email', t.email) from classes c join teachers t on t.id = c.homeroom_teacher_id where c.id = s.class_id),
    'schedule', (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('date', ts.session_date, 'period', pc.name, 'start', pc.starts_at, 'subject', ts.subject, 'kind', ts.kind,
                   'room', r.name, 'teacher', t.full_name, 'status', ts.status) order by ts.session_date, pc.starts_at), '[]'::jsonb)
                   from teaching_sessions ts join period_configs pc on pc.id = ts.period_id left join stem_rooms r on r.id = ts.room_id
                   left join teachers t on t.id = ts.teacher_id
                  where ts.class_id = s.class_id and ts.session_date between current_date and current_date + 6),
    'attendance', (select jsonb_build_object(
                     'present', count(*) filter (where la.status = 'present'), 'absent', count(*) filter (where la.status = 'absent'),
                     'late', count(*) filter (where la.status = 'late'), 'excused', count(*) filter (where la.status = 'excused'),
                     'recent', coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('date', ts.session_date, 'subject', ts.subject, 'status', la.status, 'note', la.note)
                                        order by ts.session_date desc) filter (where la.status <> 'present'), '[]'::jsonb))
                     from lesson_attendance la join teaching_sessions ts on ts.id = la.session_id
                    where la.student_id = s.id and ts.session_date >= current_date - 30),
    'assignments', (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('title', a.title, 'subject', a.subject, 'kind', a.kind, 'due_at', a.due_at, 'max_score', a.max_score,
                      'status', coalesce(sb.status, case when a.due_at < now() then 'missing' else 'todo' end), 'score', sb.score, 'feedback', sb.feedback,
                      'submitted_at', sb.submitted_at) order by coalesce(a.due_at, a.opens_at) desc), '[]'::jsonb)
                      from assignments a
                      left join lateral (select x.* from submissions x where x.assignment_id = a.id and (x.student_id = s.id
                                 or x.group_id in (select gm.group_id from student_group_members gm where gm.student_id = s.id)) limit 1) sb on true
                     where a.class_id = s.class_id and a.status <> 'draft' and a.opens_at >= now() - interval '60 days'),
    'consent', (select jsonb_build_object('guardian', c.guardian_name, 'method', c.method, 'on', c.consented_on, 'scope', c.scope)
                  from student_consents c where c.student_id = s.id and c.withdrawn_on is null limit 1)
  ) into v;
  return v;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func parent_children()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.parent_children()
 RETURNS TABLE(student_id uuid, full_name text, student_code text, class_id uuid, class_name text, school text, relation text, receive_alerts boolean)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select s.id, s.full_name, s.student_code, s.class_id, c.name, o.name, g.relation, g.receive_alerts
    from guardians g join students s on s.id = g.student_id join organizations o on o.id = s.org_id
    left join classes c on c.id = s.class_id
   where g.profile_id = auth.uid() and coalesce(s.status, 'studying') <> 'erased'
   order by s.full_name
$function$
;
-- ##### func parent_consent(p_student uuid, p_agree boolean, p_scope text[])
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.parent_consent(p_student uuid, p_agree boolean, p_scope text[] DEFAULT '{learning}'::text[])
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare g guardians;
begin
  select * into g from guardians where student_id = p_student and profile_id = auth.uid() limit 1;
  if g.id is null then raise exception 'Không phải phụ huynh của học sinh này' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if p_agree then
    if exists (select 1 from unnest(coalesce(p_scope, '{}')) x where x not in ('learning', 'photo')) then raise exception 'Phạm vi không hợp lệ' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    update student_consents set withdrawn_on = current_date, updated_at = now() where student_id = p_student and withdrawn_on is null;
    insert into student_consents (org_id, student_id, guardian_name, relation, phone, method, scope, note, recorded_by)
      values (g.org_id, p_student, g.full_name, case g.relation when 'father' then 'Cha' when 'mother' then 'Mẹ' else 'Người giám hộ' end,
              g.phone, 'online', case when 'learning' = any(p_scope) then p_scope else array['learning'] || p_scope end, 'Phụ huynh xác nhận trên hệ thống', auth.uid());
  else
    update student_consents set withdrawn_on = current_date, updated_at = now(), note = coalesce(note || ' · ', '') || 'Phụ huynh rút đồng ý trên hệ thống'
     where student_id = p_student and withdrawn_on is null;
  end if;
end $function$
;

 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE sql
