  if not public.is_platform_admin() then raise exception 'Chỉ quản trị nền tảng' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func revenue_overview(p_year integer)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.revenue_overview(p_year integer DEFAULT NULL::integer)
 RETURNS TABLE(contract_id uuid, code text, org_id uuid, org_name text, plan_name text, status text, starts_on date, ends_on date, value numeric, planned numeric, accepted numeric, invoiced numeric, paid numeric, outstanding numeric, milestones integer, overdue integer, next_due date)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if not public.is_platform_admin() then raise exception 'Chỉ quản trị nền tảng' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  return query
  select c.id, c.code, c.org_id, o.name, p.name, c.status, c.starts_on, c.ends_on, coalesce(c.value, 0),
         coalesce(sum(m.amount) filter (where m.status <> 'cancelled'), 0),
         coalesce(sum(m.amount) filter (where m.status in ('accepted', 'invoiced', 'paid')), 0),
         coalesce(sum(m.amount) filter (where m.status in ('invoiced', 'paid')), 0),
         coalesce(sum(m.paid_amount) filter (where m.status = 'paid'), 0),
         greatest(coalesce(c.value, 0) - coalesce(sum(m.paid_amount) filter (where m.status = 'paid'), 0), 0),
         count(m.id) filter (where m.status <> 'cancelled')::int,
         count(m.id) filter (where m.status not in ('paid', 'cancelled') and m.due_on < current_date)::int,
         min(m.due_on) filter (where m.status not in ('paid', 'cancelled') and m.due_on >= current_date)
    from contracts c join organizations o on o.id = c.org_id join service_plans p on p.id = c.plan_id
    left join contract_milestones m on m.contract_id = c.id
   where p_year is null or (c.starts_on <= make_date(p_year, 12, 31) and (c.ends_on is null or c.ends_on >= make_date(p_year, 1, 1)))
   group by c.id, o.name, p.name
   order by c.starts_on desc, c.code;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func role_fits_org(p_role uuid, p_org uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.role_fits_org(p_role uuid, p_org uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select exists (
    select 1 from roles r, organizations o
     where r.id = p_role and o.id = p_org
       and case
             when r.org_id is not null then r.org_id = o.id
             when r.key = 'platform_admin' then o.is_platform
             when r.key = 'judge' then true
             when o.type = 'school' then r.key in ('principal','it_admin','teacher','office_staff','student','parent')
             else r.key = o.type
           end)
$function$
;
-- ##### func room_overview(p_from date, p_to date)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.room_overview(p_from date, p_to date)
 RETURNS TABLE(room_id uuid, org_id uuid, school text, parent_name text, room text, code text, status text, capacity integer, tier text, slots integer, booked integer, from_sessions integer, from_registrations integer, from_borrows integer, conflicts integer, tkb_periods integer)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_range tstzrange;
begin
  if not public.is_staff() then raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if p_from is null or p_to is null or p_to < p_from or p_to - p_from > 400 then
    raise exception 'Khoảng ngày không hợp lệ' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  v_range := tstzrange(p_from::timestamp at time zone 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh', (p_to + 1)::timestamp at time zone 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh', '[)');
  return query
  with rm as (
    select r.*, o.name as school, par.name as parent_name from stem_rooms r
      join organizations o on o.id = r.org_id left join organizations par on par.id = o.parent_id
     where r.org_id in (select public.my_org_tree())
  ), days as (
    select count(*)::int as n from generate_series(p_from, p_to, interval '1 day') g where extract(isodow from g) between 1 and 6
  ), bk as (
    select b.room_id, count(*) as n, count(*) filter (where b.source = 'teaching_session') as s,
           count(*) filter (where b.source = 'stem_registration') as r, count(*) filter (where b.source = 'borrow_request') as w
      from resource_bookings b where b.room_id in (select id from rm) and b.during && v_range group by b.room_id
  ), cf as (
    select c.room_id, count(*) as n from booking_conflicts c where c.room_id in (select id from rm) and c.during && v_range group by c.room_id
  )
  select rm.id, rm.org_id, rm.school, rm.parent_name, rm.name, rm.code, rm.status, rm.capacity, rm.tier,
    ((select n from days) * (select count(*) from period_configs p where p.org_id = rm.org_id))::int,
    coalesce(bk.n, 0)::int, coalesce(bk.s, 0)::int, coalesce(bk.r, 0)::int, coalesce(bk.w, 0)::int, coalesce(cf.n, 0)::int,
    (select count(*) from timetable_entries e where e.room_id = rm.id)::int
  from rm left join bk on bk.room_id = rm.id left join cf on cf.room_id = rm.id
  order by rm.school, rm.name;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func rubric_class_summary(p_assignment uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.rubric_class_summary(p_assignment uuid)
 RETURNS TABLE(idx integer, name text, points numeric, graded integer, avg_level numeric, l1 integer, l2 integer, l3 integer, l4 integer)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare a assignments;
begin
  select * into a from assignments where id = p_assignment;
  if a.id is null or a.rubric is null then return; end if;
  if not (public.teaches_class(a.class_id) or (a.org_id = public.my_org_id() and public.is_admin())
          or (public.is_authority() and a.org_id in (select public.my_org_tree())) or public.is_platform_admin()) then
    raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return query
    select c.i::int - 1, c.v->>'name', (c.v->>'points')::numeric,
           count(s.lv)::int, round(avg(s.lv), 2),
           count(*) filter (where s.lv = 1)::int, count(*) filter (where s.lv = 2)::int,
           count(*) filter (where s.lv = 3)::int, count(*) filter (where s.lv = 4)::int
      from jsonb_array_elements(a.rubric->'criteria') with ordinality c(v, i)
      left join lateral (select (x.rubric_scores->>(c.i::int - 1))::int as lv from submissions x
                          where x.assignment_id = a.id and jsonb_typeof(x.rubric_scores) = 'array'
                            and jsonb_typeof(x.rubric_scores->(c.i::int - 1)) = 'number') s on true
     group by c.i, c.v order by c.i;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func rubric_score(p_rubric jsonb, p_levels jsonb, p_max numeric)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.rubric_score(p_rubric jsonb, p_levels jsonb, p_max numeric)
 RETURNS numeric
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 IMMUTABLE
AS $function$
declare n int := jsonb_array_length(p_rubric->'criteria'); tot numeric := 0; got numeric := 0; i int; lv int;
begin
  if p_levels is null or jsonb_typeof(p_levels) <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(p_levels) <> n then return null; end if;
  for i in 0 .. n - 1 loop
    if jsonb_typeof(p_levels->i) <> 'number' then return null; end if;
    lv := (p_levels->>i)::int;
    if lv not between 1 and 4 then raise exception 'Mức đạt phải từ 1 đến 4' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    tot := tot + (p_rubric->'criteria'->i->>'points')::numeric;
    got := got + (p_rubric->'criteria'->i->>'points')::numeric * lv / 4;
  end loop;
  return round(round(got / tot * p_max * 4) / 4, 2);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func rubric_valid(p jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.rubric_valid(p jsonb)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 IMMUTABLE
AS $function$
  select jsonb_typeof(p) = 'array' and jsonb_array_length(p) between 1 and 12
     and not exists (select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(p) c
                      where coalesce(btrim(c->>'name'), '') = ''
                         or jsonb_typeof(c->'points') <> 'number' or (c->>'points')::numeric <= 0
                         or jsonb_typeof(c->'levels') <> 'array' or jsonb_array_length(c->'levels') <> 4)
$function$
;
-- ##### func school_last_login()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.school_last_login()
 RETURNS TABLE(org_id uuid, last_login timestamp with time zone)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
#variable_conflict use_column
begin
  if to_regclass('auth.sessions') is null then return; end if;
  return query execute $q$select m.org_id, max(coalesce(s.updated_at, s.created_at)) from memberships m
    join auth.sessions s on s.user_id = m.user_id join organizations o on o.id = m.org_id where o.type = 'school' group by m.org_id$q$;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func school_module_events(p_days integer)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.school_module_events(p_days integer DEFAULT 28)
 RETURNS TABLE(org_id uuid, ph1 integer, ph2 integer, ph3 integer, ph4 integer)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with sc as (select id from organizations where type = 'school'),
  t1 as (select t.org_id, count(*)::int n from training_attendance a join training_sessions s on s.id = a.session_id
          join teachers t on t.id = a.teacher_id where a.present and s.starts_at >= now() - make_interval(days => p_days) group by t.org_id),
  t2 as (select org_id, sum(n)::int n from (
           select org_id, count(*) n from lesson_plans where updated_at >= now() - make_interval(days => p_days) group by org_id
           union all select org_id, count(*) from activities where updated_at >= now() - make_interval(days => p_days) group by org_id
           union all select org_id, count(*) from self_study_items where created_at >= now() - make_interval(days => p_days) group by org_id) x group by org_id),
  t3 as (select org_id, sum(n)::int n from (
           select org_id, count(*) n from borrow_requests where created_at >= now() - make_interval(days => p_days) group by org_id
           union all select org_id, count(*) from teaching_sessions where status = 'taught' and room_id is not null
             and session_date >= current_date - p_days group by org_id) x group by org_id),
  t4 as (select org_id, count(*)::int n from teaching_sessions where status = 'taught' and session_date >= current_date - p_days group by org_id)
  select sc.id, coalesce(t1.n, 0), coalesce(t2.n, 0), coalesce(t3.n, 0), coalesce(t4.n, 0)
  from sc left join t1 on t1.org_id = sc.id left join t2 on t2.org_id = sc.id left join t3 on t3.org_id = sc.id left join t4 on t4.org_id = sc.id
$function$
;
-- ##### func self_study_open(p_item uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.self_study_open(p_item uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare i self_study_items; c self_study_content; p self_study_progress; v_st uuid := public.my_student_id();
begin
  if not public.is_student() or v_st is null then raise exception 'Chỉ học sinh làm bài tự học' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  select * into i from self_study_items where id = p_item and class_id = public.my_class_id() and status <> 'draft' and opens_at <= now();
  if not found then raise exception 'Không tìm thấy bài tự học của lớp em' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  select * into c from self_study_content where item_id = i.id;
  insert into self_study_progress (item_id, student_id, org_id) values (i.id, v_st, i.org_id) on conflict do nothing;
  select * into p from self_study_progress where item_id = i.id and student_id = v_st;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'kind', c.kind,
    'content', public.activity_playable(c.kind, c.content, i.id::text || v_st::text),
    'progress', jsonb_build_object('status', p.status, 'attempts', p.attempts, 'score', p.score, 'best_score', p.best_score,
      'correct', p.correct, 'total', p.total, 'answers', p.answers, 'teacher_comment', p.teacher_comment, 'completed_at', p.completed_at),
    'attempts_left', case when i.max_attempts = 0 then null else greatest(0, i.max_attempts - p.attempts) end,
    'can_submit', i.status = 'open' and (i.max_attempts = 0 or p.attempts < i.max_attempts),
    'solution', case when public.self_study_reveal(i, p) then c.content end,
    'grade', case when p.answers is not null and c.kind not in ('lesson','link') then public.activity_grade(c.kind, c.content, p.answers) end);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func self_study_progress_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.self_study_progress_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if current_user = 'authenticated' then
    new.item_id := old.item_id; new.student_id := old.student_id; new.org_id := old.org_id; new.answers := old.answers;
    new.attempts := old.attempts; new.started_at := old.started_at; new.submitted_at := old.submitted_at;
    new.completed_at := old.completed_at; new.late := old.late; new.correct := old.correct; new.total := old.total;
    if new.score is distinct from old.score then new.best_score := greatest(coalesce(old.best_score, 0), coalesce(new.score, 0)); end if;
    if new.teacher_comment is distinct from old.teacher_comment or new.score is distinct from old.score then new.reviewed_at := now(); end if;
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func self_study_reveal(i self_study_items, p self_study_progress)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.self_study_reveal(i self_study_items, p self_study_progress)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE
AS $function$
  select i.status = 'closed' or (i.due_at is not null and i.due_at < now())
      or (i.max_attempts > 0 and coalesce(p.attempts, 0) >= i.max_attempts)
$function$
;
-- ##### func self_study_snapshot()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.self_study_snapshot()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_kind text; v_content jsonb; v_org uuid; v_status text; v_owner uuid; v_tpl boolean; v_err text;
begin
  select org_id into v_org from classes where id = new.class_id;
  new.org_id := v_org;
  if new.kind = 'activity' then
    if new.activity_id is null then raise exception 'Chọn hoạt động cần giao' using errcode = '23514'; end if;
    select kind, content, org_id, status, teacher_id, is_template into v_kind, v_content, v_org, v_status, v_owner, v_tpl
      from activities where id = new.activity_id;
    if v_org is distinct from new.org_id then raise exception 'Hoạt động không thuộc trường này' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
    if v_status <> 'published' and not v_tpl and v_owner is distinct from new.teacher_id then
      raise exception 'Chỉ giao được hoạt động đã xuất bản hoặc của chính mình' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
    v_err := public.activity_problem(v_kind, v_content);
    if v_err is not null then raise exception 'Hoạt động chưa hoàn chỉnh: %', v_err using errcode = '23514'; end if;
    new.activity_kind := v_kind;
  elsif new.kind = 'lesson' then
    if new.lesson_plan_id is null then raise exception 'Chọn bài giảng cần giao' using errcode = '23514'; end if;
    select 'lesson', jsonb_build_object('slides', slides), org_id into v_kind, v_content, v_org from lesson_plans where id = new.lesson_plan_id;
    if v_org is distinct from new.org_id then raise exception 'Bài giảng không thuộc trường này' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
    new.activity_kind := null;
  else
    v_kind := 'link'; v_content := jsonb_build_object('url', new.url); new.activity_kind := null;
  end if;
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' or new.activity_id is distinct from old.activity_id or new.lesson_plan_id is distinct from old.lesson_plan_id
     or new.url is distinct from old.url or new.kind is distinct from old.kind then
    -- Bảng nội dung có khoá ngoại tới item → ghi sau khi item đã có (trigger AFTER bên dưới)
    perform set_config('app.ss_kind', v_kind, true);
    perform set_config('app.ss_content', v_content::text, true);
  else
    perform set_config('app.ss_kind', '', true);
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func self_study_snapshot_save()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.self_study_snapshot_save()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if coalesce(current_setting('app.ss_kind', true), '') <> '' then
    insert into self_study_content (item_id, org_id, kind, content)
      values (new.id, new.org_id, current_setting('app.ss_kind', true), current_setting('app.ss_content', true)::jsonb)
    on conflict (item_id) do update set kind = excluded.kind, content = excluded.content, org_id = excluded.org_id;
    perform set_config('app.ss_kind', '', true);
  end if;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func self_study_submit(p_item uuid, p_answers jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.self_study_submit(p_item uuid, p_answers jsonb DEFAULT NULL::jsonb)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare i self_study_items; c self_study_content; p self_study_progress; v_st uuid := public.my_student_id(); g jsonb; v_score numeric;
begin
  if not public.is_student() or v_st is null then raise exception 'Chỉ học sinh làm bài tự học' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  select * into i from self_study_items where id = p_item and class_id = public.my_class_id() and status <> 'draft' and opens_at <= now();
  if not found then raise exception 'Không tìm thấy bài tự học của lớp em' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if i.status = 'closed' then raise exception 'Thầy cô đã khoá bài này' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  select * into c from self_study_content where item_id = i.id;
  insert into self_study_progress (item_id, student_id, org_id) values (i.id, v_st, i.org_id) on conflict do nothing;
  select * into p from self_study_progress where item_id = i.id and student_id = v_st for update;
  if i.max_attempts > 0 and p.attempts >= i.max_attempts then raise exception 'Em đã hết lượt làm bài này' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if p_answers is not null and length(p_answers::text) > 20000 then raise exception 'Câu trả lời quá dài' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  g := public.activity_grade(c.kind, c.content, p_answers);
  v_score := case when (g->>'total')::int > 0 then round(10.0 * (g->>'correct')::int / (g->>'total')::int, 1) end;
  update self_study_progress set status = 'done', attempts = attempts + 1, answers = p_answers,
    score = v_score, best_score = case when v_score is null then best_score else greatest(coalesce(best_score, 0), v_score) end,
    correct = case when (g->>'total')::int > 0 then (g->>'correct')::int end, total = case when (g->>'total')::int > 0 then (g->>'total')::int end,
    late = late or (i.due_at is not null and now() > i.due_at),
    submitted_at = now(), completed_at = coalesce(completed_at, now())
  where item_id = i.id and student_id = v_st
  returning * into p;
  return jsonb_build_object('score', p.score, 'best_score', p.best_score, 'correct', p.correct, 'total', p.total,
    'grade', g, 'attempts', p.attempts,
    'attempts_left', case when i.max_attempts = 0 then null else greatest(0, i.max_attempts - p.attempts) end,
    'solution', case when public.self_study_reveal(i, p) then c.content end);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func session_tally(p_session uuid, p_slide text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.session_tally(p_session uuid, p_slide text)
 RETURNS TABLE(answer integer, n bigint)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select r.answer, count(*) from session_responses r join class_sessions s on s.id = r.session_id
   where r.session_id = p_session and r.slide_id = p_slide
     and (public.teaches_class(s.class_id) or (public.is_staff() and public.can_access_org(s.org_id)))
   group by r.answer
$function$
;
-- ##### func set_current_year(p_year uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.set_current_year(p_year uuid)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_org uuid;
begin
  select org_id into v_org from academic_years where id = p_year;
  if v_org is null or not public.can_access_org(v_org) or not public.has_perm('periods', 'edit') then
    raise exception 'Không có quyền đặt học kỳ hiện tại';
  end if;
  update academic_years set is_current = false where org_id = v_org and is_current and id <> p_year;
  update academic_years set is_current = true where id = p_year;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func set_school_location(p_org uuid, p_lat double precision, p_lng double precision)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.set_school_location(p_org uuid, p_lat double precision, p_lng double precision)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if (select type from organizations where id = p_org) is distinct from 'school' then
    raise exception 'Chỉ đặt vị trí cho trường học' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if not (public.is_platform_admin()
          or (public.is_authority() and p_org in (select public.my_org_tree()))
          or (p_org = public.my_org_id() and public.is_admin())) then
    raise exception 'Không có quyền đặt vị trí trường này' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if (p_lat is null) <> (p_lng is null) then
    raise exception 'Cần cả vĩ độ và kinh độ' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  update organizations set lat = p_lat, lng = p_lng where id = p_org;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func slot_text(p_during tstzrange)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.slot_text(p_during tstzrange)
 RETURNS text
 LANGUAGE sql
 IMMUTABLE
AS $function$
  select to_char(lower(p_during) at time zone 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh', 'DD/MM/YYYY HH24:MI') || '–' ||
         to_char(upper(p_during) at time zone 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh', 'HH24:MI')
$function$
;
-- ##### func start_class_session(p_class uuid, p_title text, p_lesson uuid, p_plan uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.start_class_session(p_class uuid, p_title text, p_lesson uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_plan uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid)
 RETURNS class_sessions
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare s class_sessions; v_pin text; v_org uuid; tries int := 0;
begin
  if not public.teaches_class(p_class) then raise exception 'Bạn không dạy lớp này'; end if;
  select org_id into v_org from classes where id = p_class;
  if p_plan is not null and not exists (select 1 from lesson_plans where id = p_plan and org_id = v_org) then
    raise exception 'Bài giảng không thuộc trường này';
  end if;
  update class_sessions set status = 'ended', ended_at = now() where class_id = p_class and status = 'live';
  loop
    v_pin := lpad((floor(random() * 1000000))::int::text, 6, '0');
    begin
      insert into class_sessions (org_id, teacher_id, class_id, lesson_id, lesson_plan_id, title, pin)
        values (v_org, public.my_teacher_id(), p_class, p_lesson, p_plan, coalesce(nullif(p_title,''), 'Tiết học STEM'), v_pin)
        returning * into s;
      return s;
    exception when unique_violation then
      tries := tries + 1; if tries > 20 then raise; end if;
    end;
  end loop;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func stem_index_config()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.stem_index_config()
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with recursive up as (
    select id, parent_id, 0 as d from organizations where id = public.my_org_id()
    union all
    select o.id, o.parent_id, up.d + 1 from organizations o join up on o.id = up.parent_id where up.d < 10
  )
  select to_jsonb(c) || jsonb_build_object('source', o.name, 'own', c.org_id = public.my_org_id())
    from stem_index_configs c join organizations o on o.id = c.org_id
   where auth.uid() is not null and (c.org_id in (select id from up) or o.is_platform)
   order by case when o.is_platform and c.org_id not in (select id from up) then 99 else (select d from up where up.id = c.org_id) end
   limit 1
$function$
;
-- ##### func stock_target_ok(p_from uuid, p_to uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.stock_target_ok(p_from uuid, p_to uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare a uuid;
begin
  if p_to is null or p_to = p_from then return false; end if;
  if public.is_platform_admin() or public.can_access_org(p_to) then return true; end if;
  foreach a in array array[public.org_ancestor_of_type(p_from, 'department'), public.org_ancestor_of_type(p_from, 'province')] loop
    if a is not null and public.org_ancestor_of_type(p_to, (select type from organizations where id = a)) = a then return true; end if;
  end loop;
  return false;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func stock_targets()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.stock_targets()
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, name text, type text)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select o.id, o.name, o.type from organizations o
   where public.is_staff() and o.status is distinct from 'suspended' and public.stock_target_ok(public.my_org_id(), o.id)
   order by o.type <> 'school', o.name
$function$
;
-- ##### func stock_transfer_receive(p_transfer uuid, p_lines jsonb, p_note text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.stock_transfer_receive(p_transfer uuid, p_lines jsonb DEFAULT NULL::jsonb, p_note text DEFAULT NULL::text)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare tr stock_transfers; it record; src supplies; u record; x jsonb; v_qty numeric; v_supply uuid; v_type uuid; v_from text;
        n_sup int := 0; n_unit int := 0; n_lost int := 0; n_short int := 0;
begin
  select * into tr from stock_transfers where id = p_transfer for update;
  if tr.id is null or tr.to_org <> public.my_org_id() or not public.can_stock('create') then raise exception 'Chỉ đơn vị nhận xác nhận được' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if tr.status <> 'shipped' then raise exception 'Phiếu chưa xuất hàng hoặc đã nhận' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  v_from := (select name from organizations where id = tr.org_id);
  perform set_config('app.stock_engine', 'on', true);
  for it in select * from stock_transfer_items where transfer_id = tr.id loop
    select e into x from jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(p_lines, '[]'::jsonb)) e where e->>'id' = it.id::text;
    if it.supply_id is not null then
      v_qty := coalesce((x->>'qty')::numeric, it.qty);
      if v_qty < 0 then raise exception 'Số lượng nhận không hợp lệ' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
      select * into src from supplies where id = it.supply_id;
      select id into v_supply from supplies s where s.org_id = tr.to_org
         and ((src.sku is not null and lower(s.sku) = lower(src.sku)) or (lower(btrim(s.name)) = lower(btrim(coalesce(src.name, it.name))) and lower(s.unit) = lower(coalesce(src.unit, it.unit, 'cái'))))
       order by (src.sku is not null and lower(s.sku) = lower(src.sku)) desc, s.created_at limit 1;
      if v_supply is null then
        insert into supplies (org_id, sku, name, unit, category)
          values (tr.to_org, case when src.sku is not null and not exists (select 1 from supplies where org_id = tr.to_org and lower(sku) = lower(src.sku)) then src.sku end,
                  coalesce(src.name, it.name), coalesce(src.unit, it.unit, 'cái'), src.category)
          returning id into v_supply;
      end if;
      if v_qty > 0 then
        insert into supply_moves (org_id, supply_id, kind, qty, reason) values (tr.to_org, v_supply, 'in', v_qty, 'Nhận vận chuyển từ ' || v_from || ': ' || tr.title);
      end if;
      if v_qty < it.qty then n_short := n_short + 1; end if;
      update stock_transfer_items set received_qty = v_qty where id = it.id;
      n_sup := n_sup + 1;
    else
      select un.*, t.sku as t_sku, t.name as t_name, t.brand as t_brand, t.category as t_cat into u
        from equipment_units un join equipment_types t on t.id = un.type_id where un.id = it.unit_id;
      if coalesce((x->>'ok')::boolean, true) then
        select id into v_type from equipment_types et where et.org_id = tr.to_org
           and ((u.t_sku is not null and lower(et.sku) = lower(u.t_sku)) or lower(btrim(et.name)) = lower(btrim(u.t_name)))
         order by (u.t_sku is not null and lower(et.sku) = lower(u.t_sku)) desc limit 1;
        if v_type is null then
          insert into equipment_types (org_id, sku, name, brand, category) values (tr.to_org, u.t_sku, u.t_name, u.t_brand, u.t_cat) returning id into v_type;
        end if;
        update equipment_units set org_id = tr.to_org, type_id = v_type, room_id = null, status = 'in_stock' where id = it.unit_id;
        insert into equipment_events (org_id, unit_id, kind, title, note) values (tr.to_org, it.unit_id, 'transfer', 'Nhận vận chuyển từ ' || v_from, tr.title);
        update stock_transfer_items set received_qty = 1 where id = it.id;
        n_unit := n_unit + 1;
      else
        update equipment_units set status = 'lost' where id = it.unit_id;
        insert into equipment_events (org_id, unit_id, kind, title, note) values (u.org_id, it.unit_id, 'transfer', 'Thất lạc khi vận chuyển', tr.title);
        update stock_transfer_items set received_qty = 0 where id = it.id;
        n_lost := n_lost + 1;
      end if;
    end if;
  end loop;
  update stock_transfers set status = 'received', received_at = now(), received_by = auth.uid(), receive_note = nullif(btrim(p_note), '') where id = tr.id;
  perform set_config('app.stock_engine', 'off', true);
  perform public.notify_user(tr.created_by, tr.org_id, 'status', 'Đơn vị nhận đã xác nhận phiếu vận chuyển',
    tr.title || case when n_lost + n_short > 0 then format(' — %s dòng thiếu / thất lạc', n_lost + n_short) else '' end, 'Vận chuyển vật tư', null, tr.id);
  return jsonb_build_object('supplies', n_sup, 'units', n_unit, 'lost', n_lost, 'short', n_short);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func stock_transfer_ship(p_transfer uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.stock_transfer_ship(p_transfer uuid)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare tr stock_transfers; it record; v_to text; n int := 0;
begin
  select * into tr from stock_transfers where id = p_transfer for update;
  if tr.id is null or tr.org_id <> public.my_org_id() or not public.can_stock('edit') then raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if tr.status <> 'approved' then raise exception 'Phiếu chưa được duyệt hoặc đã xuất' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from stock_transfer_items where transfer_id = tr.id) then raise exception 'Phiếu chưa có dòng nào' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  v_to := (select name from organizations where id = tr.to_org);
  perform set_config('app.stock_engine', 'on', true);
  for it in select * from stock_transfer_items where transfer_id = tr.id loop
    if it.supply_id is not null then
      insert into supply_moves (org_id, supply_id, kind, qty, reason) values (tr.org_id, it.supply_id, 'out', it.qty, 'Xuất vận chuyển tới ' || v_to || ': ' || tr.title);
    else
      update equipment_units set status = 'in_transit', room_id = null where id = it.unit_id and org_id = tr.org_id and status not in ('lost', 'in_transit');
      if not found then raise exception 'Thiết bị % không còn ở đơn vị hoặc đang thất lạc / vận chuyển', it.name using errcode = '23514'; end if;
      insert into equipment_events (org_id, unit_id, kind, title, note) values (tr.org_id, it.unit_id, 'transfer', 'Xuất vận chuyển tới ' || v_to, tr.title);
    end if;
    n := n + 1;
  end loop;
  update stock_transfers set status = 'shipped', shipped_at = now(), shipped_by = auth.uid() where id = tr.id;
  perform set_config('app.stock_engine', 'off', true);
  perform public.notify_user(m.user_id, tr.to_org, 'status', 'Có hàng đang vận chuyển tới đơn vị', tr.title || ' — từ ' || (select name from organizations where id = tr.org_id),
                             'Vận chuyển vật tư', null, tr.id)
     from (select distinct m.user_id from memberships m where m.org_id = tr.to_org and public.user_has_perm(m.user_id, 'equipment', 'edit', tr.to_org)) m;
  return n;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func stocktake_approve(p_stocktake uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.stocktake_approve(p_stocktake uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare st stocktakes; r record; n_lost int := 0; n_broken int := 0; n_moved int := 0; n_adj int := 0; d numeric;
begin
  select * into st from stocktakes where id = p_stocktake for update;
  if st.id is null or st.org_id <> public.my_org_id() or not (public.is_admin() or public.has_perm('equipment', 'delete')) then
    raise exception 'Chỉ quản trị trường duyệt được kết quả kiểm kê' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if st.status <> 'submitted' then raise exception 'Đợt kiểm kê chưa nộp' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  for r in select i.*, u.status as cur_status, u.room_id as cur_room from stocktake_items i join equipment_units u on u.id = i.unit_id where i.stocktake_id = st.id loop
    if r.result is null or r.result = 'missing' then
      update equipment_units set status = 'lost' where id = r.unit_id;
      insert into equipment_events (org_id, unit_id, kind, title, note) values (st.org_id, r.unit_id, 'stocktake', 'Kiểm kê: không tìm thấy → Thất lạc', st.name);
      n_lost := n_lost + 1;
    else
      if r.result = 'broken' and r.cur_status <> 'broken' then
        update equipment_units set status = 'broken' where id = r.unit_id;
        insert into equipment_events (org_id, unit_id, kind, title, note) values (st.org_id, r.unit_id, 'stocktake', 'Kiểm kê: hỏng', coalesce(r.note, st.name));
        n_broken := n_broken + 1;
      elsif r.cur_status = 'lost' then
        update equipment_units set status = 'available' where id = r.unit_id;
        insert into equipment_events (org_id, unit_id, kind, title, note) values (st.org_id, r.unit_id, 'stocktake', 'Kiểm kê: tìm thấy lại', st.name);
      end if;
      if r.found_room_id is distinct from r.cur_room and r.found_room_id is not null then
        update equipment_units set room_id = r.found_room_id where id = r.unit_id;
        insert into equipment_events (org_id, unit_id, kind, title, note) values (st.org_id, r.unit_id, 'transfer', 'Kiểm kê: cập nhật phòng', st.name);
        n_moved := n_moved + 1;
      end if;
    end if;
  end loop;
  for r in select * from stocktake_supplies where stocktake_id = st.id and counted_qty is not null loop
    select r.counted_qty - qty into d from supplies where id = r.supply_id;
    if d <> 0 then
      insert into supply_moves (org_id, supply_id, kind, qty, reason, stocktake_id) values (st.org_id, r.supply_id, 'adjust', d, 'Kiểm kê: ' || st.name, st.id);
      n_adj := n_adj + 1;
    end if;
  end loop;
  update stocktakes set status = 'approved', approved_at = now(), approved_by = auth.uid() where id = st.id;
  return jsonb_build_object('lost', n_lost, 'broken', n_broken, 'moved', n_moved, 'adjusted', n_adj);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func stocktake_scan(p_stocktake uuid, p_code text, p_room uuid, p_result text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.stocktake_scan(p_stocktake uuid, p_code text, p_room uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_result text DEFAULT 'ok'::text)
 RETURNS TABLE(unit_id uuid, name text, serial text, expected_room text, status text)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
#variable_conflict use_column
declare st stocktakes; v_unit uuid; v_code text := btrim(coalesce(p_code, ''));
begin
  select * into st from stocktakes where id = p_stocktake;
  if st.id is null or st.org_id <> public.my_org_id() or not public.has_perm('equipment', 'edit') then raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if st.status <> 'open' then raise exception 'Đợt kiểm kê đã nộp/đóng' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if p_result not in ('ok', 'broken', 'missing') then raise exception 'Kết quả không hợp lệ' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  v_code := regexp_replace(v_code, '^KDI:U:', '', 'i');
  if v_code ~* '^[0-9a-f-]{36}$' then select id into v_unit from equipment_units where id = v_code::uuid and org_id = st.org_id; end if;
  if v_unit is null then
    select id into v_unit from equipment_units where org_id = st.org_id and lower(serial) = lower(v_code) order by id limit 1;
  end if;
  if v_unit is null then raise exception 'Không tìm thấy thiết bị "%" ở trường', v_code using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;
  -- Thiết bị ngoài danh sách dự kiến (vd ở phòng khác) vẫn được ghi nhận
  insert into stocktake_items (stocktake_id, unit_id, org_id, expected_room_id, expected_status)
    select st.id, u.id, st.org_id, u.room_id, u.status from equipment_units u where u.id = v_unit
  on conflict (stocktake_id, unit_id) do nothing;
  update stocktake_items i set result = p_result, found_room_id = coalesce(p_room, st.room_id, i.expected_room_id),
         checked_by = auth.uid(), checked_at = now()
   where i.stocktake_id = st.id and i.unit_id = v_unit;
  return query select u.id, t.name, u.serial, r.name, p_result from equipment_units u join equipment_types t on t.id = u.type_id
                left join stem_rooms r on r.id = u.room_id where u.id = v_unit;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func stocktake_start(p_name text, p_room uuid, p_supplies boolean)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.stocktake_start(p_name text, p_room uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_supplies boolean DEFAULT true)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_org uuid := public.my_org_id(); v_id uuid;
begin
  if not public.has_perm('equipment', 'create') then raise exception 'Không có quyền kiểm kê' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if coalesce(btrim(p_name), '') = '' then raise exception 'Cần tên đợt kiểm kê' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  if exists (select 1 from stocktakes where org_id = v_org and status = 'open' and room_id is not distinct from p_room) then
    raise exception 'Đang có đợt kiểm kê mở cho phạm vi này' using errcode = '23505';
  end if;
  insert into stocktakes (org_id, name, room_id, include_supplies) values (v_org, btrim(p_name), p_room, p_supplies and p_room is null) returning id into v_id;
  insert into stocktake_items (stocktake_id, unit_id, org_id, expected_room_id, expected_status)
    select v_id, u.id, v_org, u.room_id, u.status from equipment_units u
     where u.org_id = v_org and u.status <> 'lost' and (p_room is null or u.room_id = p_room);
  if p_supplies and p_room is null then
    insert into stocktake_supplies (stocktake_id, supply_id, org_id, expected_qty) select v_id, s.id, v_org, s.qty from supplies s where s.org_id = v_org;
  end if;
  return v_id;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func student_competencies(p_student uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.student_competencies(p_student uuid)
 RETURNS TABLE(competency text, name text, grp text, evidences integer, avg_level numeric, last_level integer, last_at timestamp with time zone)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if not public.can_view_student(p_student) then raise exception 'Không có quyền xem học sinh này' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  return query
    select c.key, c.name, c.grp, count(e.level)::int, round(avg(e.level), 2),
           (array_agg(e.level order by e.graded_at desc nulls last))[1], max(e.graded_at)
      from competencies c
      left join public.competency_evidence(array[p_student]) e on e.competency = c.key
     where c.audience = 'student'
     group by c.key, c.name, c.grp, c.sort order by c.sort;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func student_competency_evidence(p_student uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.student_competency_evidence(p_student uuid)
 RETURNS TABLE(competency text, level integer, assignment_id uuid, title text, graded_at timestamp with time zone)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if not public.can_view_student(p_student) then raise exception 'Không có quyền xem học sinh này' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  return query select e.competency, e.level, e.assignment_id, e.title, e.graded_at
                 from public.competency_evidence(array[p_student]) e order by e.graded_at desc nulls last;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func student_data_export(p_student uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.student_data_export(p_student uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_org uuid := public.my_org_id(); s students; v jsonb;
begin
  if not (public.is_admin() or public.has_perm('students', 'edit')) then raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  select * into s from students where id = p_student and org_id = v_org;
  if s.id is null then raise exception 'Không có học sinh này ở trường' using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;
  v := jsonb_build_object(
    'xuat_luc', now(),
    'truong', (select name from organizations where id = v_org),
    'hoc_sinh', to_jsonb(s) - 'org_id',
    'lop', (select name from classes where id = s.class_id),
    'tai_khoan', (select jsonb_build_object('ten', p.full_name, 'email', p.email, 'ten_dang_nhap', p.login) from profiles p where p.id = s.profile_id),
    'dong_y_cha_me', (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(c) - 'org_id' order by c.created_at), '[]') from student_consents c where c.student_id = s.id),
    'diem_danh', (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('ngay', t.session_date, 'mon', t.subject, 'trang_thai', a.status, 'ghi_chu', a.note) order by t.session_date), '[]')
                    from lesson_attendance a join teaching_sessions t on t.id = a.session_id where a.student_id = s.id),
    'bai_nop', (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('bai', g.title, 'nop_luc', x.submitted_at, 'noi_dung', x.content, 'tep', x.attachment_url, 'diem', x.score) order by x.submitted_at), '[]')
                  from submissions x join assignments g on g.id = x.assignment_id where x.student_id = s.id),
    'tu_hoc', (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('bai', i.title, 'diem', p.score, 'diem_cao_nhat', p.best_score, 'so_lan', p.attempts, 'hoan_thanh', p.completed_at) order by p.started_at), '[]')
                 from self_study_progress p join self_study_items i on i.id = p.item_id where p.student_id = s.id),
    'tien_do_bai_hoc', (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(l) - 'org_id'), '[]') from lesson_progress l where l.student_id = s.id),
    'tham_gia_tiet_truc_tuyen', (select count(*) from class_session_attendance c where c.student_id = s.id));
  insert into audit_log (actor, org_id, entity, entity_id, action, summary, detail)
    values (auth.uid(), v_org, 'students', s.id::text, 'update', 'Xuất dữ liệu cá nhân học sinh ' || coalesce(s.student_code, s.id::text), null);
  return v;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func student_erase(p_student uuid, p_reason text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.student_erase(p_student uuid, p_reason text)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_org uuid := public.my_org_id(); s students; v_pid uuid; v_other boolean;
begin
  if not public.is_admin() then raise exception 'Chỉ quản trị trường (Hiệu trưởng, QTV) được xoá dữ liệu học sinh' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if coalesce(btrim(p_reason), '') = '' then raise exception 'Cần ghi lý do xoá' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  select * into s from students where id = p_student and org_id = v_org;
  if s.id is null then raise exception 'Không có học sinh này ở trường' using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;
  if s.status = 'erased' then raise exception 'Dữ liệu học sinh này đã được xoá' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  v_pid := s.profile_id;
  update submissions set content = null, attachment_url = null where student_id = s.id;
  update self_study_progress set answers = null where student_id = s.id;
  delete from student_consents where student_id = s.id;
  delete from student_group_members where student_id = s.id;
  delete from invitations where student_id = s.id;
  update students set full_name = 'Học sinh đã xoá dữ liệu', student_code = null, profile_id = null, status = 'erased' where id = s.id;
  if v_pid is not null then
    delete from memberships where user_id = v_pid and org_id = v_org;
    v_other := exists (select 1 from memberships where user_id = v_pid);
    if not v_other then
      perform set_config('app.login_admin', 'on', true);
      update profiles set full_name = 'Tài khoản đã xoá', email = null, phone = null, login = null, org_id = null,
             locked_at = now(), locked_reason = 'Đã xoá dữ liệu cá nhân' where id = v_pid;
      perform set_config('app.login_admin', '', true);
    end if;
  end if;
  insert into audit_log (actor, org_id, entity, entity_id, action, summary, detail)
    values (auth.uid(), v_org, 'students', s.id::text, 'delete', 'Xoá dữ liệu cá nhân học sinh ' || coalesce(s.student_code, s.id::text),
            jsonb_build_object('reason', p_reason, 'account', v_pid is not null));
  return case when v_pid is not null and not v_other then v_pid end;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func student_sees_topic(p_topic uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.student_sees_topic(p_topic uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select exists (select 1 from class_topics t where t.id = p_topic and t.class_id = public.my_class_id())
$function$
;
-- ##### func supply_request_receive(p_request uuid, p_lines jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.supply_request_receive(p_request uuid, p_lines jsonb DEFAULT NULL::jsonb)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare rq supply_requests; l record; v_qty numeric; v_supply uuid; n int := 0;
begin
  select * into rq from supply_requests where id = p_request for update;
  if rq.id is null or rq.org_id <> public.my_org_id() or not public.can_stock('create') then raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if rq.status <> 'approved' then raise exception 'Đề xuất chưa được duyệt hoặc đã nhận hàng' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  perform set_config('app.stock_engine', 'on', true);
  for l in select * from supply_request_lines where request_id = rq.id order by name loop
    v_qty := coalesce((select (x->>'qty')::numeric from jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(p_lines, '[]'::jsonb)) x where x->>'id' = l.id::text), l.qty);
    if v_qty < 0 then raise exception 'Số lượng nhận không hợp lệ' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    v_supply := l.supply_id;
    if v_supply is null or not exists (select 1 from supplies where id = v_supply and org_id = rq.org_id) then
      select id into v_supply from supplies where org_id = rq.org_id and lower(btrim(name)) = lower(btrim(l.name)) and lower(unit) = lower(l.unit) order by created_at limit 1;
      if v_supply is null then
        insert into supplies (org_id, name, unit) values (rq.org_id, btrim(l.name), l.unit) returning id into v_supply;
      end if;
    end if;
    if v_qty > 0 then
      insert into supply_moves (org_id, supply_id, kind, qty, reason) values (rq.org_id, v_supply, 'in', v_qty, 'Nhận hàng theo đề xuất: ' || rq.title);
      n := n + 1;
    end if;
    update supply_request_lines set received_qty = v_qty, supply_id = v_supply where id = l.id;
  end loop;
  update supply_requests set status = 'received', received_at = now() where id = rq.id;
  perform set_config('app.stock_engine', 'off', true);
  return n;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func support_notify()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.support_notify()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare t support_tickets; v_platform_org uuid := (select id from organizations where is_platform limit 1); v_is_kdi boolean;
begin
  if tg_table_name = 'support_tickets' then
    insert into notifications (org_id, user_id, kind, title, body, page, portal, ref_id)
      select v_platform_org, m.user_id, 'support', 'Phiếu hỗ trợ mới ' || new.code, new.subject || ' · ' || (select name from organizations where id = new.org_id),
             'Vấn đề & phản hồi', 'platform', new.id
        from memberships m join roles r on r.id = m.role_id
       where r.key = 'platform_admin' and r.org_id is null and m.org_id = v_platform_org and m.user_id is distinct from new.reported_by;
    return null;
  end if;
  select * into t from support_tickets where id = new.ticket_id;
  v_is_kdi := exists (select 1 from memberships m join organizations o on o.id = m.org_id where m.user_id = new.author and o.is_platform);
  if v_is_kdi and not new.internal then
    update support_tickets set first_response_at = coalesce(first_response_at, now()),
           status = case when status = 'new' then 'in_progress' else status end where id = t.id;
    if t.reported_by is not null and t.reported_by <> new.author then
      insert into notifications (org_id, user_id, kind, title, body, page, ref_id)
        values (t.org_id, t.reported_by, 'support', 'KDI đã trả lời phiếu ' || t.code, left(new.body, 200), 'Hỗ trợ từ KDI', t.id);
    end if;
  elsif not v_is_kdi then
    update support_tickets set status = 'in_progress' where id = t.id and status in ('waiting_customer','resolved');
    insert into notifications (org_id, user_id, kind, title, body, page, portal, ref_id)
      select v_platform_org, coalesce(t.assignee, m.user_id), 'support', 'Khách hàng phản hồi phiếu ' || t.code, left(new.body, 200), 'Vấn đề & phản hồi', 'platform', t.id
        from memberships m join roles r on r.id = m.role_id
       where r.key = 'platform_admin' and r.org_id is null and m.org_id = v_platform_org
         and (t.assignee is null or m.user_id = t.assignee);
  end if;
  return null;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func support_sla(p_priority text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.support_sla(p_priority text)
 RETURNS interval
 LANGUAGE sql
 IMMUTABLE
AS $function$
  select case p_priority when 'urgent' then interval '4 hours' when 'high' then interval '1 day'
                         when 'normal' then interval '3 days' else interval '7 days' end
$function$
;
-- ##### func support_ticket_before()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.support_ticket_before()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_platform boolean := public.is_platform_admin();
begin
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
    new.code := 'HT-' || lpad(nextval('support_ticket_seq')::text, 5, '0');
    new.created_at := now();
    new.due_at := now() + public.support_sla(new.priority);
    if not v_platform then new.status := 'new'; new.assignee := null; new.reported_by := auth.uid(); end if;
  else
    if not v_platform and current_setting('role', true) = 'authenticated' then
      -- Khách hàng chỉ đóng phiếu hoặc mở lại; không đổi người xử lý, mức ưu tiên, hạn, đơn vị
      if (new.org_id, new.category, new.priority, new.assignee, new.due_at, new.reported_by, new.subject, new.warranty_ticket_id, new.first_response_at)
         is distinct from (old.org_id, old.category, old.priority, old.assignee, old.due_at, old.reported_by, old.subject, old.warranty_ticket_id, old.first_response_at)
         or (new.status is distinct from old.status and new.status not in ('closed','in_progress')) then
        raise exception 'Chỉ KDI cập nhật được phiếu hỗ trợ (bạn có thể đóng hoặc mở lại phiếu)' using errcode = '42501';
      end if;
    end if;
    if new.priority is distinct from old.priority and new.status not in ('resolved','closed') then
      new.due_at := old.created_at + public.support_sla(new.priority);
    end if;
    if new.status in ('resolved','closed') and old.status not in ('resolved','closed') then new.resolved_at := now();
    elsif new.status not in ('resolved','closed') then new.resolved_at := null; end if;
    new.updated_at := now();
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func survey_for_me(p_survey uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.survey_for_me(p_survey uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select exists (select 1 from surveys s where s.id = p_survey and s.status = 'open'
    and (s.opens_at is null or s.opens_at <= now()) and (s.closes_at is null or s.closes_at > now())
    and public.my_org_id() is not null and public.org_in_tree(public.my_org_id(), s.org_id)
    and case s.audience when 'student' then public.is_student()
                        when 'teacher' then public.my_teacher_id() is not null
                        when 'parent' then public.is_parent()
                        else public.is_staff() end)
$function$
;
-- ##### func survey_results(p_survey uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.survey_results(p_survey uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare s surveys; q jsonb; out jsonb := '[]'::jsonb; item jsonb;
begin
  select * into s from surveys where id = p_survey;
  if s.id is null then raise exception 'Không có khảo sát' using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;
  if not (public.can_manage_survey(s.org_id) or (public.is_authority() and s.org_id in (select public.my_org_tree()))) then
    raise exception 'Không có quyền xem kết quả' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  for q in select * from jsonb_array_elements(s.questions) loop
    if q->>'type' in ('single', 'multi') then
      select jsonb_build_object('id', q->>'id', 'counts', coalesce(jsonb_agg(cnt order by i), '[]'::jsonb)) into item
        from (select i - 1 as i, (select count(*) from survey_responses r where r.survey_id = s.id
                and (r.answers -> (q->>'id') = to_jsonb(i - 1) or (jsonb_typeof(r.answers -> (q->>'id')) = 'array' and r.answers -> (q->>'id') @> to_jsonb(array[i - 1]))))::int as cnt
                from generate_series(1, jsonb_array_length(q->'options')) i) t;
    elsif q->>'type' = 'scale' then
      select jsonb_build_object('id', q->>'id',
               'counts', (select jsonb_agg((select count(*) from survey_responses r where r.survey_id = s.id and r.answers -> (q->>'id') = to_jsonb(v))::int order by v) from generate_series(1, 5) v),
               'avg', round(avg((r.answers ->> (q->>'id'))::numeric), 2)) into item
        from survey_responses r where r.survey_id = s.id and jsonb_typeof(r.answers -> (q->>'id')) = 'number';
    else
      select jsonb_build_object('id', q->>'id', 'texts', coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
               'text', r.answers ->> (q->>'id'),
               'who', case when s.anonymous then null else (select full_name from profiles p where p.id = r.respondent) end,
               'org', (select name from organizations o where o.id = r.org_id)) order by r.submitted_at desc), '[]'::jsonb)) into item
        from survey_responses r where r.survey_id = s.id and coalesce(r.answers ->> (q->>'id'), '') <> '';
    end if;
    out := out || item;
  end loop;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'responses', (select count(*) from survey_responses where survey_id = s.id),
    'by_org', (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('org', o.name, 'n', t.n) order by t.n desc), '[]'::jsonb)
                 from (select org_id, count(*) n from survey_responses where survey_id = s.id group by org_id) t left join organizations o on o.id = t.org_id),
    'questions', out,
    'rows', case when s.anonymous then null else (select coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('who', p.full_name, 'org', o.name, 'at', r.submitted_at, 'answers', r.answers) order by r.submitted_at), '[]'::jsonb)
                 from survey_responses r join profiles p on p.id = r.respondent left join organizations o on o.id = r.org_id where r.survey_id = s.id) end);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func survey_submit(p_survey uuid, p_answers jsonb)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.survey_submit(p_survey uuid, p_answers jsonb)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare s surveys; q jsonb; a jsonb; v_id uuid; n int;
begin
  if not public.survey_for_me(p_survey) then raise exception 'Khảo sát không dành cho bạn hoặc đã đóng' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if jsonb_typeof(p_answers) <> 'object' then raise exception 'Câu trả lời không hợp lệ' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
  select * into s from surveys where id = p_survey;
  for q in select * from jsonb_array_elements(s.questions) loop
    a := p_answers -> (q->>'id');
    if a is null or a = 'null'::jsonb or a = '""'::jsonb or a = '[]'::jsonb then
      if coalesce((q->>'required')::boolean, false) then raise exception 'Chưa trả lời câu bắt buộc: %', q->>'text' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
      continue;
    end if;
    case q->>'type'
      when 'single' then
        if jsonb_typeof(a) <> 'number' or (a::text)::int not between 0 and jsonb_array_length(q->'options') - 1 then raise exception 'Lựa chọn không hợp lệ: %', q->>'text' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
      when 'multi' then
        if jsonb_typeof(a) <> 'array' or exists (select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(a) x where jsonb_typeof(x) <> 'number' or (x::text)::int not between 0 and jsonb_array_length(q->'options') - 1) then
          raise exception 'Lựa chọn không hợp lệ: %', q->>'text' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
      when 'scale' then
        if jsonb_typeof(a) <> 'number' or (a::text)::numeric not in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5) then raise exception 'Thang điểm 1–5: %', q->>'text' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
      else
        if jsonb_typeof(a) <> 'string' or length(a #>> '{}') > 4000 then raise exception 'Câu trả lời quá dài: %', q->>'text' using errcode = '22023'; end if;
    end case;
  end loop;
  -- Chỉ giữ câu trả lời cho các câu hỏi có trong khảo sát
  select coalesce(jsonb_object_agg(k, v), '{}'::jsonb) into p_answers
    from jsonb_each(p_answers) e(k, v) where exists (select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(s.questions) qq(x) where qq.x->>'id' = e.k);
  insert into survey_responses (survey_id, org_id, respondent, answers) values (p_survey, public.my_org_id(), auth.uid(), p_answers)
    on conflict (survey_id, respondent) do update set answers = excluded.answers, submitted_at = now(), org_id = excluded.org_id
    returning id into v_id;
  return v_id;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func switch_org(p_org uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.switch_org(p_org uuid)
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if not exists (select 1 from memberships where user_id = auth.uid() and org_id = p_org) then
    raise exception 'Bạn không thuộc tổ chức này' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  update profiles set org_id = p_org where id = auth.uid();
  return p_org;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func sync_teaching_sessions(p_org uuid, p_from date, p_to date)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.sync_teaching_sessions(p_org uuid, p_from date, p_to date)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_ins int; v_upd int; v_del int; v_today date := public.vn_today(); v_skip int;
begin
  if p_org is null or (select type from organizations where id = p_org) is distinct from 'school' then
    raise exception 'Chỉ sinh buổi dạy cho trường học' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if p_from is null or p_to is null or p_to < p_from or p_to - p_from > 62 then
    raise exception 'Khoảng ngày không hợp lệ (tối đa 63 ngày)' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;

  if to_regclass('pg_temp._ts_src') is null then      -- gọi nhiều lần trong một giao dịch (lịch nền) → tạo một lần
    create temp table _ts_src (entry_id uuid, class_id uuid, teacher_id uuid, period_id uuid, room_id uuid,
      day date, year_id uuid, subject text, kind text) on commit drop;
  end if;
  truncate _ts_src;
  insert into _ts_src
  select distinct on (e.class_id, d.day, e.period_id)
         e.id, e.class_id, e.teacher_id, e.period_id, e.room_id, d.day, d.year_id, e.subject, e.kind
  from (select g::date as day, y.id as year_id
          from generate_series(p_from, p_to, interval '1 day') g
          join academic_years y on y.org_id = p_org and g::date between y.starts_on and y.ends_on
         where extract(isodow from g) between 1 and 6
           and public.is_holiday(p_org, g::date) is null) d          -- ngày nghỉ của trường và mọi cấp trên
  join timetable_entries e on e.org_id = p_org
   and e.weekday = extract(isodow from d.day)::int + 1
   and (e.year_id is null or e.year_id = d.year_id)
  order by e.class_id, d.day, e.period_id, (e.year_id is null), e.id;   -- ưu tiên TKB riêng của học kỳ

  -- Tiết tương lai chưa điểm danh không còn trong nguồn (đổi TKB, rơi vào ngày nghỉ) → xoá
  delete from teaching_sessions s
   where s.org_id = p_org and s.session_date between greatest(p_from, v_today) and p_to
     and s.status = 'scheduled' and not s.is_makeup
     and not exists (select 1 from lesson_attendance a where a.session_id = s.id)
     and not exists (select 1 from _ts_src x where x.class_id = s.class_id and x.day = s.session_date and x.period_id = s.period_id);
  get diagnostics v_del = row_count;

  with up as (
    insert into teaching_sessions (org_id, year_id, timetable_entry_id, class_id, teacher_id, period_id, room_id, session_date, subject, kind)
    select p_org, year_id, entry_id, class_id, teacher_id, period_id, room_id, day, subject, kind from _ts_src
    on conflict (class_id, session_date, period_id) do update set
      year_id = excluded.year_id, timetable_entry_id = excluded.timetable_entry_id, teacher_id = excluded.teacher_id,
      room_id = excluded.room_id, subject = excluded.subject, kind = excluded.kind
    where teaching_sessions.status = 'scheduled' and not teaching_sessions.is_makeup and teaching_sessions.session_date >= v_today
      and (teaching_sessions.timetable_entry_id, teaching_sessions.teacher_id, teaching_sessions.room_id, teaching_sessions.subject, teaching_sessions.kind)
          is distinct from (excluded.timetable_entry_id, excluded.teacher_id, excluded.room_id, excluded.subject, excluded.kind)
    returning (xmax = 0) as inserted
  ) select count(*) filter (where inserted), count(*) filter (where not inserted) into v_ins, v_upd from up;

  select count(*) into v_skip from generate_series(p_from, p_to, interval '1 day') g
   where extract(isodow from g) between 1 and 6 and public.is_holiday(p_org, g::date) is not null;

  return jsonb_build_object('created', v_ins, 'updated', v_upd, 'removed', v_del, 'holidays', v_skip);
end $function$
;
-- ##### func teacher_evidence(p_teacher uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.teacher_evidence(p_teacher uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if not public.can_view_teacher(p_teacher) then raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'training_completed', (select count(*) from training_enrollments where teacher_id = p_teacher and completed),
    'training_enrolled', (select count(*) from training_enrollments where teacher_id = p_teacher),
    'training_present', (select count(*) from training_attendance where teacher_id = p_teacher and present),
    'certificates', (select count(*) from certificates where teacher_id = p_teacher),
    'stem_taught_12m', (select count(*) from teaching_sessions where teacher_id = p_teacher and kind = 'stem' and status = 'taught'
                          and session_date >= current_date - 365),
    'stem_planned_12m', (select count(*) from teaching_sessions where teacher_id = p_teacher and kind = 'stem' and status <> 'cancelled'
                          and session_date between current_date - 365 and current_date),
    'lesson_plans', (select count(*) from lesson_plans where teacher_id = p_teacher),
    'rubric_assignments', (select count(*) from assignments where teacher_id = p_teacher and rubric is not null),
    'assignments', (select count(*) from assignments where teacher_id = p_teacher));
end $function$
;

-- ##### func teacher_slot_busy(p_teacher uuid, p_day date, p_period uuid, p_class uuid, p_skip_session uuid, p_skip_reg uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.teacher_slot_busy(p_teacher uuid, p_day date, p_period uuid, p_class uuid, p_skip_session uuid, p_skip_reg uuid)
 RETURNS text
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select x.label from (
    select concat_ws(' · ', p.name, c.name, s.subject) as label
      from teaching_sessions s join period_configs p on p.id = s.period_id join classes c on c.id = s.class_id
     where s.teacher_id = p_teacher and s.session_date = p_day and s.status <> 'cancelled'
       and s.class_id <> p_class and s.id is distinct from p_skip_session
       and public.period_slot(s.session_date, s.period_id) && public.period_slot(p_day, p_period)
    union all
    select concat_ws(' · ', 'Đăng ký dạy STEM', c.name, r.topic)
      from stem_registrations r join classes c on c.id = r.class_id
     where r.teacher_id = p_teacher and r.on_date = p_day and r.status in ('pending', 'approved')
       and r.class_id <> p_class and r.id is distinct from p_skip_reg
       and public.period_slot(r.on_date, r.period_id) && public.period_slot(p_day, p_period)
  ) x limit 1
$function$
;
-- ##### func teacher_slot_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.teacher_slot_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_busy text; v_name text;
begin
  if coalesce(current_setting('kdi.booking_soft', true), '') = 'on' then return new; end if;
  if tg_table_name = 'teaching_sessions' then
    if new.teacher_id is null or new.status = 'cancelled' then return new; end if;
    if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and (old.teacher_id, old.session_date, old.period_id, old.class_id, old.status = 'cancelled')
       is not distinct from (new.teacher_id, new.session_date, new.period_id, new.class_id, new.status = 'cancelled') then return new; end if;
    -- Tiết sinh từ TKB, đúng GV của TKB: TKB đã chặn trùng
    if new.timetable_entry_id is not null and not new.is_makeup
       and new.teacher_id is not distinct from (select e.teacher_id from timetable_entries e where e.id = new.timetable_entry_id) then return new; end if;
    v_busy := public.teacher_slot_busy(new.teacher_id, new.session_date, new.period_id, new.class_id, new.id, null);
  else
    if new.status not in ('pending', 'approved') then return new; end if;
    if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and (old.teacher_id, old.on_date, old.period_id, old.class_id, old.status in ('pending', 'approved'))
       is not distinct from (new.teacher_id, new.on_date, new.period_id, new.class_id, true) then return new; end if;
    v_busy := public.teacher_slot_busy(new.teacher_id, new.on_date, new.period_id, new.class_id, null, new.id);
  end if;
  if v_busy is not null then
    select full_name into v_name from teachers where id = new.teacher_id;
    raise exception 'Giáo viên % đã có tiết trùng giờ: %', coalesce(v_name, ''), v_busy
      using errcode = '23P01', hint = 'Chọn tiết khác hoặc đổi giáo viên.';
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func teaches_class(p_class uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.teaches_class(p_class uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select public.is_admin() and exists (select 1 from classes c where c.id = p_class and public.can_access_org(c.org_id))
      or exists (select 1 from teaching_assignments ta where ta.class_id = p_class and ta.teacher_id = public.my_teacher_id())
      or exists (select 1 from classes c where c.id = p_class and c.homeroom_teacher_id = public.my_teacher_id())
$function$
;
-- ##### func timetable_conflicts()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.timetable_conflicts()
 RETURNS TABLE(org_id uuid, kind text, weekday integer, period_name text, resource text, classes text)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with e as (
    select e.*, p.name as period_name from timetable_entries e join period_configs p on p.id = e.period_id
    where e.org_id in (select public.my_org_tree()) and public.is_staff()
  )
  select a.org_id, 'teacher', a.weekday, a.period_name, t.full_name, string_agg(distinct c.name, ', ')
  from e a join e b on b.org_id = a.org_id and b.weekday = a.weekday and b.period_id = a.period_id
       and b.teacher_id = a.teacher_id and b.class_id <> a.class_id
       and (a.year_id is null or b.year_id is null or a.year_id = b.year_id)
  join teachers t on t.id = a.teacher_id join classes c on c.id in (a.class_id, b.class_id)
  group by a.org_id, a.weekday, a.period_name, t.full_name
  union all
  select a.org_id, 'room', a.weekday, a.period_name, r.name, string_agg(distinct c.name, ', ')
  from e a join e b on b.org_id = a.org_id and b.weekday = a.weekday and b.period_id = a.period_id
       and b.room_id = a.room_id and b.class_id <> a.class_id
       and (a.year_id is null or b.year_id is null or a.year_id = b.year_id)
  join stem_rooms r on r.id = a.room_id join classes c on c.id in (a.class_id, b.class_id)
  group by a.org_id, a.weekday, a.period_name, r.name
$function$
;
-- ##### func timetable_guard()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.timetable_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v text;
begin
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and (old.room_id, old.teacher_id, old.weekday, old.period_id, old.year_id, old.class_id)
     is not distinct from (new.room_id, new.teacher_id, new.weekday, new.period_id, new.year_id, new.class_id) then return new; end if;
  if new.room_id is not null then
    select r.name || ' đã có lớp ' || c.name || ' (' || e.subject || ')' into v
      from timetable_entries e join stem_rooms r on r.id = e.room_id join classes c on c.id = e.class_id
     where e.id <> new.id and e.org_id = new.org_id and e.weekday = new.weekday and e.period_id = new.period_id
       and e.room_id = new.room_id and e.class_id <> new.class_id
       and (e.year_id is null or new.year_id is null or e.year_id = new.year_id)
     limit 1;
    if v is not null then raise exception 'Trùng phòng: % ở cùng thứ, cùng tiết', v using errcode = '23P01'; end if;
  end if;
  if new.teacher_id is not null then
    select t.full_name || ' đã dạy lớp ' || c.name || ' (' || e.subject || ')' into v
      from timetable_entries e join teachers t on t.id = e.teacher_id join classes c on c.id = e.class_id
     where e.id <> new.id and e.org_id = new.org_id and e.weekday = new.weekday and e.period_id = new.period_id
       and e.teacher_id = new.teacher_id and e.class_id <> new.class_id
       and (e.year_id is null or new.year_id is null or e.year_id = new.year_id)
     limit 1;
    if v is not null then raise exception 'Trùng giáo viên: % ở cùng thứ, cùng tiết', v using errcode = '23P01'; end if;
  end if;
  return new;
end $function$
;
-- ##### func timetable_overview()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.timetable_overview()
 RETURNS TABLE(org_id uuid, school text, parent_name text, classes integer, classes_with_tkb integer, entries integer, stem_entries integer, teachers integer, conflicts integer, current_term text)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if not public.is_staff() then raise exception 'Không có quyền' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  return query
  with sc as (select o.id, o.name, par.name as parent_name from organizations o left join organizations par on par.id = o.parent_id
               where o.type = 'school' and o.id in (select public.my_org_tree())),
       cf as (select x.org_id, count(*)::int as n from public.timetable_conflicts() x group by x.org_id)
  select sc.id, sc.name, sc.parent_name,
    (select count(*) from classes c where c.org_id = sc.id)::int,
    (select count(distinct e.class_id) from timetable_entries e where e.org_id = sc.id)::int,
    (select count(*) from timetable_entries e where e.org_id = sc.id)::int,
    (select count(*) from timetable_entries e where e.org_id = sc.id and e.kind = 'stem')::int,
    (select count(distinct e.teacher_id) from timetable_entries e where e.org_id = sc.id)::int,
    coalesce((select cf.n from cf where cf.org_id = sc.id), 0),
    (select y.name || ' · ' || y.term from academic_years y where y.org_id = sc.id and y.is_current limit 1)
  from sc order by sc.name;
end $function$
;

;
-- ##### func training_check_in(p_session uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.training_check_in(p_session uuid)
 RETURNS text
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
declare v_teacher uuid := public.my_teacher_id(); v_row training_attendance;
begin
  if v_teacher is null then raise exception 'Tài khoản chưa liên kết hồ sơ giáo viên' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  select * into v_row from training_attendance where session_id = p_session and teacher_id = v_teacher;
  if not found then raise exception 'Bạn không có tên trong danh sách buổi tập huấn này' using errcode = '42501'; end if;
  if not public.training_session_open(p_session) then
    raise exception 'Chỉ điểm danh được trong giờ buổi tập huấn' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_row.present is true then return 'already'; end if;
  update training_attendance set present = true where session_id = p_session and teacher_id = v_teacher;
  return 'ok';
end $function$
;
-- ##### func training_session_open(p_session uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.training_session_open(p_session uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select exists (select 1 from training_sessions s where s.id = p_session
    and now() between s.starts_at - interval '15 minutes' and s.ends_at + interval '15 minutes')
$function$
;
-- ##### func user_has_perm(p_user uuid, p_module text, p_action text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.user_has_perm(p_user uuid, p_module text, p_action text)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  select public.user_has_perm(p_user, p_module, p_action, (select org_id from profiles where id = p_user))
$function$
;
-- ##### func user_has_perm(p_user uuid, p_module text, p_action text, p_org uuid)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.user_has_perm(p_user uuid, p_module text, p_action text, p_org uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  with mine as (select r.id as role_id, r.key, r.org_id from memberships m join roles r on r.id = m.role_id
                where m.user_id = p_user and m.org_id = p_org)
  select exists (select 1 from mine where org_id is null and key in ('principal','it_admin'))
      or exists (
        select 1 from mine m
        left join org_role_permissions o on o.role_id = m.role_id and o.module = p_module and o.org_id = p_org
        left join role_permissions d on d.role_id = m.role_id and d.module = p_module
        where case p_action
          when 'view' then coalesce(o.can_view, d.can_view, false)
          when 'edit' then coalesce(o.can_edit, d.can_edit, false)
          when 'create' then coalesce(o.can_create, d.can_create, false)
          when 'delete' then coalesce(o.can_delete, d.can_delete, false)
          else false end)
$function$
;
-- ##### func user_roles_write()
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.user_roles_write()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
begin
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
    insert into memberships (user_id, org_id, role_id)
      values (new.user_id, coalesce((select org_id from profiles where id = new.user_id), public.my_org_id()), new.role_id);
    return new;
  else
    delete from memberships m where m.user_id = old.user_id and m.role_id = old.role_id
      and m.org_id = (select org_id from profiles where id = old.user_id);
    return old;
  end if;
end $function$
;

 RETURNS text
 LANGUAGE sql
 IMMUTABLE
AS $function$ select to_char(d, 'DD/MM/YYYY') $function$
;
