;
-- ##### func warranty_label(s text)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.warranty_label(s text)
 RETURNS text
 LANGUAGE sql
 IMMUTABLE
AS $function$
  select case s when 'draft' then 'Nháp' when 'new' then 'Mới' when 'accepted' then 'Đã tiếp nhận' when 'waiting_parts' then 'Chờ linh kiện'
    when 'processing' then 'Đang xử lý' when 'resolved' then 'Đã xử lý' when 'rejected' then 'Từ chối' when 'closed' then 'Đã đóng'
    when 'pending' then 'Chờ nhà cung cấp' else s end
$function$
;
-- ##### policy academic_years.academic_years_delete
create policy academic_years_delete on public.academic_years as PERMISSIVE for DELETE to authenticated
  using (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT has_perm('periods'::text, 'delete'::text) AS has_perm)));
-- ##### policy academic_years.academic_years_insert
create policy academic_years_insert on public.academic_years as PERMISSIVE for INSERT to authenticated
  with check (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT has_perm('periods'::text, 'create'::text) AS has_perm)));
-- ##### policy academic_years.academic_years_read
create policy academic_years_read on public.academic_years as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT is_staff() AS is_staff)));
-- ##### policy academic_years.academic_years_student_read
create policy academic_years_student_read on public.academic_years as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using ((( SELECT is_student() AS is_student) AND (org_id = ( SELECT my_org_id() AS my_org_id))));
-- ##### policy academic_years.academic_years_update
create policy academic_years_update on public.academic_years as PERMISSIVE for UPDATE to authenticated
  using (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT has_perm('periods'::text, 'edit'::text) AS has_perm)))
  with check (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT has_perm('periods'::text, 'edit'::text) AS has_perm)));
-- ##### policy activities.activities_owner_delete
create policy activities_owner_delete on public.activities as PERMISSIVE for DELETE to authenticated
  using (((org_id = my_org_id()) AND ((teacher_id = my_teacher_id()) OR is_admin())));
-- ##### policy activities.activities_owner_insert
create policy activities_owner_insert on public.activities as PERMISSIVE for INSERT to authenticated
  with check (((org_id = my_org_id()) AND is_staff() AND (((teacher_id = my_teacher_id()) AND (NOT is_template)) OR is_admin())));
-- ##### policy activities.activities_owner_update
create policy activities_owner_update on public.activities as PERMISSIVE for UPDATE to authenticated
  using (((org_id = my_org_id()) AND ((teacher_id = my_teacher_id()) OR is_admin())))
  with check (((org_id = my_org_id()) AND (((teacher_id = my_teacher_id()) AND (NOT is_template)) OR is_admin())));
-- ##### policy activities.activities_staff_read
create policy activities_staff_read on public.activities as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using ((can_access_org(org_id) AND is_staff() AND ((status = 'published'::text) OR is_template OR (teacher_id = my_teacher_id()) OR is_admin() OR is_authority())));
-- ##### policy ai_org_limits.ai_org_limits_read
create policy ai_org_limits_read on public.ai_org_limits as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using ((can_access_org(org_id) OR is_platform_admin()));
-- ##### policy ai_org_limits.ai_org_limits_write
create policy ai_org_limits_write on public.ai_org_limits as PERMISSIVE for ALL to authenticated
  using (is_platform_admin())
  with check (is_platform_admin());
-- ##### policy ai_settings.ai_settings_read
create policy ai_settings_read on public.ai_settings as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using (true);
-- ##### policy ai_settings.ai_settings_write
create policy ai_settings_write on public.ai_settings as PERMISSIVE for UPDATE to authenticated
  using (is_platform_admin())
  with check (is_platform_admin());
-- ##### policy ai_usage.ai_usage_read
create policy ai_usage_read on public.ai_usage as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using (((user_id = auth.uid()) OR is_platform_admin() OR (is_org_admin() AND can_access_org(org_id))));
-- ##### policy approval_actions.approval_actions_read
create policy approval_actions_read on public.approval_actions as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using ((EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM approval_requests r
  WHERE (r.id = approval_actions.request_id))));
-- ##### policy approval_requests.approval_requests_read
create policy approval_requests_read on public.approval_requests as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using (((( SELECT is_staff() AS is_staff) AND (org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree))) OR (requested_by = auth.uid()) OR ((((steps -> (current_step - 1)) ->> 'org_id'::text))::uuid = ( SELECT my_org_id() AS my_org_id))));

create policy approval_steps_read on public.approval_steps as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using ((EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM approval_workflows w
  WHERE (w.id = approval_steps.workflow_id))));
-- ##### policy approval_workflows.approval_workflows_read
create policy approval_workflows_read on public.approval_workflows as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using ((( SELECT is_staff() AS is_staff) AND ((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) OR (org_id IN ( SELECT my_ancestor_orgs() AS my_ancestor_orgs)))));
-- ##### policy assignments.assignments_staff_read
create policy assignments_staff_read on public.assignments as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT is_staff() AS is_staff)));
-- ##### policy assignments.assignments_student_read
create policy assignments_student_read on public.assignments as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using ((( SELECT is_student() AS is_student) AND (class_id = ( SELECT my_class_id() AS my_class_id)) AND (status <> 'draft'::text)));
-- ##### policy assignments.assignments_teacher_write
create policy assignments_teacher_write on public.assignments as PERMISSIVE for ALL to authenticated
  using (teaches_class(class_id))
  with check (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND teaches_class(class_id)));

  using ((( SELECT is_staff() AS is_staff) AND (org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree))));
-- ##### policy borrow_requests.borrow_requests_delete

  using (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT has_perm('stem_rooms'::text, 'delete'::text) AS has_perm)));
-- ##### policy borrow_requests.borrow_requests_insert

  with check (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT has_perm('stem_rooms'::text, 'create'::text) AS has_perm)));
-- ##### policy borrow_requests.borrow_requests_read
create policy borrow_requests_read on public.borrow_requests as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated

  using ((((org_id = my_org_id()) AND is_staff() AND (has_perm('students'::text, 'view'::text) OR (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM students s
  WHERE ((s.id = guardians.student_id) AND (s.class_id IS NOT NULL) AND teaches_class(s.class_id)))))) OR (profile_id = auth.uid())));
-- ##### policy guardians.guardians_update
create policy guardians_update on public.guardians as PERMISSIVE for UPDATE to authenticated
  using (((org_id = my_org_id()) AND has_perm('students'::text, 'edit'::text)))
  with check (((org_id = my_org_id()) AND has_perm('students'::text, 'edit'::text)));

  using (( SELECT is_platform_admin() AS is_platform_admin))

  using ((( SELECT is_admin() AS is_admin) AND (org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND (status <> 'accepted'::text)));
-- ##### policy invitations.invitations_admin_insert
create policy invitations_admin_insert on public.invitations as PERMISSIVE for INSERT to authenticated
  with check ((( SELECT is_admin() AS is_admin) AND (org_id = ( SELECT my_org_id() AS my_org_id)) AND (invited_by = auth.uid()) AND (status = 'pending'::text) AND role_fits_org(role_id, org_id) AND ((teacher_id IS NULL) OR (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM teachers t
  WHERE ((t.id = invitations.teacher_id) AND (t.org_id = invitations.org_id))))) AND ((student_id IS NULL) OR (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM students s
  WHERE ((s.id = invitations.student_id) AND (s.org_id = invitations.org_id)))))));

  using ((( SELECT is_admin() AS is_admin) AND (org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree))));

  using ((( SELECT is_admin() AS is_admin) AND (org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND (status = 'pending'::text)))

  using ((( SELECT is_admin() AS is_admin) AND (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM memberships m
  WHERE ((m.user_id = profiles.id) AND (m.org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)))))))
  with check ((( SELECT is_admin() AS is_admin) AND (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM memberships m
  WHERE ((m.user_id = profiles.id) AND (m.org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)))))));
-- ##### policy profiles.profiles_platform_read
create policy profiles_platform_read on public.profiles as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using (( SELECT is_platform_admin() AS is_platform_admin));
-- ##### policy profiles.profiles_platform_update
create policy profiles_platform_update on public.profiles as PERMISSIVE for UPDATE to authenticated
  using (( SELECT is_platform_admin() AS is_platform_admin))
  with check (( SELECT is_platform_admin() AS is_platform_admin));
-- ##### policy project_logs.project_logs_insert
create policy project_logs_insert on public.project_logs as PERMISSIVE for INSERT to authenticated
  with check (((author = auth.uid()) AND (can_edit_project(project_id) OR (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM student_projects p
  WHERE ((p.id = project_logs.project_id) AND (p.org_id = my_org_id()) AND is_staff()))))));
-- ##### policy project_logs.project_logs_read
create policy project_logs_read on public.project_logs as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using (can_view_project(project_id));
-- ##### policy project_members.project_members_read
create policy project_members_read on public.project_members as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using (can_view_project(project_id));
-- ##### policy project_members.project_members_write
create policy project_members_write on public.project_members as PERMISSIVE for ALL to authenticated
  using ((can_edit_project(project_id) AND (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM student_projects p
  WHERE ((p.id = project_members.project_id) AND (p.status = ANY (ARRAY['draft'::text, 'rejected'::text, 'approved'::text])))))))
  with check (can_edit_project(project_id));

-- ##### policy stocktakes.stocktakes_update
create policy stocktakes_update on public.stocktakes as PERMISSIVE for UPDATE to authenticated
  using (((org_id = my_org_id()) AND has_perm('equipment'::text, 'edit'::text)))
  with check (((org_id = my_org_id()) AND has_perm('equipment'::text, 'edit'::text)));
-- ##### policy student_consents.student_consents_delete
create policy student_consents_delete on public.student_consents as PERMISSIVE for DELETE to authenticated
  using (((org_id = ( SELECT my_org_id() AS my_org_id)) AND ( SELECT has_perm('students'::text, 'delete'::text) AS has_perm)));
-- ##### policy student_consents.student_consents_insert
create policy student_consents_insert on public.student_consents as PERMISSIVE for INSERT to authenticated
  with check (((org_id = ( SELECT my_org_id() AS my_org_id)) AND ( SELECT has_perm('students'::text, 'edit'::text) AS has_perm) AND (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM students s
  WHERE ((s.id = student_consents.student_id) AND (s.org_id = student_consents.org_id))))));
-- ##### policy student_consents.student_consents_read
create policy student_consents_read on public.student_consents as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using (((org_id = ( SELECT my_org_id() AS my_org_id)) AND ( SELECT has_perm('students'::text, 'view'::text) AS has_perm)));
-- ##### policy student_consents.student_consents_update
create policy student_consents_update on public.student_consents as PERMISSIVE for UPDATE to authenticated
  using (((org_id = ( SELECT my_org_id() AS my_org_id)) AND ( SELECT has_perm('students'::text, 'edit'::text) AS has_perm)))
  with check (((org_id = ( SELECT my_org_id() AS my_org_id)) AND ( SELECT has_perm('students'::text, 'edit'::text) AS has_perm) AND (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM students s
  WHERE ((s.id = student_consents.student_id) AND (s.org_id = student_consents.org_id))))));
-- ##### policy student_group_members.group_members_student_read
create policy group_members_student_read on public.student_group_members as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using ((( SELECT is_student() AS is_student) AND (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM student_groups g
  WHERE ((g.id = student_group_members.group_id) AND (g.class_id = ( SELECT my_class_id() AS my_class_id)))))));
-- ##### policy student_group_members.group_members_teacher_write
create policy group_members_teacher_write on public.student_group_members as PERMISSIVE for ALL to authenticated
  using ((EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM student_groups g
  WHERE ((g.id = student_group_members.group_id) AND teaches_class(g.class_id)))))
  with check ((EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM (student_groups g
     JOIN students s ON ((s.id = student_group_members.student_id)))
  WHERE ((g.id = student_group_members.group_id) AND (s.class_id = g.class_id) AND teaches_class(g.class_id)))));
-- ##### policy student_group_members.student_group_members_staff_read
create policy student_group_members_staff_read on public.student_group_members as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT is_staff() AS is_staff)));

  using ((( SELECT is_student() AS is_student) AND (class_id = ( SELECT my_class_id() AS my_class_id))));

  using (teaches_class(class_id))
  with check (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND teaches_class(class_id)));

  using (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT is_staff() AS is_staff)));

  using ((can_edit_project(id) AND (status = ANY (ARRAY['draft'::text, 'rejected'::text]))));

  with check (((org_id = my_org_id()) AND (is_admin() OR (my_teacher_id() IS NOT NULL) OR (my_student_id() IS NOT NULL))));

  using (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT is_staff() AS is_staff)));
-- ##### policy submissions.submissions_student_insert
create policy submissions_student_insert on public.submissions as PERMISSIVE for INSERT to authenticated
  with check ((( SELECT is_student() AS is_student) AND (org_id = ( SELECT my_org_id() AS my_org_id)) AND (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM assignments a
  WHERE ((a.id = submissions.assignment_id) AND (a.class_id = ( SELECT my_class_id() AS my_class_id)) AND (a.status = 'open'::text) AND (a.opens_at <= now()) AND (((NOT a.group_mode) AND (submissions.student_id = ( SELECT my_student_id() AS my_student_id))) OR (a.group_mode AND (submissions.group_id IN ( SELECT my_group_ids() AS my_group_ids)))))))));
-- ##### policy submissions.submissions_student_read
create policy submissions_student_read on public.submissions as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using ((( SELECT is_student() AS is_student) AND ((student_id = ( SELECT my_student_id() AS my_student_id)) OR (group_id IN ( SELECT my_group_ids() AS my_group_ids)))));
-- ##### policy submissions.submissions_student_update
create policy submissions_student_update on public.submissions as PERMISSIVE for UPDATE to authenticated
  using ((( SELECT is_student() AS is_student) AND (status <> 'graded'::text) AND ((student_id = ( SELECT my_student_id() AS my_student_id)) OR (group_id IN ( SELECT my_group_ids() AS my_group_ids)))))
  with check ((( SELECT is_student() AS is_student) AND ((student_id = ( SELECT my_student_id() AS my_student_id)) OR (group_id IN ( SELECT my_group_ids() AS my_group_ids)))));

   FROM assignments a
  WHERE ((a.id = submissions.assignment_id) AND teaches_class(a.class_id)))));

  using ((EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM assignments a
  WHERE ((a.id = submissions.assignment_id) AND teaches_class(a.class_id)))))
  with check ((EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM assignments a
  WHERE ((a.id = submissions.assignment_id) AND teaches_class(a.class_id)))));

create policy teacher_assessments_write on public.teacher_assessments as PERMISSIVE for ALL to authenticated
  using ((((kind = 'self'::text) AND (teacher_id = ( SELECT my_teacher_id() AS my_teacher_id))) OR ((kind = 'manager'::text) AND (org_id = ( SELECT my_org_id() AS my_org_id)) AND ( SELECT is_admin() AS is_admin))))
  with check (((EXISTS ( SELECT 1
   FROM teachers t
  WHERE ((t.id = teacher_assessments.teacher_id) AND (t.org_id = teacher_assessments.org_id)))) AND (((kind = 'self'::text) AND (teacher_id = ( SELECT my_teacher_id() AS my_teacher_id))) OR ((kind = 'manager'::text) AND (org_id = ( SELECT my_org_id() AS my_org_id)) AND ( SELECT is_admin() AS is_admin)))));

-- ##### policy training_attendance.training_attendance_dept_write
create policy training_attendance_dept_write on public.training_attendance as PERMISSIVE for ALL to authenticated
  using ((( SELECT is_authority() AS is_authority) AND (org_id = ( SELECT my_org_id() AS my_org_id))))
  with check ((( SELECT is_authority() AS is_authority) AND (org_id = ( SELECT my_org_id() AS my_org_id))));
-- ##### policy training_attendance.training_attendance_insert
create policy training_attendance_insert on public.training_attendance as PERMISSIVE for INSERT to authenticated
  with check (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT has_perm('training'::text, 'create'::text) AS has_perm)));
-- ##### policy training_attendance.training_attendance_read
create policy training_attendance_read on public.training_attendance as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT is_staff() AS is_staff)));
-- ##### policy training_attendance.training_attendance_update
create policy training_attendance_update on public.training_attendance as PERMISSIVE for UPDATE to authenticated
  using (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT has_perm('training'::text, 'edit'::text) AS has_perm)))
  with check (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT has_perm('training'::text, 'edit'::text) AS has_perm)));
-- ##### policy training_enrollments.training_enroll_self_read
create policy training_enroll_self_read on public.training_enrollments as PERMISSIVE for SELECT to authenticated
  using ((teacher_id = ( SELECT my_teacher_id() AS my_teacher_id)));

-- ##### policy warranty_tickets.warranty_staff_report
create policy warranty_staff_report on public.warranty_tickets as PERMISSIVE for INSERT to authenticated
  with check (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT is_staff() AS is_staff) AND (reported_by = auth.uid()) AND (status = ANY (ARRAY['draft'::text, 'new'::text]))));
-- ##### policy warranty_tickets.warranty_tickets_delete
create policy warranty_tickets_delete on public.warranty_tickets as PERMISSIVE for DELETE to authenticated
  using (((org_id IN ( SELECT my_org_tree() AS my_org_tree)) AND ( SELECT has_perm('warranty'::text, 'delete'::text) AS has_perm)));

-- ##### policy warranty_tickets.warranty_tickets_platform_update
create policy warranty_tickets_platform_update on public.warranty_tickets as PERMISSIVE for UPDATE to authenticated
  using (( SELECT is_platform_admin() AS is_platform_admin))
  with check (( SELECT is_platform_admin() AS is_platform_admin));
